samyakṣaṃbodhimāsādya saṃbuddhapadamāpnuyuḥ || 31.159{52} ||
evaṃ matvātra saṃsāre mātsaryaṃ mūlamenasām |
bodhicittāsinochitvā caritavyaṃ śubhe sadā || 31.160{53} ||
tato vo maṃgalaṃ nityaṃ bhavedbhave samaṃtataḥ |
kramādbodhiṃ samāsādya saṃbuddhapadamāpsyatha || 31.161{54} ||
ityādiṣṭaṃ munīndreṇa śrutvā te sāṃghikā mudā |
sarve'pi pratibuddhitvā prābhyanaṃdan prasāditāḥ || 31.162{55} ||
iti me guruṇādiṣṭaṃ śrutaṃ mayā tathocyate |
evaṃ tvaṃ nṛpa mātsaryyaṃ tyaktvā cara śubhe sadā || 31.163{56} ||
prajāścāpi tvayā rājan bodhayitvā prayatnataḥ |
bodhicaryāśayāḥ kṛtvā pratiṣṭhāpyāḥ śubhe sadā || 31.164{57} ||
evaṃ te sarvadā bhadraṃ bhavedbhave samaṃtataḥ |
kramādbodhiṃ samāsādya saṃbuddhapadamāpnuyāḥ || 31.165{58} ||
iti tenārhatādiṣṭaṃ śrutvāśokaḥ sa bhūpatiḥ |
tatheti prativijñapya prābhyanandatsapārṣadaḥ || 31.166{89!} ||
pretībhūtāvadānaṃ duritahṛtamataiḥ śreṣṭhino lobhino ye śṛṇvaṃti śravayaṃti pramuditamanasaḥ śraddhayedaṃ manuṣyāḥ |
mātsaryaṃ te vihāya trimaṇiśaraṇagāḥ sarvasatvānurāgā dānaṃ datvā prasannāḥ satataśubhacarā yānti buddhālayaṃ te || 31.167{60} ||
iti śrīratnāvadānatatve pretībhūtāvadānaṃ samāptam
virūpāvadāna
athāśoko maharājaḥ kṛtāṃjalipuṭo mudā |
upaguptaṃ yatiṃ natvā prārthayeccaivamādarāt || 32.1{1} ||
bhadanta śrotumichāmi punaranyatsubhāṣitam |
tadyathā guruṇādiṣṭaṃ tathādeṣṭuṃ ca me'rhati || 32.2{2} ||
iti saṃprārthite rājñā śrutvā so'rhan sudhīryatiḥ |
tamaśokaṃ mahārājaṃ samālokyaivamādiśat || 32.3{3} ||
sādhu śṛṇu mahārāja yathā me guruṇoditam |
tathāhaṃ te pravakṣyāmi tava dharmapravṛddhaye || 32.4{4} ||
tadyathaivaṃ puraikasmin samaye sa munīśvaraḥ |
bhagavāṃ trijagacchāstā dharmarājastathāgataḥ || 32.5{5} ||
sarvajñaḥ sākyasiṃho'rhaṃ chrāvstyā bahirāśrame |
jetodyāne vihāre'smin vijahāra sasāṃghikaḥ || 32.6{6} ||
tadā tatra sabhāmadhye siṃhāsane sumaṃḍite |
sarvasatvahitārthena dharmamādeṣṭumāśrayat || 32.7{7} ||
tatra te bhikṣavaḥ sarve bhikṣuṇyo'pi samāgatāḥ |
yatinaścailakāścaivamupāsakā upāsikāḥ || 32.8{8} ||
vratino bodhisatvāśca saṃbodhiguṇalālasāḥ |
nirgranthāstīrthikāścāpi tāpasāśca maharṣayaḥ || 32.9{9} ||
brahmaśakrādayo devā lokapālā maharddhikāḥ |
daityā yakṣāśca gaṃdharvāḥ kinnarāścāpi rākṣasāḥ || 32.10{10} ||
siddhā vidyādharāścāpi nāgāśca garuḍā api |
sādhyā grahāḥ satārāśca vasavaścāpsarogaṇāḥ || 32.11{11} ||
brāhmaṇāḥ kṣatriyāścāpi nṛpā rājakumārakāḥ |
vaiśyāśca maṃtriṇo'mātyāḥ śreṣṭhinaśca mahājanāḥ || 32.12{12} ||
gṛhasthā vaṇijaḥ sārthavāhāśca dhanino'pi ca |
śilpinaḥ paurikāścāpi jānapadāśca nairgamāḥ || 32.13{13} ||
grāmyāḥ kārpaṭikāścāpi saddharmaśravaṇārthinaḥ |
sarve te samupāgatya vihāre samupāviśan || 32.14{14} ||
tatra taṃ śrīghanaṃ dṛṣṭvā sarve te saṃprasāditāḥ |
natvā pradakṣiṇīkṛtya purataḥ samupācaran || 32.15{15} ||
tatra sarve'pi te lokāḥ samabhyarcya yathākramam |
taṃ munīndraṃ praṇatvaiva dharmaṃ śrotumupāśrayan || 32.16{16} ||
tataḥ sa bhagavān dṛṣṭvā tān sarvān samupāśritān |
bodhicaryāṃ samārabhya saddharmaṃ samupādiśat || 32.17{17} ||
tatsaddharmāmṛtaṃ pītvā sarve te tridaśādayaḥ |
lokāḥ satyamiti jñātvā prābhyanandan prabodhitāḥ || 32.18{18} ||
tasminnavasare tatra śrāvastyāḥ pauriko gṛhī |
āsīnmahādhanaḥ śrīmāñchṛdopamo mahājanaḥ || 32.19{19} ||
sa svakulasamārāmāṃ svakuladharmacāriṇīm |
kāṃtāṃ bhāryyāṃ samānīya samyakvyūhe yathāvidhiḥ || 32.20{20} ||
tataḥ sa kāmasaṃraktaḥ sukāminyā tayā saha |
yathechayā sukhaṃ bhuktvā reme nityaṃ pracoditaḥ || 32.21{21} ||
tatastasya gṛhasthasya yathākāmaṃ prabhuṃjataḥ |
samaye sā satī bhārya garbhitābhūtkṛśāṃgikā || 32.22{22} ||
tataḥ sā samaye'sūta dārakaṃ durbhagākṛtim |
durvarṇaṃ duritākāraṃ virūpaṃ vikṛtāśrayam || 32.23{23} ||
dṛṣṭvā taṃ dārakaṃ mātā bhinnāśayā viṣeditāḥ |
kimīdṛgātmajenāpi dhigmāmiti vyaciṃtayat || 32.24{24} ||
tataḥ sa janakaḥ śrutvā taṃ jātaṃ vikṛtāśrayam |
virūpaṃ durbhagākāramityevaṃ samaciṃtayat || 32.25{25} ||
aho kiṃ prakṛtaṃ pāpaṃ janmāṃtare purā mayā |
yenāyaṃ durbhagākāro virūpo jāyate sutaḥ || 32.26{26} ||
tadatra kiṃ mayopāyaṃ karttavyaṃ nāpi manyate |
dhigmāṃ yena sute jāte lajjayā me sukhaṃ hṛtam || 32.27{27} ||
tathāpi kiṃ kariṣyāmi daivātme jāyate hyayam |
tadvirūpo'pi putro'yaṃ pālanīyo mayātmajaḥ || 32.28{28} ||
iti bhartroditaṃ śrutvā sā bhāryyāpyevamabravīt |
yadābhyāṃ prakṛtaṃ pāpaṃ tatphalaṃ bhujyate khalu || 32.29{29} ||
yadabhāvi na tadbhogyaṃ bhāvi cenna tadanyathā |
sarvatra bhāvino bhogyaṃ bhuṃjate sarvajaṃtavaḥ || 32.30{30} ||
tadatrāhamimaṃ bālaṃ pālayeyaṃ prayatnataḥ |
ityuktvā sā vibhinnāsyā nārī tasthau pralajjitā || 32.31{31} ||
taṃ virūpaṃ sutaṃ jātaṃ vīkṣya sa janako'pi ca |
lajjāvidīrṇacitto'bhūnnirutsāhaviṣāditaḥ || 32.32{32} ||
hā mayā kiṃ kṛtaṃ pāpaṃ manyate na bhavāṃtare |
yato'yaṃ dārako jāto virūpo durbhagākṛtiḥ || 32.33{33} ||
tathāpi kiṃ kariṣyāmi daivānno jāyate hyayam |
daivabhogyaṃ prabhoktavyaṃ sarvairapi hi jaṃtubhiḥ || 32.34{34} ||
avaśyaṃ bhāvino bhavanti sarvadehinām |
tadayaṃ svātmajo bālaḥ pālanīyotmayā mayā || 32.35{35} ||
iti dhyātvā pitā so'thadṛṣṭvā tasya śiśormukham |
lajjayā pratibhinnāsyastasthau daivānucintayan || 32.36{36} ||
tato jñātīn samāhūya kṛtvā jātimahaṃ sa ca |
pitāsya nāma vikhyāte kuruteti samabravīt || 32.37{37} ||
tataste jñātayaḥ sarve saṃkṛtvā samataṃ tathā |
tadgṛhasthaṃ samāmaṃtrya pura evamupādiśan || 32.38{38} ||
gṛhapate virūpo'yaṃ putro yattava jāyate |
tasmādbhavatunāmnāyaṃ prasiddho bālako bhuvi || 32.39{39} ||
tataḥ snehādvirūpo'sau mātrā yatnena pālitaḥ |
krāmātpuṣṭo vivṛddhābhūdhradasthamiva paṃkajam || 32.40{40} ||
tadā yadā kumāratvaṃ prāptaḥ sa vikṛtāśrayam |
svarūpaṃ darppaṇe dṛṣṭvā virūpo lajjito'bhavat || 32.41{41} ||
tato jehrīyamāno'sau virūpaḥ parimohitaḥ |
pāpaciṃtāparitātmā manasaivaṃ vyaciṃtayat || 32.42{42} ||
hā mayā kiṃ kṛtaṃ pāpaṃ yenāhaṃ duritākṛtiḥ |
kiṃ mamānena kāyena mṛtyuṃ me'tra varaṃ dhruvam || 32.43{43} ||
kiṃ kariṣye virūpo'haṃ kva yāsyāmi durākṛtiḥ |
kiṃ mamānena jīvena kevalaṃ duḥkhabhoginā || 32.44{44} ||
tadatrāhaṃ gṛhaṃ tyaktvā vanodyāne samāśrayan |
saṃbuddhaṃ sugataṃ dhyātvā mṛtyuṃ gaccheya muktaye || 32.45{45} ||
avaśyameva sarveṣāṃ jaṃtūnaṃ maraṇaṃ bhave |
tanmamedṛgvirūpo'smin śarīre jīvite spṛhā || 32.46{46} ||
yadyatrāhaṃ ciraṃ jīvī nindito duḥkhamāpnuyām |
tadbuddhaṃ sugataṃ dhyātvā tiṣṭheyaṃ prāṇamuktaye || 32.47{47} ||
tatsaṃbuddhaṃ jinaṃ dhyātvā mṛto'haṃ yadi sāṃpratam |
tatpuṇyaḥ paritātmā sugatiṃ gacheya sarvathā || 32.48{48} ||
ye buddhaṃ sugataṃ smṛtvā gachanti mṛtyumātmanā |
durgatiṃ te na gachanti samyāntyeva sukhāvatīm || 32.49{49} ||
tasmātte sudhiyaḥ santaḥ tyaktvā yāyātkulaṃ gṛham |
saṃbuddhasmaraṇaṃ dhyātvā tiṣṭhanti nirjane vane || 32.50{50} ||
tathāhaṃ tatsamālokya saṃbuddhaṃ śaraṇaṃ gataḥ |
samādhāya sadā smṛtvā vaseyaṃ vijane vane || 32.51{51} ||
ityevaṃ manasā dhyātvā virūpaḥ sa prasannadhīḥ |
sa saṃpadaṃ gṛhaṃ tyaktvā jīrṇodyānaṃ samāśrayat || 32.52{52} ||
tatrodyāne vivikte sa parṇakuṭṭiṃ samāśrayan |
bhagavantamanusmṛtvā tasthau dhyānasamāhitaḥ || 32.53{53} ||
tadā sa bhagavāñchāstā taṃ tathā sthitam |
viśuddhāśayamālokya samuddhartuṃ samudyataḥ || 32.54{54} ||
tataḥ sa bhagavānnātho bhikṣusaṃghasamanvitaḥ |
tatrodyāne virūpaṃ taṃ paśyan bhāsvānupācarat || 32.55{55} ||
tatprabhāparispṛṣṭo'sau virūpaḥ satsukhānvitaḥ |
vismitastaṃ samāyātaṃ sasāṃghikamapaśyata || 32.56{56} ||
taṃ munīṃdraṃ samālokya virūpaḥ sa vimohitaḥ |
jehrīyamāna utthāya parāyituṃ tato'carat || 32.57{57} ||
tatra sa bhagavān dṛṣṭvā virūpaṃ taṃ parāyitam |
sahasarddhyā diśo mārge nirudhyābhyatiṣṭhata || 32.58{58} ||
tathā nirudhyamānaḥ sa virūpastena śāsinā |
parāyituṃ prayatnena na śaśāka kathaṃ cana || 32.59{59} ||
sa parikhinnātmā virūpo lajjitāśayaḥ |
parṇakuṭṭisamāsīnaṃstasthau bhīto divāndhavat || 32.60{60} ||
tatra sa bhagavān dṛṣṭvā nilīnaṃ lajjayāsane |
saṃnirodhasamāpattiṃ samādhiṃ vidadhe tadā || 32.61{61} ||
tataḥ sa bhagavañchāstā tatsamādheḥ samutthitaḥ |
svayaṃ virūpamātmānaṃ nirmāya vikṛtāśrayam || 32.62{62} ||
bhojanapūrṇamādāya śarāvaṃ mṛnmayaṃ tataḥ |
śanaistasya virūpasya parṇakuṭṭyantike'carat || 32.63{63} ||
tatra taṃ samupāyātaṃ virūpaṃ vikṛtāśrayam |
sa virūpaḥ samālokya harṣita evamabravīt || 32.64{64} ||
svāgataṃ bho sahāye hi kutra gantumihāgataḥ |
tiṣṭhātrāvāṃ sadāvāse vatsyāvahe sukhānvitā || 32.65{65} ||
iti tenoditaṃ śrutvā bhagavān sa virūpadhṛk |
tvāṃ draṣṭumihāyāmi proktveti samupāśrayet || 32.66{66} ||
tatra sthitaḥ kathāṃ kṛtvā bhagavān sa virūpadhṛk |
dadau tasmai virūpāya surasāmṛtabhojanam || 32.67{67} ||
tatpradattaṃ prabhuktvānnaṃ sa virūpaḥ sa moditaḥ |
tatkṣaṇātparipuṣṭāṅgo babhūva prīṇitendriyaḥ || 32.68{68} ||
tataḥ sa bhagavāñchāstā dṛṣṭvā taṃ saṃpramoditam |
svarūpeṇa sthitastatra vyarājatsaṃprabhāsayan || 32.69{69} ||
taṃ saumyabhadrarūpāṅgaṃ śrīghanaṃ śubhiteṃdriyam |
sa virūpaḥ samālokya vismitaścaivamabravīt || 32.70{70} ||
aho kathaṃ bhavatyevamabhirūpataro bhavān |
kasya puṇyavipākān tadvaktumarhati me puraḥ || 32.71{71} ||
iti saṃprārthite tena bhagavān sa munīśvaraḥ |
bodhayituṃ virūpaṃ taṃ samālokyaivamādiśat || 32.72{72} ||
mahāvidyāsti me sādho saṃbodhisādhanottamā |
cittaprasādasaṃjātajananyākhyā mahebalaḥ || 32.73{73} ||
tasyā eva prabhāvena bhavāmyahaṃ śubheṃdriyaḥ |
samaṃtabhadrarūpāṅgaḥ sarvasatvamanoharaḥ || 32.74{74} ||
ityādiśya munīndraḥ sa bhagavāṃstatra śubhāsane |
saddharmaṃ samupādestuṃ samāśrayatprabhāsayan || 32.75{75} ||
tataste sāṃghikāḥ sarve saumyarūpāḥ śubheṃdriyāḥ |
parivṛtya munīndraṃ taṃ puraskṛtyopatasthire || 32.76{76} ||
tān sarvān samupāsīnān saumyarūpāñchubhendriyān |
dṛṣṭvā taṃ sugataṃ matvā babhūva vismayānvitaḥ || 32.77{77} ||
aho bhāgya mayā labdhaṃ mahatpuṇyamihādhunā |
yanmamārthe munīndro'yaṃ sasāṃghika upāgataḥ || 32.78{78} ||
nūnamatra munīndro'yaṃ matkarmaparicoditaḥ |
saddharmaṃ samupādeṣṭuṃ sasaṃghaḥ kṛpayāgataḥ || 32.79{79} ||
tadahamasya munīndrasya śraddhayā śaraṇaṃ gataḥ |
satkṛtya saugataṃ dharmaṃ śrotumarhe samāhitaḥ || 32.80{80} ||
ityevaṃ manasā dhyātvā virūpaḥ sa pramoditaḥ |
upetya sāṃjalirnatvā taṃ munīndramupāśrayat || 32.81{81} ||
tadā tasya virūpasya buddhapuṇyānubhāvataḥ |
lakṣmī prādurabhūttatra divyabhogapradāyinī || 32.82{82} ||
tadā so'bhūdvirūpo'pi divyābhirūpasundaraḥ |
pariśuddhatrikāyo'pi sadguṇārtho śubhāṃśikaḥ || 32.83{83} ||
tataḥ so'tiprasannātmā dṛṣṭvaitatmahadadbhutam |
mudā tasya muneḥ pādau vavande sa kṛtāṃjaliḥ || 32.84{84} ||
tataḥ utthāya taṃ nāthaṃ bhagavaṃtaṃ sasāṃghikam |
samālokya prasannātmā prārthayatsa samādarāt || 32.85{85} ||
bhagavan bhavatā śāstaḥ kṛpādṛṣṭiprasādataḥ |
prādurbhūtātra me lakṣmī bhavāmi yātisuṃdaraḥ || 32.86{86} ||
tadetanmahadāścaryaṃ dṛṣṭvā me rocate manaḥ |
bhavatāṃ śaraṇe sthitvā carituṃ saṃvaraṃ sadā || 32.87{87} ||
tadatra bhagavannātha kṛpayā buddhaśāsane |
anvāhṛtya śubhe dharme niyoktuṃ māṃ samarhati || 32.88{88} ||
śraddhayāhaṃ jagannātha bhavatāṃ śaraṇaṃ gataḥ |
pravrajya saṃvaraṃ dhṛtvā cariṣye'tra samāhitaḥ || 32.89{89} ||
iti tatsaṃprārthitaṃ śrutvā bhagavān sa munīśvaraḥ |
tasya śuddhāśayamālokya samāmaṃtryaivamādiśat || 32.90{90} ||
yadi vāṃchāsti te vatsa carituṃ saugataṃ vratam |
piturājñāṃ samāsādya prāgacha dāsyate tadā || 32.91{91} ||
ityādiṣṭaṃ munīndreṇa śrutvā sa pratiharṣitaḥ |
mudā tasya muneḥ pādau praṇatvaiva tato'carat || 32.92{92} ||
tatra sa svagṛhe gatvā pādau pituḥ kṛtāṃjaliḥ |
praṇatvā purataḥ sthitvā prāvadadevamādarāt || 32.93{93} ||
tatodyāne virūpo'hamiti lajjāviṣāditaḥ |
parṇakuṭyāṃ samāsīno dhyatvā vasāmi yogavit || 32.94{94} ||
tatra sa bhagavān buddhaḥ sasaṃghaḥ svayamāgataḥ |
bodhayitvāmṛtaṃ bhogyaṃ dadāti samupāyavit || 32.95{95} ||
taddattamamṛtaṃ bhuktvā bhavāmi suṃdarākṛtiḥ |
lakṣmīścāpi samudbhūtā mamaivaṃ jāyate śubham || 32.96{96} ||
etatsarvaṃ munīndrasya kṛpādṛṣṭiprasādataḥ |
nānyathā hīti vijñāya prasīda sugate sadā || 32.97{97} ||
evametatmahaccitraṃ dṛṣṭvā me saṃprasāditam |
mano'dya śāsane bauddhe carituṃ rocate vratam || 32.98{98} ||
etatsaṃprārthanāṃ kartumihāhaṃ prāgato mudā |
tadatra kṛpayā tāta tadanujñāṃ pradehi me || 32.99{99} ||
etatpuṇyavibhāgena tvaṃ cāpi sugatīṃ vrajeḥ |
tatra ca sarvadā saukhyaṃ bhuktvā yāyājjinālayam || 32.100{100} ||
iti matvā prasīdātra mā kuruṣva mano'nyathā |
śraddhayānugrahaṃ kṛtvā tadanujñāṃ pradehi me || 32.101{1} ||
iti saṃprārthitaṃ śrutvā sa gṛhasthaḥ savismayaḥ |
pitā taṃ svātmajaṃ dṛṣṭvā sucirādevamabravīt || 32.102{2} ||
aho hi bhāgyavān putra puṇyavāñchābhi sāṃpratam |
yato'sau kṛpayopetya buddhattvāmabhirakṣati || 32.103{3} ||
tatte'sti yadi vāñchati carituṃ saugataṃ vratam |
taṃ buddhaśaraṇaṃ gatvā vrataṃ cara samāhitaḥ || 32.104{4} ||
iti pitroditaṃ śrutvā sa virūpaḥ prasāditaḥ |
pādau pitroḥ praṇatvaiva sahasā prācaradgṛhāt || 32.105{5} ||
tato jetāśrame gatvā sa virūpaḥ pramoditaḥ |
pādau tasya munernatvā prārthayedevamādarāt || 32.106{6} ||
bhagavannātha sarvajña labdhānujñaḥ sāmāgatāḥ |
tadbhavān kṛpayā mahyaṃ saṃvaraṃ dātumarhati || 32.107{7} ||
iti saṃprārthite tena bhagavāṃstasya mastake |
savyahastena saṃspṛṣṭvā śāsane taṃ samagrahīt || 32.108{8} ||
tatra sa vrājitaḥ sāstrā virūpo muṇḍito'pi saḥ |
khikkhiripātrabhṛccīvaraprāvṛto vabhau || 32.109{9} ||
tataḥ sa bhikṣurātmajñaḥ saṃyamī vijiteṃdriyaḥ |
matvā saṃsārasaṃskāragatīḥ kṣaṇavighātinīḥ || 32.110{10!} ||
bhitvāvidyāgaṇān bodhipakṣe dharmeṣu prodyataḥ || 32.111{10!} ||
tataḥ sa dhāraṇīvidyāsamādhinirato yatiḥ |
sarvakleśagaṇāñjitvā sākṣādarhatvamāptavān || 32.112{11} ||
saṃsāralābhasatkāraniḥspṛho nirmalāśayaḥ |
pariśuddhatrikāyaśca nirvikalpo niraṃjanaḥ || 32.113{12} ||
sa devāsuralokānāmapi traidhātucāriṇām |
mānyaḥ pūjyo'bhivandyo'bhūdbrahmacarī sa yogavit || 32.114{13} ||
tadā te bhikṣavaḥ sarve dṛṣṭvā taṃ yatimuttamam |
vismitāstaṃ muniṃ natvā paprachustatpurākṛtam || 32.115{14} ||
bhagavannarhatānena kiṃ karma prakṛtaṃ purā |
yenāyaṃ duritākāro virūpo jāyate'dhunā || 32.116{15} ||
yaccāyaṃ bhagavācchāsta bhavatā saṃprasāditaḥ |
lakṣmīvānabhirūpaśca bhavati sadguṇārataḥ || 32.117{16} ||
yaccāyaṃ śāsane bauddhe śraddhayā śaraṇaṃ gataḥ |
sahasā kleśasaṃghārīñjitvārhattvaṃ samāptavān || 32.118{17} ||
tadasya sarvavṛttāṃtaṃ yadanena purākṛtam |
suvistaraṃ samākhyāya sarvānnaḥ paribodhaya || 32.119{18} ||
iti tairbhikṣubhiḥ sarvaiḥ prārthite sa munīśvaraḥ |
sarvāṃstān sāṃghikān bhikṣūn samālokyaivamādiśat || 32.120{19} ||
śṛṇuta bhikṣavaḥ sarve yadanena purākṛtam |
tatsarvaiḥ karma vakṣyāmi sarvalokaprabodhane || 32.121{20} ||
yathābhūtpurā buddhaḥ puṣyābhidhastathāgataḥ |
sarvajño'rhañjagacchāstā dharmarājo munīśvaraḥ || 32.122{21} ||
sa bhagavāñjagannāthaḥ kṛtvā bhadraṃ samaṃtataḥ |
sarvatra saugataṃ dharmamupadeṣṭumupācarat || 32.123{22} ||
evaṃ sarvatra lokeṣu sasāṃghikaḥ sa mārajit |
pūrvottaradiśo bhāge rājadhānīmupāśrayat || 32.124{23} ||
tatra sa bhagavāñchāstā sarvasatvahitārthabhṛt |
ādimadhyāntakalyāṇamāryadharmamupādiśat || 32.125{24} ||
tatsaddharmāmṛtaṃ pītvā sarve lokaḥ prabodhitaḥ |
triratnabhajanaṃ kṛtvā pracerire śubhe sadā || 32.126{25} ||
tatra sa bhagavān puṣyo buddhadṛṣṭya samaṃtataḥ |
loke paśyan hitaṃ kartuṃ dadarśa dvau jinātmajau || 32.127{26} ||
ekaḥ śākyamunirnāma maitreyaścāparaḥ sudhīḥ |
tasmin kāle ubhavetau bodhisatvau jinātmajau || 32.128{27} ||
maitreyasya subuddheḥ svasaṃtatiḥ paripākitā |
tasya śāstuśca vaineyāḥ satvā na paripācitāḥ || 32.129{28} ||
śākyamunestu vaineyāḥ paripakvā na saṃtatiḥ |
evaṃ sa sugataḥ puṣyo dṛṣṭvaivaṃ samaciṃtayat || 32.130{29} ||
aho śākyamunerna svasantatiḥ paripācitā |
tatsvasantatipākārthaṃ careyāhaṃ tadantike || 32.131{30} ||
iti vicintya puṣyaḥ sa tathāgataḥ sasāṃghikaḥ |
himavaṃtaṃ giriṃ gatvā pracakrāma prabhāsayan || 32.132{31} ||
tatra ratnaguhāyāṃ sa praviśya sa munīśvaraḥ |
tejodhātusamāpanno dhyātvā tasthau śubhāsane || 32.133{32} ||
tasmiṃśca samaye tatra śākyamuniḥ sa sanmatiḥ |
phalamūlasamāhartuṃ tadantikamupācarat || 32.134{33} ||
tatra ratnaguhāyāṃ taṃ puṣyaṃ tathāgataṃ munim |
suparyaṅkasamāsīnaṃ dhyānalīnaṃ prabhāsitam || 32.135{34} ||
dvātriṃśallakṣaṇāśītivyaṃjanaiḥ paribhūṣitam |
ratnāvalimivojvālaṃ śatasūryyādhikaprabham || 32.136{35} ||
saumyaṃ divyātiriktābhaṃ samaṃtabhadrarūpiṇam |
suprasannamukhāmbhojaṃ dharmarājaṃ samaikṣata || 32.137{36} ||
tatra taṃ sugataṃ dṛṣṭvā śākyamuniḥ sa moditaḥ |
sahasā sāṃjalirnatvā pūjāṃgainārcayanmudā || 32.138{37} ||
tataḥ sa ekapādena sthitvā saptaniśāṃ mudā |
sāṃjaliḥ praṇatiṃ kṛtvā tuṣṭāva gāthayānayā || 32.139{38} ||
na divi bhuvi vā nāsmilloke na vaiśravaṇālaye |
na marubhavane divye sthāne na dikṣu vidikṣu vā || 32.140{39} ||
caratu vasudhāṃ sphītāṃ kṛtsnāṃ saparvatakānanām |
puruṣavṛṣabhasya tulyānyo mahāśramaṇaḥ kutaḥ || 32.141{40} ||
etayo gāthayā stutvā taṃ puṣyaṃ sugataṃ munim |
sa śākyamunirutsāhātsaptarātriṃ mudā bhajan || 32.142{41} ||
tataḥ saptadinānte sa puṣyathāgato mudā |
paristutastamālokya śākyamunimabhāṣata || 32.143{42} ||
sādhu sādhu mahāsatva tvamevaṃ yatsamudyataḥ |
anena balavīryeṇa saṃpannena dvijottama || 32.144{43} ||
navakalpāḥ parāvṛttāḥ saṃstutyādya tathāgatam |
kramātpāramitāḥ pūrya saṃbodhiṃ samavāpnuyāḥ || 32.145{44} ||
ityādiśya munīndro'sau puṣyo dharmādhipo jinaḥ |
parivṛtto maheśākhyaistatra dhyānārato'bhasat || 32.146{45} ||
so'pi śākyamunirbodhisatvo dvijottamaḥ sudhīḥ |
tasya puṣyasya sadbhaktya śaraṇasthaḥ sadābhajat || 32.147{46} ||
tatra tasmin sthite buddhe devī guhānivāsinī |
tadguhāyāṃ praveṣṭum'sau na śaśāka kathaṃ cana || 32.148{47} ||
tataḥ sātivirūpākṣā vikṛtāṃśā durākṛtiḥ |
bhūtvā taṃ sugataṃ puṣyaṃ dhyānālīnamabhīṣapat || 32.149{48} ||
tathā sā sucireṇāpi bhīṣapītvāpi durmatiḥ |
kiñcittasya muneścittaṃ cārayituṃ śaśāka na || 32.150{49} ||
tadā sā śaṃkitākhinnā vismayāhatamānasā |
svarūpeṇaiva taṃ draṣṭuṃ praṇatā samupācarat || 32.151{50} ||
tatra taṃ sugataṃ puṣyaṃ tathāgataṃ munīśvaram |
dvātriṃśallakṣaṇāśītivyaṃjanaiḥ parimaṇḍitam || 32.152{51} ||
sarvātikrāṃtasaumyābhaṃ śatasūryādhikaprabham |
śāntarūpaṃ subhadrāṃgaṃ dhyānālīnamapaśyata || 32.153{52} ||
dṛṣṭvaiva sā prasādantī puṇyātmāyaṃ susiddhimān |
iti dhyātvā surūpeṇa tadantikamupācarat || 32.154{53} ||
tatra sā sāṃjalirnatvā pādau tasya muneḥ puraḥ |
sthitvā kṣamārthanāṃ karttuṃ prārthayadevamānatā || 32.155{54} ||
bhagavannātha sarvajña yanmayā prakṛtaṃ tvayi |
tatkṣamasva jagannātha kṣāntidharmādhipo'si hi || 32.156{55} ||
tadatrāhaṃ jagacchāstarbhavatāṃ śaraṇaṃ gatā |
sarvadā samupāśritya bhajeyaṃ śraddhayā mudā || 32.157{56} ||
yadatra te sasaṃghasya yathāvidhi samarcitum |
ichāmyahaṃ jagannātha tadadhyuṣitumarhati || 32.158{57} ||
iti tenārthite puṣyo bhagavān sa munīśvaraḥ |
tāṃ viśuddhāśayāṃ dṛṣṭvā tūṣṇībhūtvādhyuvāsa tat || 32.159{58} ||
tathādhivāsitaṃ śāstrā matvā sābhyanumoditā |
āśu tadbhojyasāmagrīṃ sahasā samasādhayat || 32.160{59} ||
tataḥ sā muditopetya pūjāṃgaistaṃ munīśvaram |
abhyarcya bhojanairdivyaiḥ sasaṃghaṃ samatoṣayat || 32.161{60} ||
tataḥ sā bhojanānte'pi praṇatvā sāñjalirmudā |
bhūtvā kṣamāpayitvā ca prārthayadevamānatā || 32.162{61} ||
bhagavannātha sarvajña bhavatāṃ śaraṇe sthitā |
sadopasthānamādhāya bhajiṣyāmi samāhitā || 32.163{62} ||
tatkṣamasva jagannātha yanmayāpakṛtaṃ vṛthā |
prasīdatu bhavāñchāstā saṃbuddho hi kṣamākaraḥ || 32.164{63} ||
iti tayārthitaṃ śrutvā puṣyaḥ sa bhagavānmuniḥ |
devatāṃ tāṃ samālokya saddharmaṃ samupādiśat || 32.165{64} ||
śṛṇuṣva devate dharmamihāmutra śubhāptaye |
dharmeṇa rakṣitā loke sarvatrāpi sukhī bhavet || 32.166{65} ||
tadādau śraddhayā dharmaṃ śrotavyaṃ saugataṃ varam |
tatastriratnamabhyarcya dātavyaṃ dānamarthine || 32.167{66} ||
tataḥ śuddhatrikāyaḥ syācchuddhaśīlaḥ śubhāśayaḥ |
tataḥ satyasamādhānaḥ sarvasatvakṣamākaraḥ || 32.168{67} ||
saṃbodhisādhanodyogātsarvakleśān vinirjayet |
tato dhyānasamādhisthaḥ prajñāratnamavāpnuyāt || 32.169{68} ||
tatsuratnānubhāvena sarvamārān vinirjayan |
sarvasatvahitārthena saṃbuddhapadamāpnuyāt || 32.170{69} ||
evaṃ matvātra saṃsāre sarvadā śubhavāñchibhiḥ |
dharmaṃ śrutvā sadā dānaṃ prakarttavyaṃ yathepsitam || 32.171{70} ||
tvaṃ cāpyevaṃ sadā saukhyaṃ yadīchasi śubhānvitam |
triratnaṃ bhajanaṃ kṛtvā kuru dānaṃ yathepsitam || 32.172{71} ||
etatpuṇyaṃ tu saṃbodhiprāptaye pariṇāmaya |
etatpuṇyavipākena nūnaṃ bodhiṃ samāpnuyāḥ || 32.173{72} ||
ityādiṣṭaṃ munīndreṇa śrutvā sā devatā mudā |
tathā hītipratijñāya prābhyanandatprabodhitā || 32.174{73} ||
tataḥ sa sugataḥ puṣyastathāgataḥ sasāṃghikaḥ |
prabhāsayan samutthāya svāśrame samupāśrayat || 32.175{74} ||
eṣa hyayaṃ virūpo'sau devatā yā guhāśritā |
yuṣmābhiḥ satyameveti manyatāṃ nānyathā khalu || 32.176{75} ||
evaṃ matvātra saṃsāre sarvadā sukhavāñchibhiḥ |
triratnabhajanaṃ kṛtvā caritavyaṃ śubhe sadā || 32.177{76} ||
śubhasya karmaṇaḥ pāke śubhataivaṃ sadā bhave |
kṛṣṇasya duḥkhataivaṃ hi miśritasyāpi miśritam || 32.178{77} ||
abhuktaṃ kṣīyate naiva karma kvāpi kadā cana |
yenaiva yatkṛtaṃ karma tenaiva bhujyate phalam || 32.179{78} ||
nāgnibhirdahyate karma klidyate nāpi codakaiḥ |
śuṣyate vāyubhirnaiva kṣīyate ca na bhūmiṣu || 32.180{79} ||
nānyathāpi bhave naiva karma kvāpi kathaṃ cana |
yadyathā prakṛtaṃ karmaṃ tattathaiva kuleddhruvam || 32.181{80} ||
yadasau devatā tatra guhāśritā durāśayā |
vikṛtāṅgā virūpākṣā bhūtvā puṣyaṃ vyabhīṣapat || 32.182{81} ||
etatpāpavipākena paṃcajanmaśatānyapi |
vikṛtāṅgo virūpākṣo babhūvāyaṃ bhave sadā || 32.183{82} ||
etatpāpāvimukto'yaṃ virūpo vikṛtākṛtiḥ |
vibhransyo durbhagākāro bhavatyatrāpi sāṃpratam || 32.184{83} ||
yaccāsau devatā paścāttāpasaṃtapitāśayā |
tasya puṣyasya buddhasya prasannā śaraṇaṃ gatā || 32.185{84} ||
śraddhayā bhojanairdivyaiḥ satkṛtya prābhajanmudā |
etatpuṇyavipākena lebhe'nantaṃ sukhaṃ bhave || 32.186{85} ||
atrāpi śāsane bauddhe śraddhayā samupāgataḥ |
pravrajyāsaṃvaraprāptaḥ sākṣādarhatvamāptavān || 32.187{86} ||
iti yūyaṃ parijñāya caradhvaṃ sarvadā śubhe |
tato bodhiṃ samāsādya saṃbuddhapadamāpnuyuḥ || 32.188{87} ||
ityādiṣṭaṃ munīndreṇa sarve te sāṃghikā api |
śrutvānumoditāḥ satyamityuktvā parimenire || 32.189{88} ||
iti me guruṇādiṣṭaṃ mayātra kathyate |
tvayāpyevaṃ mahārāja caritavyaṃ śubhe sadā || 32.190{89} ||
prajāścāpi tathā rājan bodhayitvā prayatnataḥ |
bodhimārge pratiṣṭhāpya pālanīyaḥ sadādarāt || 32.191{90} ||
tena te sarvadā nityaṃ sarvatrāpi śubhaṃ bhavet |
kramādbodhiṃ samāsādya saṃbuddhapadamāpnuyāḥ || 32.192{91} ||
iti tenārhatādiṣṭaṃ śrutvāśokaḥ sa bhūpatiḥ |
tathā hīti pratijñāya prābhyanandatprabodhitaḥ || 32.193{92} ||
śāstrādiṣṭaṃ prasannā idamapi manuja ye virūpāvadānaṃ śṛṇvanti śrāvayanti pramuditamanaso ye ca puṇyābhirāgāḥ |
sarve te śrīsametāḥ sakalaguṇadharāḥ satsukhāni prabhuktvā saṃbuddhabhaktiraktāḥ sugatavaragṛhe saṃprayānti pramodam || 32.194{93} ||
iti ratnāvadānatatve virūpāvadānaṃ samāptam
kṣemāvadāna
athāśoko mahārājaḥ kṛtāṃjaliḥ puraḥ sthitaḥ |
upaguptaṃ yatiṃ natvā punarevamabhāṣata || 33.1{1} ||
bhadanta śrotumichāmi punaranyatsubhāṣitam |
tadyathā guruṇākhyātaṃ tathādeṣṭuṃ samarhasi || 33.2{2} ||
iti saṃprārthite rājñā śrutvā so'rhan sudhīryatiḥ |
aśokaṃ taṃ mahīpālaṃ samālokyaivamabravīt || 33.3{3} ||
śṛṇu rājanmahābhāga yathā me guruṇoditam |
tathāhaṃ tatpravakṣyāmi tava dharmavivṛddhaye || 33.4{4} ||
tadyathābhūtpurā pūryyāṃ śrāvastyāṃ kauśalādhipaḥ |
prasenajiditi khyāto mahārājā mahīpatiḥ || 33.5{5} ||
vārāṇasyāṃ tadācāro brahmadattābhidho nṛpaḥ |
mahārājo mahīpālanṛpādhipastadābhavat || 33.6{6} ||
tadā tayormahībhoktroḥ parasparaviruddhayoḥ |
mahāhavasamāraṃbhādbabhūva kṣubhitāśayam || 33.7{7} ||
tatra sa kṣatriyo rājā prasenajinnarādhipaḥ |
svastyayanavidhiṃ kṛtvā sarvāntaḥpurikānvitaḥ || 33.8{8} ||
caturaṃgavalaiḥ sārddhaṃ mahotsāhaṃ praṇādayan |
vaijayantīṃ samucchrāya kośalāviṣayāntataḥ || 33.9{9} ||
paryante sahasā gatvā vīryotsāhasamanvitaḥ |
vadhvā kāṣṭapaṭaṃ tatra prāvatasthe pure yathā || 33.10{10} ||
tacchrutvā brahmadatto'pi kṣatriyo bhūpatirnṛpaḥ |
svastyayanavidhiṃ dhṛtvā svāntaḥpurasamanvitaḥ || 33.11{11} ||
caturaṃgavalaiḥ sārddhaṃ jayotsāhaṃ ninādayan |
vaijayantīṃ samucchrāya nadyā tīramupācarat || 33.12{12} ||
tatraiva samupāśritya vīryotsāhasamanvitaḥ |
vadhvā kāṣṭapaṭaṃ tatra saṃtasthe nagare yathā || 33.13{13} ||
tatra sa bhūpatī rājā brahmadatto narādhipaḥ |
agrarājñyā sahāraktaḥ saṃreme paricārayan || 33.14{14} ||
tadā sa mahiṣī rājñā saha krīḍāratā satī |
svalpāhāraratā gaurā dohadalakṣaṇaṃ dadhau || 33.15{15} ||
tathā tasmin kṣaṇe so'pi prasenajinnarādhipaḥ |
mahiṣyā saha saṃraktaḥ prareme paricārayan || 33.16{16} ||
tadā sāpi mahārājñī svāmikrīḍānucāriṇī |
svalpāhārotsahā gaurā dadhau dohadalakṣaṇam || 33.17{17} ||
kramātto'pi mahārājñyāṃ pravṛddhagarbhahārite |
pīnastane haridrābhavarṇe'bhūtāṃ kṛśāṅgike || 33.18{18} ||
tato'gramahiṣī rājño brahmadattasya bhūpateḥ |
sā sūta samaye putraṃ divyāṃśamatisuṃdaram || 33.19{19} ||
tacchrutvā sa bhūpālo brahmadattaḥ pramoditaḥ |
sahasopetya taṃ putraṃ dṛṣṭvā na tṛptimāyayau || 33.20{20} ||
tasminneva dine tasya prasenajinmahīpateḥ |
bhāryāpi samaye sūta sutāṃ divyātisuṃdarīm || 33.21{21} ||
strīratnalakṣaṇopetaṃ subhadrāṃgīmanoharām |
ramopamaṃ śubhākārāṃ manāpāṃ ratisaṃnibhām || 33.22{22} ||
tacchrutvā sa mahīpālaḥ prasenajitprasāditaḥ |
sahasopetya tāṃ putrīṃ dadarsātipramoditaḥ || 33.23{23} ||
tāṃ dṛṣṭvā sa nareṃdro'pi me vabhūva samāgataḥ |
tāmeva suciraṃ paśyannavatasthe suniścalaḥ || 33.24{24} ||
tayorapyubhayo rājñoḥ skaṃdhācārāḥ pravarttitāḥ |
ye nityaṃ tatpravṛttāṃtaṃ svaprabhorupahārakāḥ || 33.25{25} ||
ye prasenajitaścārā brahmadattasya bhūpateḥ |
putro jāta iti śrutvā parijñāya prasedire || 33.26{26} ||
te prasenajito rājñaḥ sahasā samupāgatāḥ |
kṛtāṃjalipuṭo natvā pura evaṃ nyavedayan || 33.27{27} ||
jaya deva mahārāja śṛṇu tatra pravarttitam |
brahmadattasya bhūpasya putro jāto'dhunā khalu || 33.28{28} ||
sāṃprataṃ tatra rājendraśubhotsāhaṃ pravarttate |
tattvayā saha saṃvaṃdhaṃ kṛtvā sa svapuraṃ vrajet || 33.29{29} ||
tatprasīda mahārāja mā vibhaiṣīḥ śubhaṃ bhavet |
tena saṃdhiṃ bhavān kṛtvā viharatāṃ yathāsukham || 33.30{30} ||
iti taiḥ kathitaṃ śrutvā sa prasenajinnarādhipaḥ |
satyametaditi jñātvā prasasāda'viśaṃkitaḥ || 33.31{31} ||
tathā ye brahmadattasya gūḍhacārāḥ pravarttitāḥ |
te prasenajitaḥ putrī jāteti śuśruvustadā || 33.32{31} ||
tataste sahasā tatra brahmadattasya bhūpateḥ |
purataḥ samupāśritya praṇatvaivaṃ nyavedayan || 33.33{33} ||
jaya deva mahārāja śṛṇu tatra pravarttitam |
prasenajinmahīpasya putrī jātā'dhunā khalu || 33.34{34} ||
dārikā sā subhadrāṃgī divyakāmā ramopamā |
sarvalakṣaṇasaṃpannā suṃdarī ratisaṃnibhā || 33.35{35} ||
tena tatra śubhotsāhaṃ pravarttate'dhunā prabho |
tena sahātra saṃvaṃdhaṃ karttumarhati sarvathā || 33.36{36} ||
iti taiḥ kathitaṃ śrutvā brahmadatto narādhipaḥ |
bhadramiti parijñāya tatraivaṃ samaciṃtayat || 33.37{37} ||
aho citra mahotsāho bhadramekasmin divase yataḥ |
jāto me'tra suto bhadrastasyāpi jāyate sutā || 33.38{38} ||
tattāṃ bhadrāṃ ramākārāṃ taṃ prasenajitamādarat |
prārthayitvātra putrāya sandhiṃ kṛtvā carevahi || 33.39{39} ||
iti niścitya sa brahmadatto rājā prasāditaḥ |
sujanaṃ dūtamāmaṃtrya pura evaṃ samabravīt || 33.40{40} ||
gacha dūta mahīpālaṃ taṃ prasenajitamādarāt |
mamaivaṃ vacasopetya prārthayasva prabodhayan || 33.41{41} ||
brahmadatto mahīpālo vadatyevaṃ samādarāt |
bhavatā saha saṃvaṃdhaṃ karttumichāmi sāṃpratam || 33.42{42} ||
yadekasmin dine jātau dārakāvāvayorapi |
sutā te bhadrikā khyātā mama putro'pi bhadrikaḥ || 33.43{43} ||
tadetayorhi saṃvaṃdhaṃ karttumarhāvahe dhruvam |
tadbhavānmama putrāya svātmajāṃ dātumarhati || 33.44{44} ||
evaṃ kṛte'pi saṃvaṃdhe yāvajjīvaṃ sadāvayoḥ |
vairotsargo bhavennūnaṃ prītisaṃdhirna bhetsyati || 33.45{45} ||
tadāvayoḥ sadā bhadraṃ viṣayeṣu samaṃtataḥ |
nirītikaṃ śubhotsāhaṃ bhaviṣyati na saṃśayaḥ || 33.46{46} ||
iti me vacasā rājā prasenajitsa kauśalaḥ |
prārthanīyastvayā sādho gachāstu te sumaṃgalam || 33.47{47} ||
ityādiṣṭaṃ narendreṇa śrutvā dūtaḥ sa bodhitaḥ |
tathetyuktvā nṛpaṃ natvā samutthāya tato'carat || 33.48{48} ||
tatra sa sahasā gatvā taṃ prasenajitaṃ nṛpam |
upetya sāṃjalirnatvā purastha evamabravīt || 33.49{49} ||
jaya deva mahārāja yadarthe'hamihāgataḥ |
tadarthaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi bhavāṃ śrotuṃ samarhati || 33.50{50} ||
brahmadatto mahārājo bhavatāṃ kuśalaṃ mudā |
pṛchati sarvadā nityaṃ sarvatra viṣayeṣvapi || 33.51{51} ||
punarevaṃ sa bhūpālo brahmadatto bravītyapi |
bhavatā saha saṃvaṃdhaṃ kartumichāmi sāṃpratam || 33.52{52} ||
yadekasmin dine jātau dārakāvāvayoriha |
sutā te bhadrikā khyāto mama putro'pi bhadrikaḥ || 33.53{53} ||
tadetayorhi saṃvaṃdhaṃ kartumarhāmahe dhruvam |
tadbhavānmama putrāya svātmajāṃ dātumarhati || 33.54{54} ||
evaṃ kṛte'pi saṃvaṃdhe yāvajjīvaṃ sadāvayoḥ |
vairotsargo bhavennūnaṃ prītisaṃdhirna bhetsyate || 33.55{55} ||
tadāvayoḥ sadā bhadraṃ viṣayeṣu samaṃtataḥ |
nirītikaṃ śubhotsāhaṃ bhavedeva na saṃśayaḥ || 33.56{56} ||
iti me vacasā rājā prasenajitsa kauśalaḥ |
prārthanīyastvayā sādho gachāstu tvaṃ samāhitaḥ || 33.57{57} ||
ityādiśya narendreṇa brahmadattena bhūrbhujā |
preṣito'haṃ mahārāja bhavatāmaṃtike khalu || 33.58{58} ||
tattena bhūpatīśena yathādiṣṭaṃ narādhipa |
bhavānapi tathā kartumarhatyedanna nānyathā || 33.59{59} ||
evaṃ hi bhavatāṃ bhadraṃ yāvājjīvaṃ sukhaiḥ sadā |
sarvatra maṅgalotsāhaṃ bhavennūnaṃ na saṃśayaḥ || 33.60{60} ||
tadbhavānapi rājendra brahmadattasya bhūpateḥ |
putrāya svātmajāṃ bhadrāṃ saṃpradātumihārhati || 33.61{61} ||
iti tena samākhyātaṃ śrutvā sa kośalādhipaḥ |
tuṣṇībhūtvā kṣaṇaṃ tatra manasaivaṃ vyaciṃtayet || 33.62{62} ||
aho me jāyate kṣemaṃ yadasau kṣatriyādhipaḥ |
vairaṃ vihāya saṃvandhaṃ karttumichati sāṃpratam || 33.63{63} ||
tadahaṃ tena bhūpena yathādiṣṭaṃ tathā'dhunā |
datvaināṃ svātmajāṃ bhadrāṃ saṃdhiṃ kuryāṃ śubhāptaye || 33.64{64} ||
evaṃ kṛte sadā kṣemaṃ sarvatra viṣayeṣvapi |
mama tasyāpi bhūpasya bhavennūnaṃ na cānyathā || 33.65{65} ||
iti dhyātvā viniścitya prasenajitsa kauśalaḥ |
taṃ dūtaṃ samupāmaṃtrya samālokyaivamabravīt || 33.66{66} ||
sādho tena narendreṇa yathādiṣṭaṃ tathādarāt |
ājñāṃ śirasā dhṛtivā kariṣyāmi sadāpyaham || 33.67{67} ||
evaṃ me vacasā sādho sarvametatsuvistaram |
samākhyāya narendraṃ taṃ prasādayitumarhasi || 33.68{68} ||
iti tena narendreṇa samākhyātaṃ niśamya saḥ |
dūtastatheti vijñapya prābhyanandatprasāditaḥ || 33.69{69} ||
tataḥ sa dūtastaṃ bhūpaṃ kośalādhipatiṃ mudā |
natvā dṛṣṭvā samāmaṃtrya tato'caratpramoditaḥ || 33.70{70} ||
tataḥ sa sahasā gatvā brahmadattasya bhūpateḥ |
upetya sāṃjaliḥ pādau praṇatvevamabhāṣata || 33.71{71} ||
jaya deva mahārāja diṣṭyā varddhasva sāṃpratam |
yathābhilaṣitaṃ sarvaṃ tatsiddhamiti manyatām || 33.72{72} ||
tadyathā bhavatādiṣṭaṃ tathā vijñāpitaṃ mayā |
tacchrutvānena bhūpena pramoditvādhyuvāsitam || 33.73{73} ||
iti satyaṃ samādhāya pālayasva yathoditam |
sarvadā te bhavedbhadraṃ sarvatra viṣayeṣvapi || 33.74{74} ||
iti tenoditaṃ śrutvā brahmadattaḥ sa bhūpatiḥ |
prabodhito'numoditvā prābhyanaṃdatprasāditaḥ || 33.75{75} ||
tataḥ sa bhūpatī rājā brahmadatto mahotsavaiḥ |
caturaṃgavalaiḥ sārddhaṃ jayavādyaṃ pravādayan || 33.76{76} ||
tataḥ saṃprasthito lokaiḥ stūyamānaḥ surairapi |
pracaran sahasā tatra svapuraṃ samupāyayau || 33.77{77} ||
tataḥ so'pi mahīpālaḥ prasenajitpramoditaḥ |
caturaṃgavalaiḥ sārddhaṃ jayotsāhaṃ praṇādayan || 33.78{78} ||
tataḥ pratyāgato lokai stūyamāno'marairapi |
pracaran sa nṛpo tatra śrāvastyāṃ samupācarat || 33.79{79} ||
tatra svanagare prāptaḥ prasenajitsa kauśalaḥ |
svaprāsādatalāsīnaḥ sarvāntapurikānvitaḥ || 33.80{80} ||
tāṃ bhadarikāṃ sutāṃ dṛṣṭvā suprasannāśayo mudā |
aho kṣemaṃ prajātaṃ me ityevaṃ muhurabravīt || 33.81{81} ||
tatastasyāḥ sutāyāḥ sa kṛtvā jātimahaṃ mudā |
sarvañjñātīn samāhūya pura evamupādiśat || 33.82{82} ||
bhadantaḥ śrūyatāmasyā bhadrāyā duhiturmama |
yathāyuktaṃ nāmadheyaṃ sthāpayata prasiddhitam || 33.83{83} ||
iti tena narendreṇa samādiṣṭaṃ niśamyate |
saṃpaśyañjñātayastatra saṃmīlyaivaṃ samabruvan || 33.84{84} ||
yasmin dine prajāteyaṃ tasminneva dine yataḥ |
kṣemaṃ no jāyate tasmātkṣemābhidhā bhavantviyam || 33.85{85} ||
iti te jñātayaḥ sarve kṛtvaivaṃ saṃmataṃ tataḥ |
kauśalendraṃ tamāmaṃtrya praṇatvaivaṃ samabruvan || 33.86{86} ||
śṛṇu rājan yadasmākamabhiprāyaṃ pracakṣyate |
tadasyā nāmadheyaṃ karotu bhuvi viśrutam || 33.87{87} ||
yasmin dine prajāteyaṃ kṣemaṃ bhavati sarvataḥ |
tenāsyā kriyatāṃ nāma kṣemeti viśrutaṃ bhuvi || 33.88{88} ||
tathā tairgaditaṃ śrutvā rājā sa janako mudā |
tasyāḥ putryāstathā nāma kṣemeti prārthitaṃ vyadhāt || 33.89{89} ||
tataḥ sā dārikāṣṭābhirdhātribhiḥ pratipālitā |
paripuṣṭā pravṛddhābhūddhradasthamiva paṃkajam || 33.90{90} ||
tathā sa brahmadatto'pi rājā svāntargṛhe gataḥ |
taṃ putrāṃ muhurālokya prābhyanaṃdatpramoditaḥ || 33.91{91} ||
tatra tasya śiśo rājā kṛtvā jātimahaṃ pitā |
sarvāñjñātīn samāhūya pura evamabhāṣata || 33.92{92} ||
bhavaṃto dārakasyāsya putrasya me śubhākṛteḥ |
kriyatāṃ nāmadheyan sanmaṃgalākṣarasaṃyutam || 33.93{93} ||
ityādiṣṭaṃ narendreṇa śrutvā te jñātayo mudā |
sarve'pyekatra saṃgamya mitharevaṃ samabruvan || 33.94{94} ||
yadayaṃ jātamātro'pi kṣemaṃ karoti sarvataḥ |
tathāsya bhavatu kṣemaṃkara ityāhvayaṃ dhruvam || 33.95{95} ||
iti te jñātayaḥ sarve brahmadattasya bhūpateḥ |
purataḥ samupāsṛtya praṇatvaivaṃ nyavedayan || 33.96{96} ||
śṛṇu rājan yadasmābhirnāmāsya kriyate śiśoḥ |
tannāma kṣemasaṃpannaṃ prathayatu bhāvān bhuvi || 33.97{97} ||
yadayaṃ jātamātro'pi kṣemaṃ karoti sarvataḥ |
tena kṣemaṃkarākhyo'yaṃ bhavaṃtu lokanaṃdanaḥ || 33.98{98} ||
etattaiḥ kathitaṃ śrutvā brahmadattaḥ sa bhūpatiḥ |
tadeva nāme putrasya tasyākarotpravisṛtam || 33.99{99} ||
tataḥ sa dārako'ṣṭābhi dhātribhiḥ pratipālitaḥ |
paripuṣṭaḥ pravṛddho'bhūddhradasthaṃ kamalaṃ yathā || 33.100{100} ||
tataḥ kramātkumāratvamāptavān sa nṛpātmajaḥ |
gurūṇāṃ samupāśritya lipī kramādaśikṣat || 33.101{1} ||
yathākramamadhītyevaṃ kṣemaṃkaraḥ sa buddhimān |
sarvaśāstrakalāsvādi vidyāparaṃ yayau laghu || 33.102{2} ||
tataḥ sa daharaḥ kāntaḥ kumāro ratilālitaḥ |
savayeḥsacivaiḥ sārddhaṃ reme pure paribhraman || 33.103{3} ||
tadā sa dārikā kṣemā prasenajitsutāpi ca |
kramādabhūtkumārītvaprāptā kāntābhisuṃdarī || 33.104{4} ||
tataḥ sā bhadrikā kanyā sumatirguṇalālasāḥ |
gurūṇāṃ samupāśritya kramāllipiraśikṣata || 33.105{5} ||
yathākramamadhītyaivaṃ sā subuddhimatī satī |
sarvaśāstrakalāvidyāpāraṃ yayau vicakṣaṇā || 33.106{6} ||
tadā sā bhāvinī kṣemā svasakhībhiḥ samanvitā |
saṃbuddhabhāṣitaṃ dharmamanuśrutvā mudānamat || 33.107{7} ||
tadā sa brahmadattasya kumāro'tivicakṣaṇaḥ |
tāṃ kṣemāṃ suṃdarīṃ draṣṭuṃ samaichadratilālasaḥ || 33.108{8} ||
tataḥ sa dūtamāhūya bodhayitvā prasādayan |
datvā suratnamālāṃ taṃ kṣemāyāḥ preṣayetsvayam || 33.109{9} ||
tataḥ sa dūta ādāya tāṃ mālāṃ sahasā caran |
śrāvastyāḥ puri saṃprāptaḥ kṣemāyāḥ sadane'carat || 33.110{10} ||
tatra sa dūta ālokya sakhīmekāmupāgatām |
tāmupetya samāmaṃtrya suguptamevamabravīt || 33.111{11} ||
kṣemaṃkarakumāreṇa kṣemāye prahitāmimām |
ratnamālāṃ tvamādāya bhadre tasyai samarpaya || 33.112{12} ||
ityuktvā ratnamālāṃ tāṃ kṣemāsakhīkare rahaḥ |
datvā sa dūta āmaṃtrya sahasā svapuraṃ yayau || 33.113{13} ||
tatra sāpi sakhī tasyāḥ kṣemāyāḥ purato gatā |
prābhṛtaṃ tamupasthāpya rahasyevaṃ nyavedayat || 33.114{14} ||
ayi bhadramimaṃ paśya prahitaṃ svāminā tava |
tadidaṃ tvaṃ samāśliṣya patimiva vibhāvaya || 33.115{15} ||
iti tayoditaṃ śrutvā sā kṣemā lajjitāśayā |
kāmaviṣāgnisaṃtrastā tasthau dinamadhomukhā || 33.116{16} ||
tataḥ sā kanyakā kṣemā tāṃ sakhīṃ purataḥ sthitām |
sucirātsamupārabhya hartsayantyevamabravīt || 33.117{17} ||
are re kiṃ vadasyevaṃ svāmī me kātra vidyate |
nilajje vada tatkena prahitaṃ prābhṛtaṃ tava || 33.118{18} ||
ityuktaṃ sā sakhī śrutvā kṣemāyā durituḥ prabhoḥ |
ājanme sarvavṛttāṃtaṃ vistareṇa nyavedayat || 33.119{19} ||
evaṃ sakhyā samākhyātaṃ śrutvā sā nṛpanaṃdinī |
kṣemā saṃsārasaṃcārasamudvignāśayābhavat || 33.120{20} ||
tata utthāya sā kṣemā kanyāpi sahasā pituḥ |
upetya caraṇau natvā sāṃjalirevamabravīt || 33.121{21} ||
tāta kāmārthinī nāhamapi tu sadguṇārthinī |
sadguṇāṃ sādhayiṣyāmi tadanujñāṃ dadātu me || 33.122{22} ||
yadahaṃ śāsane bauddhe pravrajitvā samāhita |
brahmacaryaṃ samādhāya prechāmi carituṃ prabho || 33.123{23} ||
tadbhavāṃ kṛpayā tāta saṃbodhivratasādhane |
anujñāṃ saṃpradatvme sarvathānugrahaṃ kuru || 33.124{24} ||
ityarthitaṃ tayā putryā śrutvā sa kośalādhipaḥ |
tāṃ kumārīṃ samālokya tasthau saṃdigdhamānasaḥ || 33.125{25} ||
tataḥ sa janako rājā tāṃ kṣemāṃ svātmajāṃ priyām |
bodhayituṃ samāmaṃtrya samālokyaivamabravīt || 33.126{26} ||
naivaṃ putri mayā kartuṃ śakyate tatkṣamasva me |
yatte janmani me rāṣṭre kṣemamevaṃ prajāyate || 33.127{27} ||
tadāpi tvaṃ pratijñaya brahmadattasya bhūpateḥ |
kṣemaṃkarāya putrāya saṃkalpitā mayātmaje || 33.128{28} ||
tadyathā me pratijñātaṃ vacanaṃ pūrayātmaje |
mā kṛthāḥ sarvathā kṣeme pravrajyāsādhane mama || 33.129{29} ||
yathā mayā pratijñātaṃ kariṣye na tadanyathā |
matveti me vacaḥ śrutvā kuladharme carātmaje || 33.130{30} ||
evaṃ pitroditaṃ śrutvā sā kṣemā prahatāśayā |
utthāya svālaye gatvā tūṣṇībhūtā nyaṣīdata || 33.131{31} ||
tadā sa janako rājā dūtamāhūya sādaram |
sarvametatpravṛttāṃtaṃ samākhyāyaivamabravīt || 33.132{32} ||
gacha sādho narendrasya brahmadattasya bhūpateḥ |
pura etatpravṛttāṃtaṃ nivedyaivaṃ prabodhaya || 33.133{33} ||
yadiyaṃ me sutā kṣemā pravrajituṃ samichati |
tadimāṃ sahasāgatvā bhavānādātumarhati || 33.134{34} ||
iti rājñā samādiṣṭaṃ śrutvā puraḥ sa sādaram |
tatheti saṃpratijñāya praṇatvā taṃ tato'carat || 33.135{35} ||
tatra sa sahasā gatvā brahmadattasya bhūpateḥ |
upetya sāṃjalirnatvā pura evaṃ nyavedayat || 33.136{36} ||
jaya eva yadarthe'haṃ preṣito'tra mahībhṛtā |
kośalendreṇa tadbhavāṃ śṛṇotvevaṃ bravīti saḥ || 33.137{37} ||
yadiyaṃ me sutā kṣemā pravrajituṃ samichati |
tadimāṃ sahasāgatvā bhavānāhartumarhati || 33.138{38} ||
yathā mayā pratijñātaṃ karttuṃ na śakyate'nyathā |
tadahaṃ te kumārāya dāsyāmi svātmajāmimām || 33.139{39} ||
evaṃ tena nareṃdreṇa samākhyāya bhavatpuraḥ |
preṣito'hamiti jñātvā tattatrāgaṃtumarhati || 33.140{40} ||
iti tenoditaṃ śrutvā brahmadattaḥ sa bhūpatiḥ |
taṃ dūtaṃ sahasāmaṃtrya pura evamabhāṣata || 33.141{41} ||
evaṃ cedāgamiṣyāmi saputraḥ saptame'ham |
yattatkṛtyaṃ hi tatsarvaṃ karttavyamiti kathyatām || 33.142{42} ||
ityādiṣṭaṃ nareṃdreṇa brahmadattena sāṃjaliḥ |
natvā tatheti vijñapya samutthāya tato'carat || 33.143{43} ||
tatra sa sahasā gatvā svapuraṃ samupācaran |
nṛpatiṃ sāṃjalirnatvā pura evaṃ nyavedayat || 33.144{44} ||
jaya deva yathādiṣṭaṃ brahmadattasya bhūpateḥ |
tathā vijñāpitaṃ sarvaṃ taduktaṃ yācyate śṛṇu || 33.145{45} ||
evaṃ cedāgamiṣyāmi saputraḥ saptame'hani |
tadyatkṛtyaṃ hi tatsarvaṃ sādhayituṃ tvamarhasi || 33.146{46} ||
iti tena narendreṇa pratijñāya nideśitam |
iti satyaṃ parijñāya sarvaṃ kṛtyaṃ prakāraya || 33.147{47} ||
iti tenoditaṃ śrutvā prasenajitsa bhūpatiḥ |
tadarhasarvasāmagrīṃ sahasā samasādhayat || 33.148{48} ||
tadaitatsarvavṛttāṃtaṃ śrutvā cā dārikā sudhīḥ |
udvignamānasā kṣemā manasaivaṃ vyaciṃtayat || 33.149{49} ||
hā kiṃ mayātra karttavyaṃ vivāhāṃ me bhave yataḥ |
kāmabhogaṃ parityajya vāṃchāmi saugatavratam || 33.150{50} ||
kāmā hi garhyate sadbhi varṇyate saugataṃ vratam |
tadatra kiṃ kariṣyāmi yanme na gocaraṃ vrate || 33.151{51} ||
dhigjanma tasya saṃsāre yasya na gocaraṃ vrate |
vrataṃ vinātra kiṃ sāraṃ kāmabhogyai guṇairapi || 33.152{52} ||
tadatra kva gamiṣyāmi nimagnā kāmapaṃkile |
ko me trātā bhavedatra saṃbuddhādaparaḥ kṛtī || 33.153{53} ||
tadatra taṃ jagannāthaṃ sarvajñaṃ sarvatāyinam |
saṃsmṛtvā śaraṇaṃ gatvā prārthaye sādhunādarāt || 33.154{54} ||
nūnaṃ buddho jagannātho jagacchāstā munīśvaraḥ |
kṛpayā māṃ samālokya buddhavrate niyojayet || 33.155{55} ||
yadi me daivayogena vrataṃ naiva labheya hi |
saṃbuddhaśaraṇodbhūtaṃ puṇyaṃ tu labhyate dhruvam || 33.156{56} ||
saṃbuddhasmṛtijaṃ puṇyaṃ na kṣiṇoti kadā cana |
saṃbuddhasaṃvaraṃ dadyāditi prajñairnigadyate || 33.157{57} ||
tadahaṃ taṃ jagannāthaṃ smṛtvātra maraṇaṃ varam |
na tu kāmaviṣaṃ bhokṣye bhojye puṇyāmṛtaṃ dhruvam || 33.158{58} ||
iti niścitya sā kṣemā snātvā śuddhāmvarāvṛtā |
śaraṇapṛṣṭhamāruhya abhavaddinmukhā sthitā || 33.159{59} ||
jānubhyāṃ bhuvi saṃsthitā kṛtāṃjalipuṭā mudā |
bhagavantamanusmṛtvā natvaivaṃ prārthayattridhā || 33.160{60} ||
bhagavannātha sarvajña sarvasatvānukaṃpaka |
brahmacārinmahābhijña maharṣe duritāntakaḥ || 33.161{61} ||
praśamopāyavidvijña śānta citta mahāmune |
tvameva hi jagannātha tatte'haṃ śaraṇaṃ vraje || 33.162{62} ||
tanmāṃ bhavodadhau magnāṃ kṛpayoddhartumarhati |
iti saṃprārthite satyo kanyayā kṣemayā tayā |
bhagavān kṛpayā dṛṣṭyā dadarśa tāṃ śubhāśayām || 33.163{63} ||
tataḥ sa bhagavān dṛṣṭvā tāṃ kṣemāṃ bodhibhāvinīm |
ṛddhyākaśātsamāgatya tatra kṣemāntike yayau || 33.164{64} ||
tatra taṃ śrīghanaṃ dṛṣṭvā sā kṣemā sahasotthitā |
prajñapya svāsanaṃ natvā sāṃjalirevamabravīt || 33.165{65} ||
bhagavannātha saṃbuddha praviśātrāsane śubhe |
prasīda kṛpayapāyānmāṃ samuddhartumarhasi || 33.166{66} ||
iti tasyārthitaṃ śrutvā bhagavān sa munīśvaraḥ |
tāṃ kṣemāṃ bhadrikāṃ kanyāṃ samālokyaivamādiśat || 33.167{67} ||
mā bhaiṣi bhadrike kanye tvadīhitaṃ samṛdhyati |
tathā me śaraṇaṃ gatvā cara śubhe samāhitā || 33.168{68} ||
ityādiśya punastasyāḥ saṃbodhipadasādhanam |
āryyasatyaṃ samārabhya saddharmaṃ samupādiśat || 33.169{69} ||
tadāryyadharmamākarṇya kṣemā paribodhitā |
anāgāmiphalaṃ prāptā labdhābhijñābhavattadā || 33.170{70} ||
tataḥ sā muditā kanyā dṛṣṭasatyā prasāditā |
sāṃjalistaṃ muniṃ natvā prārthayaccaivamādarāt || 33.171{71} ||
bhagavan sarvavinnātha sadaivaṃ kṛpayā bhavān |
dṛṣṭvā māṃ saugate dharme niyojya trātumarhati || 33.172{72} ||
ityarthitaṃ tayā śrutvā bhagavān sa munīśvaraḥ |
tathā hīti pratijñāya tathārddhyā cāśrame yayau || 33.173{73} ||
tadā sā kanyakā kṣemā saṃbuddhaśaraṇaṃ gatā |
saṃsmṛtya śraddhayā nityaṃ prācaranmuditā śubhe || 33.174{74} ||
tadā sa brahmadatto'pi putrapurohitādibhiḥ |
svajanaiḥ sahasotsāhaiḥ śrāvastyāṃ samupācarat || 33.175{75} ||
tadāgataṃ samākarṇya prasenajitsa bhūpatiḥ |
sahasā maṃgalotsāhaiḥ pratyudyayau tamādarāt || 33.176{76} ||
tatra sa kośaleśastaṃ brahmadattaṃ narādhipam |
upetya kuśalaṃ dṛṣṭvā svapure saṃnyaveśayat || 33.177{77} ||
tatrānekāḥ samāyātā lokāḥ sarve pramoditāḥ |
tadvivāhamahotsāhaṃ draṣṭuṃ samupatasthire || 33.178{78} ||
tataḥ sa kauśalo rājā tadvivāhadine śubhe |
sādhitasarvasāmagrīṃ hotuḥ samatyaḍhaukayat || 33.179{79} ||
tadā kṣemā kumārī sā sarvālaṃkārabhūṣitā |
mātrā saha samāgatya tatrāsane samāśrayat || 33.180{80} ||
kṣemaṃkarakumāro'pi sarvālaṃkārabhūṣitaḥ |
pitrā saha samāgatya tatrāsane upāviśat || 33.181{81} ||
tataḥ sa vidhividdhotā śuddhāsane samāśrayan |
vidhināgniṃ samāvāhya sa havyaiḥ samatarpayat || 33.182{82} ||
tataḥ purohito viprastadvelā samupāgate |
kṣemaṃkarāya tāṃ kṣemāṃ saṃkalpayitumārabhat || 33.183{83} ||
yathāvidhi samārabhya brāhmaṇaḥ sa purohitaḥ |
kṣemāyā hastamādāya kumārasyāsya prāgrahīt || 33.184{84} ||
tasminneva kṣaṇe tatra sā kṣemā sahasodgatā |
pakṣivatkhaṃ samāśritya tasthau dhyātvā munīśvaram || 33.185{85} ||
tātra sā bhāsayantyeva sthitā ciraṃ samāhitā |
prātihāryāṇi citrāṇi vividhānyabhyadarśayat || 33.186{86} ||
taddṛṣṭvā kauśalo rājā prasenajitpitāpi saḥ |
brahmadatto narendro'pi purohito dvijo'pi saḥ || 33.187{87} ||
kṣemaṃkaraḥ kumāro'pi sarve'pi brāhmaṇāśca te |
amātyā maṃtriṇaścāpi sarve sainyagaṇā api || 33.188{88} ||
paurāścāpi striyo'nye'pi dārakā dārikā api |
sarve'tivismayākrāṃtahṛdayā avatasthire || 33.189{89} ||
tatra kṣemaṃkaro rājakumāro brāhmaṇaiḥ saha |
tāṃ kṣemāṃ sāṃjalirnatvā prārthayadevamādarāt || 33.190{90} ||
namaste bhagini kṣeme yatte'haṃ kāmalālasāḥ |
pāṇiṃ grahītumichāmi tatkṣamasva dayānidhe || 33.191{91} ||
yadevaṃ tvaṃ mahābhijñāpadaprāptā maharddhikā |
yatkāmān paribhuṃjīthā ityasthānameva hi || 33.192{92} ||
tatkṣamasva tvamākāśādavatīrya mahītale |
sarvalokābhibodhārthaṃ saddharmaṃ samupādiśa || 33.193{93} ||
iti saṃprārthitaṃ tena kumāreṇa niśamya sā |
kṣemā prasannāsyā tatrākāśādavātarat || 33.194{94} ||
tatra sā samupāgatya janakāye sabhāsane |
sthitvāryasatyamārabhya saddharmaṃ samupādiśat || 33.195{95} ||
tatsaddharmāmṛtaṃ pītvā sarve lokāḥ prabodhitāḥ |
satyamiti parijñāya prābhyanandan prasāditāḥ || 33.196{96} ||
tataḥ kṣemaṃkaraḥ pitrā saha taṃ kośalādhipam |
āmaṃtrya tadguṇāhṛṣṭamanāḥ svaṃ nagaraṃ yayau || 33.197{97} ||
tataḥ sā kanyakā kṣemā sāñjaliḥ purato gatā |
pituḥ pādāmbuje natvā prārthayedevamādarāt || 33.198{93} ||
nāhaṃ kāmārthinī tāta saṃbuddhaśaraṇārthinī |
taditaḥ śāsane bauddhe gantumichāmi sāṃpratam || 33.199{99} ||
tadyadi te mayi prītirasti dhārme'pi vā ruciḥ |
tadatra kṛpayānujñāṃ dātumarhati me pita || 33.200{100} ||
iti saṃprārthitaṃ putryā śrutvā sa kauśalādhipaḥ |
tāṃ kṣemāṃ svātmajāṃ kanyāṃ ciraṃ dṛṣṭvaivamabravīt || 33.201{1} ||
kimetra vakṣyate putri yattvaṃ saddharmavāṃchinī |
kiṃ cāpyasi mahābhijñāvatī prajñāvatī sudhīḥ || 33.202{1} ||
kiṃ tu tvaṃ dārikā kanyā śītavātātapāhatā |
tatpravrajyāvrataṃ vālye carituṃ śaknuyāḥ katham || 33.203{3} ||
saddharma yadi te vāṃchā gṛhe sthitvā samāhitā |
triratnabhajanaṃ kṛtvā saddharmaṃ sādhayātmaje || 33.204{4} ||
triratnabhajanodbhūtaṃ puṇyaṃ saddharmasādhanam |
tena saṃbodhimāsādya saṃbuddhapadamāpnuyāḥ || 33.205{5} ||
evaṃ matvā svagehe tvaṃ śuddhaśīlavratāvṛtā |
triratnabhajanaṃ kṛtvā cara nityaṃ śubhe sadā || 33.206{6} ||
iti pitroditaṃ śrutvā śā kṣemā kāmaniḥspṛhā |
pitaraṃ taṃ samālokya punarevamabhāṣata || 33.207{7} ||
na me tāta gṛhāvāse kleśamāragaṇākule |
kṣaṇaṃ sthātuṃ manastena vāṃchati saugataṃ vratam || 33.208{8} ||
sarve'pi saugataḥ santo vihāre sugatāśrame |
sadā dharmāmṛtaṃ bhuktvā vasanti sadguṇodyatāḥ || 33.209{9} ||
ta eva sadguṇādhārā bodhisatvāḥ sukhānvitāḥ |
yāvajjīvaṃ vrataṃ dhṛtvā yānti cānte jinālayam || 33.210{10} ||
iti dṛṣṭvāhamichāmi pravrajituṃ jināśrame |
tadbhavān kṛpayānujñāṃ dātumarhati me prabho || 33.211{11} ||
iti saṃprārthitaṃ putryā śrutvā sa bodhitaḥ pitā |
tathānumodanāṃ kṛtvā tāṃ kṣemāmevamabravīt || 33.212{12} ||
kiṃ mayā vakṣyate vatse tvamevaṃ satsubhārthinī |
tadvraja śāsane bauddhe vrataṃ cara samāhitā || 33.213{13} ||
iti pitrābhyanujñātaṃ śrutvā sā muditāśayā |
kṣemā tasya pituḥ pādau natvaiva niryayau tataḥ || 33.214{14} ||
tataḥ sā muditā kṣemā sahasā tatra jināśrame |
gatā taṃ śrīghanaṃ dṛṣṭvā muditā samupācarat || 33.215{15} ||
tatra sā sāñjalirnatvā sāstāraṃ taṃ munīśvaram |
purataḥ samupāsīnā prārthayadevamādarāt || 33.216{16} ||
bhagavannātha sarvajña bhavatāṃ śaraṇaṃ vraje |
tatpravrajyāvrataṃ datvā sadā me'nugrahaṃ kuru || 33.217{17} ||
ityarthitaṃ tayā śrutvā bhagavān sa munīśvaraḥ |
tacchiro dakṣahastena pṛṣṭvā tāmevamabravīt || 33.218{18} ||
ehi vatse pragacha tvaṃ gautamyāḥ śaraṇaṃ vraja |
sā mātā bhikṣuṇīśāstā pravrajyāṃ te pradāsyati || 33.219{19} ||
ityādiṣṭaṃ munīndreṇa śrutvā sā muditāśayā |
kṣemā tasyā jaganmāturgautamyāḥ samupācarat || 33.220{20} ||
tatra sā samupāsṛtya dṛṣṭvā tāṃ gautamīṃ mudā |
kṛtāñjalipuṭo natvā prārthayadevamādarāt || 33.221{21} ||
jaganmātarahaṃ kṣemā bhavatyāḥ śaraṇaṃ vraje |
tan pravrajyāvrataṃ mahyaṃ bhavantī dātumarhati || 33.222{22} ||
ityarthitaṃ tayā śrutvā gautāmī sā prasāditā |
tacchiro dakṣahastena pṛṣṭvaivaṃ tāmabhāṣata || 33.223{23} ||
ehi bhikṣuṇi vatse śāsane saugate śubhe |
pravrajyāsaṃvaraṃ dhṛtvā brahmacaryaṃ samācara || 33.224{24} ||
ehīti samādiṣṭe gautamyāsya śubhāśayā |
kṣemābhūnmuṇḍitā pātradharā sucīvarāvṛtā || 33.225{25} ||
tataḥ sā bhikṣuṇī kṣemā saumyarūpā jitendriyā |
bhitvā'vidyāgaṇān duṣṭān prāptavidyāgaṇāñchubhān || 33.226{26} ||
samādhidhāraṇīvidyā ghaṭamānā samudyatā |
mahābhijñāpadaprāptā saṃbodhipadasādhinī || 33.227{27} ||
tataḥ sā sumatīrmatvā bhavacakraṃ calācalam |
matvā ca sarvasaṃskāragatiścāpi vighātinīḥ || 33.228{28} ||
sarvakleśagaṇān hitvā jitvā māragaṇānapi |
sākṣādarhatpadaṃ prāpya babhūva brahmacāriṇī || 33.229{29} ||
tataḥ sākāśasādṛśyā nirvikalpā niraṃjanā |
vāsicaṃdanakalpāṃśā pariśuddhatrimaṃḍalā || 33.230{30} ||
saṃsāralābhasatkāraniḥspṛhā sarvadehinām |
sa devāsuralokānāṃ vaṃdyā pūjyābhavatsatī || 33.231{31} ||
tataḥ sa bhagavāñchāstā sarvajño'rhanmunīśvaraḥ |
sarvānstāṃ śrāvakāṃ bhikṣūn samāmaṃtryaivamādiśat || 33.232{32} ||
eṣāgrā bhikṣavaḥ sarvabhikṣūṇīnāṃ mamāśrame |
abhijñāpratibhāṇasatprajñānāṃ yadiyaṃ sudhīḥ || 33.233{33} ||
kṣemākhyā bhikṣuṇī satyavādinī brahmacāriṇī |
satyadvayaparijñātā saṃbodhipadagāminī || 33.234{34} ||
ityādiṣṭe munīndreṇa sarve te sāṃghikā api |
vismitāstaṃ muniṃ natvā paprachustatpurākṛtam || 33.235{35} ||
bhagavannanayā karma kiṃ purā sukṛtaṃ kṛtam |
yeneyaṃ bhadrikā kṣemā prajñāvatī maharddhikā || 33.236{36} ||
mahāprajñāvatīnāṃ ca sarvāsāmapi saddhiyām |
maharddhipratibhāṇānāmagrā nirdiśyate tvayā || 33.237{37} ||
tadime sāṃghikāḥ sarve śrotumichaṃti sāṃpratam |
tatsarvaṃ samupādiśya sarvāllokān prabodhaya || 33.238{38} ||
iti taiḥ prārthitaṃ śrutvā bhagavān sa munīśvaraḥ |
sarvānstān sāṃghikān bhikṣūn samālokyaivamādiśat || 33.239{39} ||
śṛṇuta bhikṣavaḥ sarve purā yadanayā kṛtam |
tadahaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi sarvalokābhibodhane || 33.240{40} ||
purābhūtkāśyapo nāma tathāgato munīśvaraḥ |
sarvajño'rhañjagacchāstā sugato dharmādhipo jinaḥ || 33.241{41} ||
vārāṇasyāmupāraṇye mṛgadāve jināśrame |
saddharmaṃ samupādiśya vijahāra sasāṃghikaḥ || 33.242{42} ||
tacchāsane tadā śreṣṭhiduhitā śaraṇaṃ gatā |
pravrajyāvratamādāya pracacāra samāhitā || 33.243{43} ||
sā tatra śāsane bauddhe dadau dānaṃ samādarāt |
dvādaśābda sahasrāṇi brahmacaryaṃ cacāra ca || 33.244{44} ||
na kesmiṃ viguṇe'bhyāsaṃ cakāra sā nirudyamā |
kevalaṃ saṃvaraṃ dhṛtvā triratnaṃ sarvadābhajat || 33.245{45} ||
tasyāyābhūdupādhyāyā bhikṣuṇī brahmacāriṇī |
sā kāśyapena nirdiṣṭā prajñāvatīgaṇāgragā || 33.246{46} ||
tatsamākarṇya sā sādhvī bhikṣuṇī cānumoditā |
manasaivaṃ samādhāya praṇidhānaṃ vyadhānmudā || 33.247{47} ||
yathāyamuttaro vipro vyākṛtastena tāyinā |
kalau śākyamunirnāma tathāgato bhavediṭi || 33.248{48} ||
tadā tasya munīndrasya śāsane śaraṇaṃ gatā |
pravrajyāsaṃvaraṃ dhṛtvā careyaṃ brahmacārikām || 33.249{49} ||
ahamapi tayā tatra prajñāvatīgaṇādhipā |
bhaveya hi yathāpīyamupādhyāyā mamādhunā || 33.250{50} ||
sadaitatpraṇidhānena caraṃti sā śubhāśayā |
triratnasmaraṇaṃ kṛtvā samaye maraṇaṃ yayau || 33.251{51} ||
yāsau śreṣṭhisutā hyeṣā kṣemā rājasutādhunā |
yānmeti bhikṣavo yūyaṃ manyadhvaṃ vratameva hi || 33.252{52} ||
yattadā prakṛtaṃ dānaṃ kāśyapaśāsane'nayā |
etaddharmavipākena bhavatīyaṃ nṛpātmajā || 33.253{53} ||
yaccāpi hyanayā tatra brahmacaryaṃ supālitam |
etatpuṇyairihārhantī bhavatīyaṃ kilā'dhunā || 33.254{54} ||
yadyāpi hyanayā tatra praṇidhānaṃ kṛtaṃ yathā |
tatheyaṃ bhavati hyatra prajñāvatīgaṇādhipā || 33.255{55} ||
evaṃ hi yatkṛtaṃ yena tenaiva bhujyate phalam |
abhuktaṃ kṣīyate naiva karma kvāpi kadā cana || 33.256{56} ||
nāgnibhirdahyate karma klidyate nodakairapi |
vāyubhiḥ śuṣyate naiva kṣīyate nāpi bhūmiṣu || 33.257{57} ||
na praṇaśyaṃti karmāṇi janmakalpaśatairapi |
sāmāgrīṃ prāpya kāle hi phalanti prāṇināṃ khalu || 33.258{58} ||
anyathāpi bhavennaiva kṛtakarmaphalaṃ kvacit |
kṛṣṇakarmavipāke hi duḥkhataiva sadā bhave || 33.259{59} ||
śubhakarmavipāke tu sukhataiva sadā bhavet |
miśritakarmapākena miśritatā sadaiva hi || 33.260{60} ||
yathaiva yatkṛtaṃ karma tathaiva tatphalaṃ dhruvam |
evaṃ matvātra saṃsāre sarvadā śubhavāṃchibhiḥ || 33.261{61} ||
triratnabhajanaṃ kṛtvā caritavyaṃ sadā śubhe || 33.262{62} ||
ityādiṣṭaṃ munīndreṇa śrutvā te sarvasāṃghikāḥ |
tatheti prativijñapya prābhyanandan prabodhitāḥ || 33.263{63} ||
etanme guruṇādiṣṭaṃ śrutaṃ mayā tathocyate |
tvaṃ cāpyevaṃ mahārāja cara nityaṃ śubhe sadā || 33.264{64} ||
prajāścāpi tathā rājan bodhayitvā prayatnataḥ |
bodhimārge pratiṣṭhāpya pālayasva samāhitaḥ || 33.265{65} ||
tena te sarvadā nityaṃ sarvatrāpi śubhaṃ bhavet |
kramādbodhiṃ ca saṃprāpya saṃbuddhapadamāpnuyāḥ || 33.266{66} ||
iti tenārhatādiṣṭaṃ śrutvā'śokaḥ sa bhūpatiḥ |
tatheti prativijñapya prābhyanandatsapārṣadaḥ || 33.267{67} ||
kṣemāvadānaṃ manujā idaṃ ye śṛṇvaṃti cāpi niśāmayanti |
te sarva evaṃ satataṃ sukhāni bhuktvā prayāntyeva jinālayan te || 33.268{68} ||
iti ratnāvadānatatve kṣemāvadānaṃ samāptam
ārāmikāvadāna
athāśoko mahīpālaḥ kṛtāñjaliḥ pramoditaḥ |
upaguptaṃ yatiṃ natvā punarevamabhāṣata || 34.1{1} ||
bhadanta śrotumichāmi punaranyatsubhāṣitam |
tadyathā guruṇādiṣṭaṃ tathādeṣṭuñca me'rhati || 34.2{2} ||
iti saṃprārthitaṃ rājñā śrutvā so'rhanmahāmatiḥ |
tamaśokaṃ mahārājaṃ samālokyaivamādiśat || 34.3{3} ||
śṛṇu sādhu mahārāja yathā me guruṇoditam |
tathātrāhaṃ pravakṣyāmi tava dharmapravṛddhaye || 34.4{4} ||
tadyathā bhagavān buddhaḥ śākyasiṃho munīśvaraḥ |
sarvajñaḥ sugato śāstā dharmarājastathāgataḥ || 34.5{5} ||
śrāvakairbhikṣubhiḥ sārddhaṃ bhikṣuṇībhirupāsakaiḥ |
upāsikāgaṇaiścāpi cailakaiścāpi tīrthikam || 34.6{6} ||
bodhisatvairmahāsatvaiḥ sarvasatvahitaṃkaram |
ṛṣibhiryogibhiścāpi yatibhirbrahmacāribhiḥ || 34.7{7} ||
śrāvastyā bahirudyāne jetāraṇye jināśrame |
vihāre vyaharallokahitārthaṃ dharmamādiśan || 34.8{8} ||
tatsaddharmāmṛtaṃ pātuṃ sarve satvāḥ pramoditāḥ |
śakrabrahmādayo devāḥ sarvalokādhipā api || 34.9{9} ||
nagendrā asurendrāśca yakṣagaṃdharvakinnarāḥ |
siddhā vidyādharāḥ sādhyā garuḍā rākṣasā api || 34.10{10} ||
brāhmaṇāḥ kṣatriyā bhūpā rājaputrāśca maṃtriṇaḥ |
amātyāḥ śreṣṭhinaḥ paura gṛhādhipā mahājanāḥ || 34.11{11} ||
vaṇijaḥ sārthavāhāśca śilpino'pi śubhārthinaḥ |
grāmyā jānapadāścānyalokāḥ kārpaṭikādayaḥ || 34.12{12} ||
sarve te samupāśritya vihāre taṃ munīśvaram |
dṛṣṭvā sāñjalayo natvā muditāḥ samunpāviśan || 34.13{13} ||
tatra sarve'pi te nāthaṃ tamabhyarcya yathākramam |
tridhā pradakṣiṇīkṛtya praṇatvā samupāśrayan || 34.14{14} ||
tadā tān samupāsīnāṃ dṛṣṭvā sa bhagavāñjinaḥ |
āryasatyaṃ samārabhya saddharmaṃ samupādiśat || 34.15{15} ||
tatsaddharmāmṛtaṃ pītva sarve brahmādayo'pi te |
lokāḥ satyamiti matvā prābhyanandan prabodhitāḥ || 34.16{16} ||
tasminnavasare kaścit'sau'tha āramikaḥ sudhīḥ |
dantakāṣṭhaṃ samādāya śrāvastyāṃ samupāviśat || 34.17{17} ||
tatra naimittiko dvāre'vasthitastamāgatam |
dṛṣṭvā taddantakāṣṭhaṃ ca samālokyaivamupāgamat || 34.18{18} ||
vataitaddantakāṣṭhaṃ yo bhakṣayiṣyati mānavaḥ |
nūnaṃ ṇataraṇaṃ bhogyaṃ prabhokṣyati sa bhāgyavān || 34.19{19} ||
etattenoditaṃ śrutvā sa āramika unmukhaḥ |
tatraiva kṣaṇamāśritya manasaivaṃ vyacintayat || 34.20{20} ||
kasmāyetadahaṃ dadyāṃ saṃmāno yena me bhavet |
evaṃ dhyātvā punastatra manasaivaṃ vyacintayat || 34.21{21} ||
yadayaṃ bhagavān buddhaḥ sarvatraidhātukādhipaḥ |
jagacchāstā jagannātho dharmarājo munīśvaraḥ || 34.22{22} ||
yadasmai dīyate kiñcidapi tatphalamuttamam |
aprameyamasaṃkhyeyaṃ mahatkhyātaṃ śrutaṃ mayā || 34.23{23} ||
tadasmai dharmarājāya buddhāya sarvatāyine |
jagacchāstre munīndrāya dadyāṃ kāṣṭhamidaṃ nvaham || 34.24{24} ||
iti dhyātvā viniścitya sa āramika ādarāt |
dantakāṣṭhaṃ tadādāya jetodyāne upācarat || 34.25{25} ||
tatra taṃ śrīghanaṃ dṛṣṭvā bhikṣusaṃghapuraskṛtam |
vihāre sa prasannāsyāḥ praviṣṭaḥ samupācarat || 34.26{26} ||
tatra tasya munīndrasya dantakāṣṭhaṃ tadādarāt |
upasthāpya praṇatvā sa sāṃjalirevamabravīt || 34.27{27} ||
bhagavannātha sarvajña mamānugrahakāraṇāt |
dantakāṣṭhamimaṃ śāstā bhavānādātumarhati || 34.28{28} ||
evaṃ saṃprārthite tena bhagavān sa munīśvaraḥ |
dṛṣṭvā tatkāṣṭhamādāya tasyāgrato vyasṛjata || 34.29{29} ||
tadā tatkāṣṭhamādāya sa udyānapālako mudā |
tatraiva saugatārāme nikhanya nidadhe bhuvi || 34.30{30} ||
tatra nihitamātraṃ tatkāṣṭhaṃ mūlapratiṣṭhitam |
mahacchākhāharitpatrapuṣpaphalasamṛddhitaḥ || 34.31{31} ||
tatkṣaṇena mahān vṛkṣo nyagrodhaḥ parimaṇḍalaḥ |
sarvasatvamanohārī mahāmegha ivābhavat || 34.32{32} ||
tatra sa bhagavān gatvā tacchāyāṃ samupāśrayan |
sabhāmadhyāsanāsīnaḥ saddhārmaṃ samupādiśat || 34.33{33} ||
etadadbhutamālokya sarve lokāḥ savismayāḥ |
tatsaddharmāmṛtaṃ pītvā prābhyanandan pramoditāḥ || 34.34{34} ||
tadā tatra mahātmā sa gṛhīśo'nāthapiṇḍadaḥ |
samāgatya munīndraṃ taṃ natvaivaṃ prārthayanmudā || 34.35{35} ||
bhagavannahamadyeha bhagavantaṃ sasāṃghikam |
pūjayituṃ samichāmi tanmamānugrahaṃ kuru || 34.36{36} ||
iti tatprārthitaṃ śrutvā bhagavān sa munīśvaraḥ |
tatheti taṃ samālokya tūṣṇīṃ bhūtvādhyuvāsa tat || 34.37{37} ||
tadadhivāsitaṃ śāstra matvā so'nāthapiṇḍadaḥ |
tatrāsanāni prajñapya tatsāmagrīṃ samānayat || 34.38{38} ||
tadā sa bhagavānstatra sasāṃghikaḥ samutthitaḥ |
taddattaṃ pādyamādāya svasvāsane samāśrayat || 34.39{39} ||
tatrāsanasamāsīnaṃ taṃ munīndraṃ sasāṃghikam |
dṛṣṭvā sa muditaḥ śreṣṭhī yathākramaṃ samarcayet || 34.40{40} ||
tataḥ śatarasai bhojyaiḥ supraṇītaiḥ sa dīnabhṛt |
saṃbuddhapramukhaṃ sarvasaṃghaṃ taṃ samatarpayat || 34.41{41} ||
tataḥ sa śrīghanaṃ sarvasaṃghaṃ ca parituṣṭitam |
matvā panīyapātrāṇi taddhastādīṃ vyaśodhayat || 34.42{42} ||
tataḥ kramukatāmbūlagaṇauṣadhīrasāyanam |
datvā sa sāṃjalirnatvā sasaṃghaṃ tamupāśrayat || 34.43{43} ||
tataḥ sa bhagavān dṛṣṭvā taṃ gṛhasthamupasthitam |
ādimadhyāmṭakalyāṇaṃ sāśiṣaṃ dharmamādiśat || 34.44{44} ||
taddṛṣṭva samahotsāhamāramikaḥ sa mohitaḥ |
naimittikasamākhyātaṃ satyaṃ matvābhyanaṃdata || 34.45{45} ||
tataḥ sa suprasannātmā ārāmikaḥ pramoditaḥ |
samutthāya munīndrasya purataḥ samupācarat || 34.46{46} ||
tatra tasya munīndrasya pādayoḥ sa kṛtāñjaliḥ |
praṇatvā manasā bodhipraṇidhānaṃ vyadhānmudā || 34.47{47} ||
yadasmai dharmarājāya jagacchāstrai jagadbhute |
mayātra śraddhayā dattakāṣṭhamātraṃ samarppitam || 34.48{48} ||
etatpuṇyavipākena caritvāhaṃ śubhe sadā |
pratyekāṃ bodhimāsādya nirvṛtipadamāpnuyām || 34.49{49} ||
iti tena sucittena praṇidhānaṃ kṛtaṃ sadā |
matvā sa bhagavān smitaṃ visasarja saraśmikam || 34.50{50} ||
tataste raśmayaḥ sarvā munimukhābjaniḥsṛtāḥ |
nānā varṇāḥ samanteṣu bhuvaneṣu sa bhāsayan || 34.51{51} ||
ye cādho bhuvane yātāḥ sarvatra narakeṣu te |
avabhāsya sukhīkṛtya nārakīyān pracerire || 34.52{52} ||
tadraśmisaṃparispṛṣṭāḥ sarve te narakasthitāḥ |
nirduḥkhāḥ sukhasaṃpannā vismitā evamūcire || 34.53{53} ||
aho citraṃ kathaṃ duḥkhaṃ prasrabdhaṃ jāyate sukham |
itaścyutāḥ kuhānyatra saṃprāptāḥ sāṃprataṃ vayam || 34.54{54} ||
iti saṃdigdhacittānāṃ teṣāṃ narakavāsinām |
bodhārthaṃ nirmitaṃ buddhaṃ bhagavān vyasṛjattadā || 34.55{55} ||
tannirmitaṃ muniṃ dṛṣṭvā sarve te narakasthititāḥ |
mahaccitrasamākrāntahṛdayāścaivamūśire || 34.56{56} ||
naivānyatra gatā sarve ihaiva saṃsthitā vayam |
kintvayaṃ puruṣo'pūrvadarśanaḥ samupāgataḥ || 34.57{57} ||
nūnamayaṃ mahāsattvaḥ samīkṣyāsmān suduḥkhinaḥ |
kṛpayā coditaḥ sarvān samuddharttumihāgataḥ || 34.58{58} ||
tadasya puruṣasyātra sarvairasmābhirādarāt |
śraddhayā śaraṇaṃ gatvā karttavyaṃ bhajanaṃ mudā || 34.59{59} ||
iti saṃbhāṣya te sarve nārakīyā prasāditāḥ |
tasya nirmitabuddhasya prābhajañcharaṇaṃ gatāḥ || 34.60{60} ||
tadaitadbhajanotpannaiḥ puṇyaiste narakotthitāḥ |
nirmuktapātakāḥ sarve śuddhakāyā divaṃ yayuḥ || 34.61{61} ||
evaṃ te raśmayaḥ sarve sarvānstānnirayāśṛtān |
avabhāsya samuddhṛtya pratyāyayurmuneḥ puraḥ || 34.62{62} ||
evamurddhagatā ye ca raśmayaste prasāritāḥ |
avabhāsya diśaḥ sarvāḥ saṃprasusruḥ surālayān || 34.63{63} ||
sarvān devālayāṃścaivamavabhāsya samantataḥ |
gatvākaniṣṭhaparyantaṃ sarvān devānacodayan || 34.64{64} ||
anityaṃ khalu saṃsāraṃ duḥkhaśūnyamanātmakam |
puṇyameva jagatsāraṃ matvā taccinutādarāt || 34.65{65} ||
niṣkrāmatārabhadhvaṃ tadyujyadhvaṃ buddhaśāsane |
mārasainyān vinirjitya caradhvaṃ saṃvare sadā || 34.66{66} ||
yo'pramatto munīndrasya śāsane saṃcariṣyate |
sa hitvā janma saṃsāre duḥkhasyāntaṃ kariṣyati || 34.67{67} ||
ityudghoṣaiḥ surān sarvāñcodayitvā samantataḥ |
sarve te raśmayastatra puraḥ pratyāyayurmuneḥ || 34.68{68} ||
tatra te raśmayaḥ sarve'pyekībhūtāśca piṇḍitāḥ |
muniṃ pradakṣiṇīkṛtya tadūrṇāyāṃ samāviśan || 34.69{69} ||
taddṛṣṭvā sabhāsīnāḥ sarve lokāḥ savismayāḥ |
kiṃ śāstehādiśeddharmamityevaṃ samacintayat || 34.70{70} ||
athānaṃdaḥ samutthāya kṛtāṃjaliḥ savismayaḥ |
bhagavantaṃ tamānamya prārthayadevamādarāt || 34.71{71} ||
nāhetupratyayaṃ śāstarhasanti sugatāḥ kvacit |
tatkimarthañjagannātha bhavān hasati sāṃpratam || 34.72{72} ||
yadbhavataḥ smitaṃ dṛṣṭvā sarve ime sabhājanāḥ |
vismayasamupākrāntacittāstiṣṭhanti sarvavit || 34.73{73} ||
tadyadarthe bhavān smitaṃ muṃcati tajjagadguro |
samupādiśya sarveṣāṃ saṃśayaṃ chetumarhati || 34.74{74} ||
ityānandoditaṃ śrutvā bhagavānmunīśvaraḥ |
tamānandaṃ sabhāṃ cāpi samālokyaivamādiśat || 34.75{75} ||
evametattathānaṃda yathākhyātaṃ tvayā kila |
nāhetupratyayaṃ buddhā muñcaṃti hi smitaṃ kva cit || 34.76{76} ||
yadarthe'hamihānanda smitaṃ muñcāmi sāṃpratam |
tatsatyaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi śṛṇuta yūyamādarāt || 34.77{77} ||
yadayaṃ śraddhayānanda sudhīrudyānapālakaḥ |
dantakāṣṭhamupasthāpya satkāraṃ kurute mama || 34.78{78} ||
etatpuṇyavipākena kalpānyapi trayodaśa |
vinipātamayaṃ kvāpi gamiṣyati kadā cana || 34.79{79} ||
sarvadāyaṃ mahāsatvaḥ saddharmasādhanodyataḥ |
sadguṇī sukhasaṃbhoktā bodhisatvo bhaviṣyati || 34.80{80} ||
tato'nte'yaṃ mahābhijñaḥ pariśuddhatrimaṇḍalaḥ |
pratyekaṃ bodhimāsādya pratyekasugato jinaḥ || 34.81{81} ||
vimalo nāma satvānāṃ hitakarī śubhaṃkaraḥ |
brahmacārī viśuddhātmā lokanātho bhaviṣyati || 34.82{82} ||
evamānaṃda vijñāya saṃbuddhaśāsane kvacit |
kiñcidvāpi pradātavyaṃ tatphalaṃ syānmahattaram || 34.83{83} ||
ityādiṣṭaṃ munīndreṇa śrutvā te sāṃghikādayaḥ |
sarve lokāḥ sabhāsīnāḥ prābhyanandan prabodhitāḥ || 34.84{84} ||
so'pi cārāmikaḥ śrutvā vyākṛtaṃ sugatena tat |
triratnabhajanaṃ kartuṃ samaichanmuditaḥ sadā || 34.85{85} ||
tacchrutvā sa mahādātā gṛhastho'nāthapiṇḍadaḥ |
sāñjalistaṃ jinaṃ natvā muditaḥ svagṛhaṃ yayau || 34.86{86} ||
tataḥ sa bhagavāṃcchāstā samutthāya sasāṃghikaḥ |
svāsanasamupāsīnastasthau dhyānasamāhitaḥ || 34.87{87} ||
tataḥ āramikaḥ so'pi sarvādā saṃpramoditaḥ |
triratnabhajanaṃ kurvan prācaratsamupasthitaḥ || 34.88{88} ||
iti me guruṇādiṣṭaṃ śrutaṃ mayā tathocyate |
tvayāpyevaṃ mahārāja bhaja ratnatrayaṃ sadā || 34.89{89} ||
lokāścāpi tathā rājaṃ bodhayitvā prayatnataḥ |
triratnabhajanotsāhe yojanīyāstvayā sadā || 34.90{90} ||
tena te maṅgalaṃ nityaṃ sarvatrāpi bhavetsadā |
kramādbodhiṃ samāsādya saṃbuddhapadamāpnuyāḥ || 34.91{91} ||
iti tenārhatādiṣṭaṃ śrutvāśokaḥ sa bhūpatiḥ |
tatheti prativijñapya prābhyanandatsapārṣadaḥ || 34.92{92} ||
āramikasyedamihāvadānaṃ śṛṇvanti ye ye ca niśāmayanti |
te sarvamevaṃ śubhasatsukhāni bhuktvā prayaāsyanti jinālayan te || 34.93{93} ||
iti ratnāvadānatatve āramikāvadānaṃ samāptam
śobhitāvadāna
athāśoko mahārājaḥ kṛtāṃjalipuṭo mudā |
upaguptaṃ yatiṃ natvā prārthayeccaivamādarāt || 35.1{1} ||
bhadanta śrotumichāmi punaranyatsubhāṣitam |
tadyathā guruṇādiṣṭaṃ tathākhyātuṃ ca me'rhati || 35.2{2} ||
iti saṃprārthitaṃ tena śrutvā so'rhaṃ sudhīryatiḥ |
upagupto nareṃdraṃ taṃ samālokyaivamabravīt || 35.3{3} ||
śṛṇu rājanmahābhāga yathā me gurubhāṣitam |
tathātra hi pravakṣyāmi tava cittābhibodhane || 35.4{4} ||
tadyathā bhagavācchāstā śākyasiṃho munīśvaraḥ |
sarvajño'rhañjagannātho dharmarājastathāgataḥ || 35.5{5} ||
saṃbuddhaḥ sa tathā tatra pure kapilavastuni (
nyagrodharucirārāme vijahāra sasāṃghikaḥ || 35.6{6} ||
tasmiṃśca samaye tatra pure kapilavastuni |
śākya āsīnmahāsādhuḥ śrīmān yakṣādhipopamaḥ || 35.7{7} ||
sarvalokapradhānākhyaḥ sarvadravyasamṛddhimān |
tasya bhāryā surūpākhyā suṃdarī ratisaṃnibhā || 35.8{8} ||
subhāvinī ramā kāntā svakuladharmacāriṇī |
tayā saha samāraktaḥ sa śrīmān paricārayan || 35.9{9} ||
yathākāmaṃ sukhaṃ bhuktvā rarāma saṃpramoditaḥ |
tataḥ svāpannasatvābhūtsā surūpā pragarbhitā |
kramādvṛddhodarā pāṇḍuvarṇā svalpāśinī kṛśā || 35.10{10} ||
tataḥ sā samaye sūta dārakaṃ divyasuṃdaram |
darśanīyaṃ subhadrāṅgaṃ prāsādikaṃ manoharam || 35.11{11} ||
tasya janmanyanekāni mahāścaryakarāṇyapi |
prādurbhūtāni taiḥ sarvaṃ nagaraṃ pariśobhitam || 35.12{12} ||
tamabhiśobhitaṃ jātaṃ śrutvā sa janako mudā |
sahasopetya saṃpaśyanstasthau citrānvitāśayaḥ || 35.13{13} ||
tataḥ sa mudito jñātīnāhūya saha vāṃdhavaiḥ |
kṛtva jātimahaṃ tasya nāmā hi khyātumabravīt || 35.14{14} ||
bhavanto jñātayaḥ sarve dṛṣṭvāsya lakṣaṇaṃ yathā |
tathā nāmaprasiddhena vyavasthāpitumarhatha || 35.15{15} ||
iti tenoditaiḥ śrutvā sarve te jñātayastathā |
tasya nimittamālokya taṃ śākyamevamabruvan || 35.16{16} ||
sādho yajjāyate ātmābhaiḥ śobhayati puraṃ tataḥ |
śobhita iti nāmāsya bhavatu prathitaṃ bhuvi || 35.17{17} ||
iti tai jñātibhiḥ sarve samākhyātaṃ niśamya saḥ |
tathā tenābhidhānena prākhyāpayattamātmajam || 35.18{18} ||
tataḥ śobhito'ṣṭābhirdhātrībhiḥ prātipālitaḥ |
paripuṣṭo'bhivṛddho'bhū hradāruhāmbujaṃ yathā || 35.19{19} ||
yadā so dārakaḥ prauḍhaḥ kumāratvamavāptavān |
tadā pitrā sa vidyārthī guruhaste samarppitaḥ || 35.20{20} ||
tataḥ sa guruṇā śāstrā kramāllipiraśiṣyate |
tathābhiśiṣyamāṇaḥ sa sarvavidyāntamāyayau || 35.21{21} ||
tataḥ sa śobhito dhīmān sarvavidyāṃ vicaksaṇaḥ |
tīrthikavādasaṃrakto virakto'bhūdgṛhāśrame || 35.22{22} ||
sadā sa tīrthikārāme gatvā sa tīrthikaiḥ saha |
śāstrasaṃcodanotsāhai reme jayan pravādinaḥ || 35.23{23} ||
tasmiṃśca samaye tatra nyagrodharucirāśramam |
sa bhagavān sabhāsīno dharmamādeṣṭumārabhat || 35.24{24} ||
tadā te bhikṣavaḥ sarve bhikṣuṇyo'pi samāgatāḥ |
vratinaścailakāścānye upāsakā upāsikāḥ || 35.25{25} ||
bodhisatvā mahāsatvāstathānye śrāvakā api |
tatsaddharmāmṛtaṃ pātuṃ sarve te samupācaran || 35.26{26} ||
tatra taṃ śrīghanaṃ natvā parivṛtya samaṃtataḥ |
puraskṛtya samālokya sarve te upatasthire || 35.27{27} ||
tadānye'pi samāyātā brahmaśakrādayaḥ surāḥ |
catvāro lokapālāśca sarve lokādhipā api || 35.28{28} ||
siddhā vidyādharāścāpi yakṣagaṃdharvakinnarāḥ |
rakṣasā garuḍā nāgāstathānye'pi maharddhikāḥ || 35.29{29} ||
yatayo yoginaścāpi nirgranthāstīrthikā api |
ṛṣayo brāhmaṇāścāpi tāpasā brahmacāriṇaḥ || 35.30{30} ||
rājānaḥ kṣatriyā vaiśyā amātyā maṃtriṇo janāḥ |
śreṣṭhino dhaninaḥ paurāḥ sārthavāhā mahājanāḥ || 35.31{31} ||
vaṇijaḥ śilpinaścāpi tathānyadeśavāśinaḥ |
grāmyā jānapadāścāpi tathā kārpaṭikā api || 35.32{32} ||
sarve te samupāgatya dṛṣṭvā taṃ śrīghanaṃ mudā |
yathākramaṃ samabhyarcya natvā kṛtvā pradakṣiṇām || 35.33{33} ||
sāṃjalayaḥ punarnatvā parivṛtyā samaṃtataḥ |
tatsaddharmāmṛtaṃ pātumupatasthuḥ samāhitāḥ || 35.34{34} ||
tataḥ sa śobhitaścāpi tacchrutvā kauṭukānvitaḥ |
tatra tāṃ parṣadaṃ draṣṭuṃ sahasā samupācarat || 35.35{35} ||
tatra sa sahasopetya prādrakṣīttaṃ munīśvaram |
sarvalokasabhāmadhyasamāsīnaṃ prabhāsvaram || 35.36{36} ||
dvātriṃśallakṣaṇāśītivyaṃjanaparibhūṣitam |
vyāmaprabhāsamudbhāsaṃ śatasūryādhikaprabham |
saumyaṃ śāntendriyaṃ kāntaṃ samaṃtabhadrarūpikam |
dṛṣṭvā sa suprasannātmā natvā kṛtvā pradakṣiṇām || 35.37{37} ||
taddharmadeśanāṃ śrotuṃ tatraikānte upāśrayat |
tadā sa bhagavān dṛṣṭvā tāṃ sarvāṃ samupasthitān || 35.38{38} ||
āryasatyaṃ samārabhya saddharmaṃ samupādiśat |
tadāryadharmamākarṇya sarve te sa surādayaḥ || 35.39{39} ||
lokā dharmaviśeṣatvamājñāya pratibodhitāḥ |
bodhicittaṃ samādhāya triratnabhajanodyatāḥ || 35.40{40} ||
sarvasatvahitārthena babhūvurbodhicāriṇaḥ |
tadā sa śobhitaścāpi śrutvāryasatyamuttamam || 35.41{41} ||
prabodhitaḥ prasannātmā saṃsāravirato bhavān |
satkāyadṛṣṭibhūmīndhraṃ viṃsatiśikharodgamam || 35.42{42} ||
hitvā jñānāsinā śrotaāpattiphalamāptavān || 35.43{43} ||
dṛṣṭasatyo viśuddhātmā saddharmaguṇalālasaḥ |
saṃbuddhapadasaṃprāptyai pravrajituṃ samaichata || 35.44{44} ||
tataḥ sa samupāśritya kṛtāṃjalipurogataḥ |
taṃ munīndraṃ jagannāthaṃ svaivaṃ prārthayanmudā || 35.45{45} ||
bhagavannātha sarvajña bhavatāṃ śaraṇaṃ gataḥ |
pravrajituṃ samichāmi tatkṛpayā prasīdatu || 35.46{46} ||
iti tenārthitaṃ śrutvā bhagavān sa munīśvaraḥ |
śobhitaṃ taṃ samālokya samāmaṃtryaivamādiśat || 35.47{47} ||
yadi tvaṃ śāsane bauddhe pravrajituṃ samichasi |
piturājñāṃ samāsādya prehi dāsyāmi te vratam || 35.48{48} ||
ityādiṣṭaṃ munīndreṇa śrutvā sa śobhito mudā |
pādau tasya munernatvā sahasā niryayau tataḥ || 35.49{49} ||
tatra sa svagṛhe gatvā pādau natvā puraḥ sthitaḥ |
pitroretatsamākhyāya prārthayadevamādarāt || 35.50{50} ||
tātāhamadya gachāmi nyagrodharucire vane |
tatra taṃ śrīghanaṃ buddhaṃ paśyāmi parṣadāśritam || 35.51{51} ||
dhanyāste śrāvakāḥ sarve bhikṣavo bhadrikāśikāḥ |
śāṃteṃdriyāḥ samācārā arhanto brahmacāriṇaḥ || 35.52{52} ||
teṣāmeva hi saṃsāre sāphalyaṃ janma mānuṣe |
ye bhajanti munīndrasya śāsane śaraṇaṃ gatāḥ || 35.53{53} ||
teṣāṃ caivaṃ susāraṃ syān saṃsāre bhavacāraṇe |
ye saddharmaṃ munīndrasya śrutvā caraṃti saṃvaram || 35.54{54} ||
teṣāṃ cāpi bhave sāraṃ janmadravyaguṇārjanam |
satkāraiḥ sāṃghike sevāṃ kṛtvā caranti ye vratam || 35.55{55} ||
kiṃ teṣāṃ mānuṣe janma nisphalaṃ sarvasādhanam |
ye dhyātvāpi jinaṃ smṛtvā bhajaṃti na kadā cana || 35.56{56} ||
teṣāṃ ca kiṃ bhave janma kevalaṃ pāpasādhanam |
ye na śṛṇvaṃti saddharmaṃ saṃbuddhabhāṣitaṃ kva cit || 35.57{57} ||
teṣāṃ ca nisphalaṃ janma mānuṣye duḥkhasādhanam |
ye na kurvanti satkāraṃ sāṃghikeṣu kadā cana || 35.58{58} ||
evaṃ ye paśuvadgehe bhogyaṃ bhuktvā vasaṃti vai |
teṣāṃ kiṃ janma saṃsāre pāpaduḥkhārthameva hi || 35.59{59} ||
evaṃ te prāṇinaḥ sarve bhramaṃti bhavasāgare |
yato yairdṛśyate kvāpi triratnaṃ na kadā cana || 35.60{60} ||
tathāsmākamapi cātra saṃsāre janma nisphalam |
saṃpado'pi nirarthā hi triratnabhajanaṃ vinā || 35.61{61} ||
dharmārthajanmasaṃsāre yadi dharmo na labhyate |
kimevaṃ jīvitenāpi kevalāśubhasādhinā || 35.62{62} ||
tadvaraṃ mṛtyuradyevaṃ vinā dhārmārthasādhanam |
kiṃ tena jīvitenāpi yanmithyāduḥkhasādhanam || 35.63{63} ||
na prāptaṃ bhagavatpūjā mahotsāhaṃ kadāpi yaiḥ |
dharmo'pi na śrutaḥ kaścinna kārāḥ sāṃghike kṛtāḥ || 35.64{64} ||
bhītebhyo nābhayaṃ dattaṃ daridrāśā na pūritā |
duḥkhāya kevalaṃ māturgatāste garbhaśalyatām || 35.65{65} ||
dhanyāste sukhino loke subhadrāḥ śubhabhāvinaḥ |
satpuruṣā mahātmānaḥ śrīmaṃtaḥ sadguṇāśrayāḥ || 35.66{66} ||
ye buddhe śaraṇaṃ gatvā dhyātvā smṛtvā samāhitāḥ |
saddharmāmṛtamāpīya carantaḥ saugataṃ vratam || 35.67{67} ||
sarve satvahitārthaṃ ca dānaṃ datvā yathepsitam |
dayālavaḥ susaumyāṃśāḥ sarvasatvahitāśayāḥ || 35.68{68} ||
saṃbodhisādhanotsāhasarvasatvahitodyatāḥ |
niḥkleśā vimalātmānaḥ samādhiniścalāśayāḥ || 35.69{69} ||
prajñāvaṃto mahādhīrāḥ saṃbodhiratnalābhinaḥ |
pravrajyāvratinārhanto nirvikalpā niraṃjanāḥ || 35.70{70} ||
bhikṣāhārā nirātaṃkā niḥśaṃkā damiteṃdriyāḥ |
niḥśeṣanirjitāvidyāḥ prāptavidyā viśāradāḥ || 35.71{71} ||
svachaṃdacāriṇaḥ santaḥ sarvasatvātmabhāvinaḥ |
niḥspṛhā viratotsāhā nirdainyaḥ kṣubhitāśayāḥ || 35.72{72} ||
nirmadā nirahaṃkārā āryā nirabhimāninaḥ |
nirmāyā nirmamākhaḍgā niḥsaṃgā niḥparigrahāḥ || 35.73{73} ||
muṇḍitā khikkhirīpātradadhānāścīvarāvṛtāḥ |
yatinaḥ sugatākārāstriyānamokṣadeśakāḥ || 35.74{74} ||
svaparātmahitārthena saṃbodhimārgadeśakāḥ |
vaṃdyāḥ pūjyāḥ pramānyāśca caturbrahmavihāriṇaḥ || 35.75{75} ||
te eva sugatiṃ yāṃti yāṃti cānte jinālaye |
saṃbodhiṃ ca samāsādya bhaveyuḥ sugatā api || 35.76{76} ||
iti satyaṃ munīndreṇa samādiṣṭaṃ niśamya me |
mano'tra bhavasaṃcāre carituṃ nābhivāṃchati || 35.77{77} ||
triratnaśaraṇaṃ kṛtvā saṃbuddhaśāsane śubhe |
pravrajyāsaṃvaraṃ dhṛtvā caritumichati sāṃpratam || 35.78{78} ||
kiṃ ca me janmakāle yadidaṃ yuḥ śobhitāruṣā |
saṃpado'pi pravarddhante divyābhisuṃdarāsmi ca || 35.79{79} ||
tatsarvaṃ me purā bhadrakarmadharmavipākataḥ |
saṃpadyate'dhunā nūnaṃ viddhi mā tvanyathā pita || 35.80{80} ||
etannūnamiti matvā bhūyo'pi me manastathā |
saddharmasādhanaṃ kartumichati saugataṃ vratam || 35.81{81} ||
etaddhetorahaṃ tāta prārthayāmyevamādarāt |
tadanujñāṃ pradattaṃ me yadi vāṃchāsti vā śubhe || 35.82{82} ||
etatpuṇyavibhāgena yūyamapi surālayam |
gatvā divyasukhānyeva bhuktvā ciraṃ nivatsyatha || 35.83{83} ||
tataḥ kāle divaścyutvā martyaloka ihāgatāḥ |
triratnabhajanaṃ kṛtvā cariṣyatha śubhe sadā || 35.84{84} ||
tacchubhapuṇyapākena śuddhāśayāḥ śubhāvinaḥ |
saṃbuddhaśāsane gatvā saddharmaṃ śroṣyathādarāt || 35.85{85} ||
tatastatpuṇyapākena saṃghānāṃ śaraṇaṃ gatāḥ |
satkāraiḥ samupasthitvā bhajiṣyatha samādarāt || 35.86{86} ||
tatastatpuṇyapākena bodhicittamavāpsyatha |
tato lokahitārtheṣu bodhicaryāṃ cariṣyatha || 35.87{87} ||
tataḥ pāramitāḥ sarvāḥ pūrayitvā yathākramam |
tato bodhiṃ samāsādya saṃbuddhapadamāpsyatha || 35.88{88} ||
evaṃ vijñāya saṃbuddhapadasaṃpratipattaye |
tadanujñāṃ pradattaṃ me saṃbuddhapadavāṃchire || 35.89{89} ||
anityaṃ khalu samṣāraṃ jīvitaṃ kṣaṇabhaṃguram |
kṣaṇabhaṃgiśarīraṃ ca sarveṣāṃ bhavacāriṇām || 35.90{90} ||
saṃpado'pi sthirā naiva satkṣaṇaṃ ca sudurlabham |
mānuṣye sarvadā janma labhyate na sudurllabham || 35.91{91} ||
tatrāpi durlabhā dharme matirjātāpi na sthirāḥ |
dharmaṃ tu saugataiḥ śreṣṭhaṃ yatsaṃbodhipadasādhanam || 35.92{92} ||
tatsaṃbodhipadaprāptyai dharmamichāmi saugatam |
tadanujñāṃ pradatvā me mano harṣayata drutam || 35.93{93} ||
dharmaṃ vinātra kiṃ sāraṃ bhogyairarthairguṇairapi |
kiṃ kāmasukhaṃ bhuktvā sthitvā ca paśuvadgṛhe || 35.94{94} ||
tadanujñāṃ na datyaśyen sthāsyāmi na gṛhe'pyaham |
sarvān parigrahānstyaktvā gamiṣyāmi tapovanam || 35.95{95} ||
tatra ko nirjane sthitvā caritvā duṣkaraṃ tapaḥ |
anāhāro mariṣye'haṃ sarvatra maraṇaṃ dhruvam || 35.96{96} ||
kasya mṛtyu bhave nāsti kutra mṛtyorbhayaṃ na hi |
sarvalokān grasenmṛtyureko'pi sa mahāvaliḥ || 35.97{97} ||
tadatra kiṃ viṣādena yaddamopāyamatra na |
avaśyaṃ bhāvino bhāvā bhavanti mahatāmapi || 35.98{98} ||
mṛtyukāle sahāyo'pi ko'pi nāsti sahānugaḥ |
dharma eva tadaiko hi sahāyaḥ syātsahānugaḥ || 35.99{99} ||
evaṃ matvātra saṃsāre sthātuṃ nechanti sajjanāḥ |
sarvān parigrahānstyaktvā pravrajanti śubhārthinaḥ || 35.100{100} ||
tathāhamapi tanmṛtyubhayaṃ dṛṣṭvābhiśaṃkitaḥ |
saṃbuddhaśaraṇaṃ gatvā carituṃ vratamutsahe || 35.101{1} ||
iti dharme nidheddhaṃ mā kiṃ cidvā vaktumarhatha |
suprasannādhiyānujñāṃ pradātumeva me'rhati || 35.102{2} ||
iti tenātmajenaivaṃ saṃprārthitaṃ niśamya tau |
pitarau vismayākrāṃtacittau taṣṭhaturmūrchitau || 35.103{3} ||
tatastau dhairyamālambya tameva svātmajaṃ cirāt |
dṛṣṭvā viyogaduḥkhārttau taṣṭhaturlikhitāviva || 35.104{4} ||
tataḥ sa janakaḥ śākyastaduktaparibodhitaḥ |
galadaśrumukho dṛṣṭvā taṃ putramevamabravīt || 35.105{5} ||
hā putra kimatrāhaṃ vadeya sāṃprataṃ khalu |
yattvameva sudhīrvijñaḥ paṇḍito'si vicakṣaṇaḥ || 35.106{6} ||
yattvaṃ śāsane bauddhe pravrajituṃ samichasi |
tacchāstuḥ śaraṇaṃ kṛtvā vrataṃ cara samāhitaḥ || 35.107{7} ||
iti pitroditaṃ śrutvā sa sobhitaḥ pramoditaḥ |
pitroḥ pādān praṇatvaiva sahasā niryayau gṛhāt || 35.108{8} ||
tataḥ sa sahasā gatvā nyagrodhatarumaṇḍite |
vihāre sugatārame praviśya samupācarat || 35.109{9} ||
yatra sa purato gatvā kṛtāṃjalipuṭo mudā |
praṇatvā taṃ jagannāthaṃ prārthayadevamādarāt || 35.110{10} ||
bhagavannātha sarvajña prāpyānujñāṃ samāvraje |
tadbhavān sāṃprataṃ mahyaṃ pravrajyāṃ dātumarhati || 35.111{11} ||
bhavatāṃ śaraṇe sthitvā pravrajya saugataṃvratam |
dhṛtvā sadā samādāya cariṣye bodhicārikām || 35.112{12} ||
iti saṃprārthitaṃ tena bhagavān sa munīśvaraḥ |
savyena pāṇinā tasya śiraḥ spṛṣṭvaivamādiśat || 35.113{13} ||
ehi ca vatsa samādhāya vrataṃ carasva saugatam |
ityuktvā sa munīndraḥ svasāṃghike taṃ samagrahīt || 35.114{14} ||
ehītyukte munīṃdreṇa śobhito'bhūtsa muṇḍitaḥ |
khikkhirīpātravibhrāṇaḥ kākhāyacīvarī yatiḥ || 35.115{15} ||
tataḥ sa sumatirbhikṣuḥ saṃsāragatinispṛhaḥ |
bhitvāvidyāgaṇān sarvān prāptavidyā viśāradaḥ || 35.116{16} ||
sarvakleśagaṇāṃ jitvā sākṣādarhatvamāptavān || 35.117{17!} ||
tataḥ so'rhanmahābhijñaḥ pariśuddhatrimaṇḍalaḥ |
nikleśaḥ suviśuddhātmā śuddhendriyo jināṃśabhṛt || 35.118{18} ||
nirvikalpaḥ samākāro brahmacārī niraṃjanaḥ |
saṃsāralābhasatkāraniḥspṛhaḥ khasamāśayaḥ || 35.119{19} ||
sa devāsuralokānāṃ sarveṣāṃ bhavacāriṇām |
mānyaḥ pūjyo'bhivaṃdyaśca babhūva sa jinātmajaḥ || 35.120{20} ||
taddṛṣṭvā bhikṣavaḥ sarve vismayoddhatamānasāḥ |
bhavantaṃ praṇatvaivaṃ paprachustatpurākṛtam || 35.121{21} ||
bhagavan kiṃ purānena sukṛtaṃ prakṛtaṃ kuha |
yenāyaṃ sukule jāto divyakalpātisuṃdaraḥ || 35.122{22} ||
janmani vāsya jātāni mahādbhutakarāṇyapi |
yairetannagaraṃ sarvaṃ caṃdrābhairiva śobhitam || 35.123{23} ||
bhavatāṃ śāsane cāpi triratnaśaraṇaṃ gataḥ |
pravrajyāsaṃvaraṃ dhṛtvā yatirarhan bhavatyapi || 35.124{24} ||
etatsarvaṃ samākhyāya bhavāṃcchāstā jagadguruḥ |
sarvānasmān sabhāṃścāpi prabodhayitumarhati || 35.125{25} ||
iti tairbhikṣubhiḥ sarvaiḥ prārthite sa munīśvaraḥ |
sarvān yatīn sabhāṃ cāpi samālokyaivamādiśat || 35.126{26} ||
śṛṇuta bhikṣavaḥ sarve yadanena purākṛtam |
tatsarvaṃ samāśakhye yuṣmākaṃ paribodhane || 35.127{27} ||
tadyathābhūtpurā śāstā krakuchandastathāgataḥ |
sarvajño'rhaṃ jagannātho dharmarājo munīśvaraḥ || 35.128{28} ||
tadā sa bhagavān loke bodhicaryāṃ prakāśayan |
saddharmaṃ samupādiśya pracacāra samaṃtataḥ || 35.129{29} ||
evaṃ sa bhagavāṃ chāstā kṛtvā sarvatra maṃgalam |
śobhāvatyā mahāpūryyā rājadhānyā upāśrame || 35.130{30} ||
vihāre saugatāvāse samāśritya prabhāsayan |
saddharmaṃ samupādeṣṭuṃ vijahāra sasāṃghikaḥ || 35.131{31} ||
tatrāgatya samānīnaṃ taṃ munīndraṃ sasāṃghikam |
śrutvā śobho mahārājo draṣṭuṃ sa samupācarat || 35.132{32} ||
tatra sa nṛpatiḥ śobho dṛṣṭvā taṃ sugataṃ munim |
krakuchaṃdaṃ samāsīnaṃ bhikṣusaṃghapuraskṛtam || 35.133{33} ||
muditaḥ samupāsṛtya kṛtāṃjaliḥ purogataḥ |
praṇatvā suprasannāsyaḥ prārthayamevamādarāt || 35.134{34} ||
bhagavannātha sarvajña bhavāñchāstā jagadguruḥ |
tadasmākaṃ hitārthena dharmamādeṣṭumarhati || 35.135{35} ||
iti saṃprārthite tena rājñā sa bhagavānapi |
taṃ śobhaṃ kṣitipāleṃdraṃ samālokyaivamādiśat || 35.136{36} ||
śṛṇu rājan samādhāya satvānāṃ hitakāraṇam |
saṃbodhisādhanaṃ dharmaṃ vakṣyāmi te śubhārthataḥ || 35.137{37} ||
anityaṃ khalu saṃsāre sarvaṃ śūnyamanātmakam |
māyākleśasamudbhūtaṃ jagallokamaśāśvatam || 35.138{38} ||
tadatra kiṃ bhave sāraṃ nirarthaṃ duḥkhasādhanam |
iti vijñāya rājendra kleśāñjitvā śubhe cara || 35.139{39} ||
śubhena sadgatiṃ yāyā sadgatau sarvadā sukham |
sukhena sarvadā bhadre caritavyaṃ tathā bhavam || 35.140{40} ||
śubhaṃ saṃjāyate puṇyātsarvatrāpi sadā dhruvam |
puṇyaṃ saṃjāyate hyādau saṃbuddhadarśanādapi || 35.141{41} ||
etatpuṇyānubhāvena saddharmaśravaṇaṃ labhet |
tatsaddharmarasāsvādasaṃsaktaśca samutsahet || 35.142{42} ||
tadutsāhātpunaḥ śrotumabhilāṣaḥ samudbhavet |
samudbhūtābhilāṣaśca satkṛtya mānayenmudā || 35.143{43} ||
sadguruṃ samupāsṛtya saddharmaṃ śṛṇuyātsadā |
tatsaddharmāmṛtasvādaguṇalabdhaḥ pramoditaḥ || 35.144{44} ||
saddharmacāraṇo bhikṣūn satkṛtya mānayenmudā |
tatastaddharmamākarṇya saṃbodhidharmavāṃchitaḥ |
triratnabhajanaṃ kṛtvā svaparārthahite caret || 35.145{45} ||
etatpuṇyavipākena sa kule śrīsamālaye |
jāto lokahitārthena dānaṃ dadyādyathepsitam || 35.146{46} ||
taddānapuṇyapākena sa dātā sadgatiṃ vrajet |
sadgatisthaḥ sukhenaivaṃ suśīlaḥ saṃvaraṃ caret || 35.147{47} ||
tatsuśīlavratotpannaiḥ puṇyaiḥ sa pariśuddhadhīḥ |
sarvasatvahitārtheṣu dayālu maitravaccaret || 35.148{48} ||
tatastatpuṇyapākena sa sudhīro vicakṣaṇaḥ |
svaparātmahitārthena sarvakāryāṇi sādhayet || 35.149{49} ||
etatpuṇyaviśuddhātmā niḥkleśo vimalāśayaḥ |
samādhidharaṇīvidyāsamādhānaḥ sudhīrbhavet || 35.150{50} ||
tatastatpuṇyasaṃbhārasaṃbodhiguṇasārthabhṛt |
prajñābdhipāramāsādya bodhicittamaṇiṃ labhet || 35.151{51} ||
tanmaneranubhāvena sarvopāyavidhānavit |
sarvasatvahito dhānaṃ bodhicaryāvrataṃ caret || 35.152{52} ||
tatpuṇyaiḥ sa mahāsatvaḥ sarvasatvaśubhaṃkaraḥ |
saṃbodhipraṇidhānena saddharmābhirato bhavet || 35.153{53} ||
tatastaddharmapūtātmā duṣṭakleśān vinirjayan |
sarvahitārthasaṃbhartā mahābhijñā valī bhave || 35.154{54} ||
tato māragaṇāñjitvā pariśuddhatrimaṇḍalaḥ |
so'rhan saṃbodhisaṃprāptaḥ saṃbuddhapadamāpnuyāt || 35.155{55} ||
tataḥ sa sugataḥ śāstā kṛtvā dharmamayaṃ jagat |
bodhimārge pratiṣṭhāpya nirvṛtaḥ svālayaṃ vrajet || 35.156{56} ||
tataḥ sa svālaye līno dharmadhātvīśvaro jinaḥ |
viśvarūpā viśuddhābhajyotirūpo niraṃjanaḥ || 35.157{57} ||
evaṃ vijñāya rājendra saṃbuddhapadavāṃchibhiḥ |
