triratnabhajanaṃ kṛtvā caritavyaṃ śubhe sadā || 35.158{58} ||
tathā taṃ sarvadā bhadraṃ sarvatrāpi bhaveddhruvam |
kramātsaṃbodhimāsādya saṃbuddhapadamāpsyatha || 35.159{59} ||
iti śāstrā samādiṣṭaṃ śrutvā śobhaḥ sa bhūpatiḥ |
tathā hīti pravijñapya prābhyanandatprabodhitaḥ || 35.160{60} ||
tadārabhya sa bhūmīndrastriratnaśaraṇaṃ gataḥ |
sarvasatvahitaṃ kṛtvā prācaratsarvadā śubhe || 35.161{61} ||
tadā tadviṣaye tatra sarvadābhūcchubhotsavam |
sarva lokāśca saddharmāratotsāhāḥ pracerire || 35.162{62} ||
evaṃ sa nṛpatiḥ śobhastaṃ munīndraṃ sasāṃghikam |
yathārhabhojanaiścāpi trimāsyaṃ samasevita || 35.163{63} ||
tataḥ sa bhūmīrājo trimāsānte sasāṃghikam |
krakuchaṃdaṃ munīndraṃ taṃ natvaivaṃ prārthayanmudā || 35.164{64} ||
bhagavannātha sarvajña sarvadātra sasāṃghikaḥ |
saddharmaṃ kṛpayāsmadbhyamādeṣṭuṃ sthātumarhati || 35.165{65} ||
iti saṃprārthite rājñā bhagavān sa jagadguruḥ |
tada śobhaṃ jagatīpālaṃ samālokyaivamādiśat || 35.166{66} ||
nāhaṃ rājaṃstavaikasya hitārthe sugato bhave |
api tu sarvasatvānāṃ śubhahetau bhave jinaḥ || 35.167{67} ||
tadevaṃ sarvalokeṣu bodhicaryāṃ prakāśayan |
saddharmaṃ samupākhyātuṃ careyaṃ saha sāṃghikaiḥ || 35.168{68} ||
yadyasti te sadā rājaṃ bhaktiśraddhāmatirmayi |
mannāmābhisamuddiśya stūpaṃ kṛtvā sadā bhaja || 35.169{69} ||
etasmiṃ yatkṛtaṃ karma tanmayi kṛtameva hi |
tiṣṭhato nirvṛtasyāpi tulyaṃ bhaktimatāṃ phalam || 35.170{70} ||
tadyathā mayi rājendra śraddhayā śaraṇaṃ gataḥ |
triratnabhajanaṃ kṛtvā carasvaivaṃ śubhe mudā || 35.171{71} ||
tathāsmin sarvadā stūpe śraddhayā śaraṇaṃ gataḥ |
triratnabhajanaṃ kṛtvā śubhe cara samāhitaḥ || 35.172{72} ||
tathā te sarvadā kṣemaṃ sarvatrāpi bhaveddhruvam |
kramātsaṃbodhimāsādya saṃbuddhapadamāpnuyāt || 35.173{73} ||
ityādiśya sa saṃbuddhastasmai śobhāya bhūrbhuje |
svanakhakeśamutkṛtya dadau stūpārthamādarāt || 35.174{74} ||
taṃ munīndrapradattaṃ sa bhūpatiḥ saṃpramoditaḥ |
praṇatvā samupāgṛhya sānaṃdaḥ svālayaṃ yayau || 35.175{75} ||
tatra sa nṛpatī rājā tannakhakeśamādarāt |
garbhe nidhāya sadratnairmahastūpamakārayat || 35.176{76} ||
tatastaṃ sa nṛpo rājā pratiṣṭhāpya yathāvidhiḥ |
mahotsavaiḥ sadā nityaṃ samabhyarcya bhajanmudā || 35.177{77} ||
tatraikasmin dine bhadre parvaṇi pratyupasthite |
sametya goṣṭhikāḥ sarve taṃ stūpaṃ draṣṭumāgatāḥ || 35.178{78} ||
dṛṣṭvā te goṣṭhikāḥ sarve suprasannābhimoditāḥ |
nānāpuṣpaistamabhyarcya mahotsavaiḥ sahābhajat || 35.179{79} ||
tatraiko goṣṭhiko dṛṣṭvā daridro'hamiti bruvan |
tatpūjāmahotsāhe lajjayā svagṛhaṃ yayau || 35.180{80} ||
tatastairgoṣṭhikaiḥ sarvairdṛṣṭvā sa svagṛhe tataḥ |
bahuśaḥ paribhāṣitvā goṣṭhimadhyādbahiṣkṛtaḥ || 35.181{81} ||
tataḥ sa lajjayākhinnaḥ paścāttāpāgnitāpitaḥ |
hā maṃdaḥ kathameko'haṃ tiṣṭheyamityaciṃtayat || 35.182{82} ||
tataḥ sa sugataṃ smṛtvā daridro'pi samaṃtataḥ |
sarvapuṣpāṇi saṃgṛhya taistaṃ stūpaṃ samarcayat || 35.183{83} ||
tataḥ pradakṣiṇīkṛtya sāṃjaliḥ sa pramoditaḥ |
tatpādayoḥ praṇatvaivaṃ praṇidhānaṃ mudākarot || 35.184{84} ||
yanmayātra munīndrasya stūpaḥ puṣpaiḥ samarcitaḥ |
etatpuṇyavipākena bhaveyaṃ śrīsamṛddhimān || 35.185{85} ||
ityevaṃ praṇidhiṃ kṛtvā tatstūpaṃ śaraṇaṃ gataḥ |
sa daridraḥ samabhyarcya prābhajan saṃpramoditaḥ || 35.186{86} ||
manyatāṃ bhikṣavo yo'sau daridro'yaṃ hi śobhitaḥ |
yatstūpo'rcitaḥ puṣpaistenāyaṃ śrīsamṛddhimān || 35.187{87} ||
anyadapi yathānena sukṛtaṃ prakṛtaṃ purā |
tatsarvaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi śṛṇudhvan yūyamādarāt || 35.188{88} ||
tathāthābhūtpurā pūryyāṃ vārāṇasyāṃ gṛhādhipaḥ |
śreṣṭhī mahājanaḥ sādhurvyavahāravicakṣaṇaḥ || 35.189{89} ||
tadaikasmin dine tasya gṛhāntike śanaiścaran |
pratyekasugato glāno bhikṣārthaṃ samupācaran || 35.190{90} ||
taṃ gṛhāntikamāyātaṃ dṛṣṭvā sa karuṇāhataḥ |
sahasopetya natvainamāmaṃtrya svagṛhe nayet || 35.191{91} ||
tatra śreṣṭhī sa taṃ glānaṃ pratyekabuddhamāsane |
upasthāpya samabhyarcya paṭenāchādayanmudā || 35.192{92} ||
tasmai kṣīrodanaiḥ pūrṇaṃ pātraṃ paṃcāmṛtaiḥ saha |
pradatvā sāṃjalirnatvā manasaivaṃ vyaciṃtayat || 35.193{93} ||
yadayaṃ sugato glāno'bhyarcito mayādarāt |
etatpuṇyavipākena bhaveyaṃ kṣemavān sudhīḥ || 35.194{94} ||
ityevaṃ praṇidhānena sa śreṣṭhī pratimoditaḥ |
satkṛtya sādaraṃ tatra svāśrame samacārayat || 35.195{95} ||
manyatāṃ bhikṣavo yo'sau śreṣṭhī śrīmānmahājanaḥ |
ayameva mahābhijñaḥ śobhito'rhanmahāmatiḥ || 35.196{96} ||
yadanena tadā glānapratyekabuddha ādarāt |
paṭenāchādya piṃḍena pratipādya samarcitaḥ || 35.197{97} ||
etatpuṇyavipākena śobhito'yaṃ samṛddhimān |
paṃcajanmaśatānyevaṃ babhūva sadguṇākaraḥ || 35.198{98} ||
bhūyo'pi yatpurānena sukṛtaṃ sādhitaṃ yathā |
tatsarvaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi śṛṇudhvaṃ yūyamādarāt || 35.199{99} ||
tadyathābhūtpurā śāstā kāśyapo'rhanstathāgataḥ |
sarvajñaḥ sugato nātho dharmarājo munīśvaraḥ || 35.200{100} ||
vārāṇasyāṃ sa saṃbuddho mṛgadāve sasāṃghikaḥ |
sarvasatvahitārthena vyaharaddharmamādiśat || 35.201{11} ||
tasmiṃśca samaye tatra vārāṇasyāmabhūdgṛhī |
saṃbuddhabhaktimān sādhurdaridraḥ kāṣṭhahārikaḥ || 35.202{2} ||
sa ekasmin dine kāṣṭhamāhartu parvaṭe vrajan |
tatraikatra mahatstūpaṃ dadarśāchāditaṃ tṛṇaiḥ || 35.203{3} ||
dṛṣṭvā sa samupāśritya tattṛṇāni samaṃtataḥ |
sarvāṇyapi samutpāṭya saṃmṛjya samaśodhayat || 35.204{4} ||
tatastaṃ śobhitaṃ dṛṣṭvā sa śreṣṭhī saṃpramoditaḥ |
natvā pradakṣiṇīkṛtya manasaivaṃ vyaciṃtayat || 35.205{5} ||
yadayaṃ saugatastūpo mayā saṃmṛjya śodhitaḥ |
etatpuṇyavipākena bhaveyaṃ divyasuṃdaraḥ || 35.206{6} ||
divyātiriktābhaiḥ śobhitaḥ syān samṛddhimān |
anāgatāṃśca saṃbuddhānārāgayeyamādarāt || 35.207{7} ||
tadā tacchāsane gatvā triratnaśaraṇaṃ gataḥ |
pravrajyārhatpadaṃ prāpya vrajeyāhaṃ sunirvṛtim || 35.208{8} ||
ityevaṃ praṇidhiṃ kṛtvā praṇatvā taṃ jinālayam |
satataḥ kāṣṭhamādāya svagṛhaṃ samudācarat || 35.209{9} ||
tataḥ sa sumatiḥ śrīmānabhūttatpuṇyavān kṛtiḥ |
sukhāni suciraṃ bhuktvā kāle mṛto divaṃ yayau || 35.210{10} ||
svarge sa divyabhogyāni bhuktvā reme suraiḥ saha |
paṃcajanmaśatānyevaṃ sukhaṃ bhuktvā bhramedbhave || 35.211{11} ||
atrāpi yadeyaṃ śrīmāñchāsane me samāgataḥ |
pravrajyāsaṃvaraṃ dhṛtvā carannarhan bhavatyapi || 35.212{12} ||
tatsarvaṃ hi vijānīta tatstūpasaṃmṛṣṭapuṇyataḥ |
yathā ca ciṃtitaṃ tatra tathāsya siddhyate'dhunā || 35.213{13} ||
daridrakāṣṭhahāro yo bhavetso'yaṃ hi śobhitaḥ |
anya iti na manyantavyaṃ yuṣmābhirnātra saṃśayaḥ || 35.214{14} ||
evaṃ sarvatra saṃsāre sarveṣāmapi prāṇinām |
yathaiva yatkṛtaṃ karma tathaiva tatphalaṃ dhruvam || 35.215{15} ||
abhuktaṃ kṣīyate naiva karma kvāpi kadā cana |
anyathāpi bhavennaiva karmaphalaṃ kathaṃ cana || 35.216{16} ||
evaṃ matvātra saṃsāre sarvadā sukhavāṃchibhiḥ |
triratnabhajanaṃ kṛtvā caritavyaṃ śubhe sadā || 35.217{17} ||
ityādiṣṭaṃ munīndreṇa śrutvā te sarvasāṃghikāḥ |
sarve lokāśca saṃbodhiprāptuṃ dharmārthino'bhavan || 35.218{18} ||
iti me guruṇākhyātaṃ śrutaṃ mayā tathocyate |
tvamapyevaṃ mahārāja saddharmanirato bhava || 35.219{19} ||
prajāścāpi mahāraja bodhayitvā prayatnataḥ |
bodhimārge'bhisaṃsthāpya pātumarhati sarvadā || 35.220{20} ||
evaṃ kṛte mahārāja sarvatra vaḥ śubhaṃ bhavet |
kramātsaṃbodhimāsādya saṃbuddhapadamāpnuyāḥ || 35.221{21} ||
iti tenārhatākhyātaṃ śrutvāśokaḥ sa bhūmipaḥ |
tathā hīti pratijñapya prābhyanandatsa pārṣadaḥ || 35.222{22} ||
ye śraddhāḥ suprasannā idamapi manujāḥ śobhitasyāvadānam |
śṛṇvanti śrāvayaṃti pramuditamanasā ye ca saṃbodhikāmāḥ |
te sarve bodhisatvāḥ sakalaguṇabhṛto bodhicaryāṃ caranto |
bhuktvā saukhyaṃ prakāmaṃ daśabalanilaye saṃprayānti pramuktvā || 35.223{23} ||
iti ratnāvadānatatve śobhitāvadānaṃ samāptam
muktāvadāna
athāśoko mahīpālaḥ sāṃjaliḥ samupāśritaḥ |
upaguptaṃ yatiṃ natvā punarevamabhāṣata || 36.1{1} ||
bhadanta śrotumichāmi punaranyatsubhāṣitam |
tadyathā guruṇākhyātaṃ tathādeṣṭuṃ ca me'rhati || 36.2{2} ||
iti saṃprārthitaṃ rājñā śrutvā so'rhan sudhīryatiḥ |
tamaśokaṃ mahārājaṃ samālokyaivamabravīt || 36.3{3} ||
śṛṇu sādhu mahārāja yathā me gurubhāṣitam |
tathātrāhaṃ pravakṣyāmi tava cittaviśuddhaye || 36.4{4} ||
tadyathābhūnmahāpūryyāṃ śrāvastyāṃ śrīdasaṃnibhaḥ |
śreṣṭhī mahādhanaḥ sādhuḥ puṣyābhidho mahājanaḥ || 36.5{5} ||
tasya bhāryā subhadrāṃgī rāmākhyā śrīsamānikā |
kuladharmasamācarā svāmicaryānucāriṇī || 36.6{6} ||
yayā patnyā subhāvinyā sārddhaṃ puṣyaḥ sa sanmatiḥ |
yathākāmaḥ sukhaṃ bhuktvā reme samupacārayan || 36.7{7} ||
tataḥ sā samaye kāṃtā garbhiṇī varddhitodarā |
svalpāhāraratā pāṇḍuvarṇā kṛśābhavatkramāt || 36.8{8} ||
tataḥ sā samaye'sūta dārikāṃ divyasuṃdarīm |
śironivaddhamuktā srakkohitāmabhirocinīm || 36.9{9} ||
tāṃ saṃjātāṃ śirovaddhamuktāmālāṃ praśobhitām |
dārikāṃ sā prasū dṛṣṭvā sacitrānaṃdito'bhavat || 36.10{10} ||
tacchrutvā sa pitā puṣyo vismayoddhatamānasaḥ |
upetya tāṃ sutāṃ dṛṣṭvā tasthau paśyan savismayaḥ || 36.11{11} ||
tataste jñātayaḥ sarve vaṃdhumitrasuhṛjjanāḥ |
tāṃ saṃjātāṃ niśamyāśu draṣṭumājagmurādarāt || 36.12{12} ||
tatra tāṃ dārikāṃ kāṃtāṃ śiromuktāsraganvitām |
darśanīyāṃ subhadrāṃgīṃ prādrākṣuste ciraṃ mudā || 36.13{13} ||
ahosviddevakanyeyaṃ nūnaṃ mānyā na mānavī |
yadiyaṃ jātamātrāpi bhābhiḥ śobhate puram || 36.14{14} ||
iti saṃbhāṣya te sarve jñātivaṃdhū suhṛjjanāḥ |
suciraṃ tāṃ samālokya vismitā niryayustataḥ || 36.15{15} ||
tataḥ sa janakastasyāḥ kṛtvā jatimahaṃ mudā |
sarvāñjñātīn samāmaṃtrya pura evamabhāṣata || 36.16{16} ||
bhavanto'syāḥ sutāyā me dṛṣṭvā saṃpannimittamām |
yathā loke prasiddhaṃ syāttathā nāmābhidhīyatām || 36.17{17} ||
iti tenoditaṃ śrutvā sarve te jñātivāṃdhavāḥ |
tannimittaṃ samālokya taṃ puṣyamevamabruvan || 36.18{18} ||
sādho yatsaṃprajāteyaṃ muktāmālāśiroruhā |
tadasyā nāma mukteti prasiddhaṃ bhavatu dhruvam || 36.19{19} ||
evaṃ taiḥ jñātibhiḥ sarvaiḥ samākhyātaṃ niśamya saḥ |
puṣyastasyāḥ sutāyāstannāmaprasiddhamacārayat || 36.20{20} ||
tataḥ sā dārikā muktā dhātrībhiḥ pratipālitā |
aṣṭābhirabhipuṣyāṃgā pravṛddhābhūdhradābjavat || 36.21{21} ||
tataḥ sā dārikā kāntā pravṛddhābhūtkumārikā |
guruṇāṃ samupāśritya kramādvidyā aśikṣate || 36.22{21*} ||
tatra sā sumatīrmuktā lipipāraṃ gatā kramāt |
sarvaśāstrakalāvidyāpāraṃ yayau vicakṣaṇā || 36.23{22} ||
tataḥ sā dānasaṃraktā sarvārthibhyo yathepsitam |
saddharmapraṇidhānena dadau nityaṃ prasāditā || 36.24{23} ||
sarve'pi yācakāstasyā gṛhaṃ dṛṣṭvā samāgatāḥ |
taddattaṃ dravyamādāya prayayuḥ saṃpramoditāḥ || 36.25{24} ||
tadaiko brāhmaṇastasyā muktāmālāṃ śiroruhām |
dṛṣṭvā lobhākulātmā sa gṛhaṃ gatvā samāśrayat || 36.26{25} ||
tatra sa brāhmaṇo dṛṣṭvā tāṃ kanyāṃ saṃpradāyanīm |
upetyāśīrvacoddatvā prārthayadevamādarāt || 36.27{26} ||
jayo'stu te sadā bhadre ciraṃ jivyāḥ sukhānvitā |
yathā te vāṃchate cittaṃ tathā sarvaṃ prasiddhyatu || 36.28{27} ||
bhadre'haṃ te guṇācchrutvā dūrato yadihāvraje |
tadarthaṃ śṛṇu te vakṣye tacchrutvā me prasīdatu || 36.29{28} ||
yadiyaṃ te śirobaddhā muktāmālā praśobhitā |
tadimāṃ me pradatvātra mano mama vinodaya || 36.30{29} ||
iti saṃprārthite tena sā muktā saṃpradānikā |
tāṃ pradātuṃ samichantī taṃ vipramevamabravīt || 36.31{30} ||
sādhu vipra kṣaṇaṃ tiṣṭha mā vrajasvānyataḥ kvacit |
pitrorājñāṃ samāyācya dāsyāmi te imāmapi || 36.32{31} ||
ityuktvā sa samāśvāsya brāhmaṇaṃ taṃ pralobhitam |
sahasā purato gatvā pitrorevamabhāṣata || 36.33{32} ||
pitaramba gṛhe'smākaṃ brāhmaṇo'rthī samāgataḥ |
sa mamemāṃ śirorūḍhāṃ muktāmālāṃ prayācate || 36.34{33} ||
tadimāṃ dātumichāmi tasmai viprāya sādhave |
tadanujñāṃ pradattaṃ me yadyasti vāṃ dayāmapi || 36.35{34} ||
iti putryoditaṃ śrutvā sa pitā vismayāhataḥ |
tāṃ putrīṃ suciraṃ dṛṣṭvā paribhāṣyaivamabravīt || 36.36{35} ||
bho vatsa kiṃ samīkṣyaivaṃ svamastaka samudbhavām |
netravatkathamutkṛtya pradadyādarthine sute || 36.37{36} ||
yadi dātuṃ samichā te brāhmaṇāyārthine sute |
tadanyatprārthitaṃ dravyaṃ pradadasva yathepsitam || 36.38{37} ||
iti pitroditaṃ śrutvā sā muktā duhitā sudhīḥ |
janakaṃ taṃ samālokya praṇatvā caivamabravīt || 36.39{38} ||
śṛṇu tāta yadarthe'haṃ svaśiraḥsaṃbhavāmapi |
muktāmālaṃ samuddhṛtya ditsāmi tatpravakṣyate || 36.40{39} ||
anityaṃ khalu saṃsāre mānuṣye janma durlabham |
kṣaṇadhvaṃsi śarīraṃ ca jīvitaṃ ca tṛṇāmbuvat || 36.41{40} ||
tatrāpi bahavo rogāḥ saṃpado'pyaticaṃcalaḥ |
durlabhā kṣaṇasaṃpacca dharmamatiśca durlabhā || 36.42{41} ||
kiṃ nityamatra saṃsāre ko hi nityasthito bhave |
ityanityāmimāṃ muktāmālāṃ ditsāmyahaṃ pita || 36.43{42} ||
ko'nyatra mānuṣā dehāddānaṃ kartuṃ praśaknuyāt |
ityāpyahaṃ samīkṣyātra dātumiche kajāmapi || 36.44{43} ||
kasyātra vigrahe mṛtyorviśvāsaṃ vidyate khalu |
iti matvāpi tātemāṃ ditsāmi muktakāsrajam || 36.45{44} ||
kasyātra jīvitaṃ kāye svechayā samavasthitam |
evaṃ vijñāya tātemaṃ mālāṃ ditsāmi puṣpavat || 36.46{45} ||
ko nātra pīḍyate rogaiḥ sakleśairdevasaṃbhavaiḥ |
ityevaṃ samīkṣyaimāṃ dātumichāmi muktikām || 36.47{46} ||
kasya saṃpatsthirā gehe astāścapi sthitirna hi |
evaṃ vijñāya tatemaṃ ditsāmi kusumākṛtim || 36.48{47} ||
kasya cātra śubhe dṛṣṭirjāyāttiṣṭhecca sarvadā |
evaṃ vāpi parijñāya ditsāmīmāṃ kajāmapi || 36.49{48} ||
sadā na jāyate naivaṃ dharme matiḥ kathaṃ cana |
iti dṛṣṭvāpi me cittamimāṃ ditsati puṣpavat || 36.50{49} ||
imāṃ me daivasaṃjātāṃ śiraḥsthāṃ keśasādṛśīm |
dātumichāmyahaṃ tāta saddharmaratnalabdhaye || 36.51{50} ||
kiyatkālaṃ ca jīveyaṃ sarveṣāṃ maraṇaṃ dhruvam |
yāvadjīvitamuktāyāmiyaṃ muktāpi nakṣyati || 36.52{51} ||
yadiyaṃ me śirojāpi prāṇena saha nakṣyati |
taddattā vāpyadattāpi sarvathāpi vinakṣyati || 36.53{52} ||
etaddevaṃ samīkṣye māṃ muktāṃ puṣpasrajaṃ yathā |
kajāmapi samuddhṛtya dātumichāmyahaṃ pita || 36.54{53} ||
tadatra mā kṛthā vighnaṃ mama saddharmasādhane |
tadanujñāṃ pradatvā me prārthitaṃ saphalaṃ kuru || 36.55{54} ||
yadi na dīyate'nujñāṃ dānavighnaphalaṃ lābheḥ |
tato te duṣkṛtirjāyāttato hi durgatiṃ vrajeḥ || 36.56{55} ||
yadyanujñāṃ pradānena satyaṃ pūrayase mama |
tataste sukṛtirjāyāttena yāyāddhi sadgatim || 36.57{56} ||
sadgatau sarvadā saukhyaṃ bhuktvā careḥ śubheṣvapi |
tataḥ krameṇa saṃbodhiṃ prāpya buddhatvamāpnuyāḥ || 36.58{57} ||
evaṃ vījñāya tātātra vārayituṃ na me'rhati |
tadanujñapya me satyaṃ saṃpūrayitumarhati || 36.59{58} ||
iti putryā tayākhyātaṃ śrutvā pitā sa bodhitaḥ |
tāṃ muktāṃ sumātiḥ putrīṃ samālokyaivamabravīt || 36.60{59} ||
yattvayaivaṃ samākhyātaṃ tatsarvaṃ satyameva hi |
yadi śraddhāsti te dātuṃ pradehi kiṃ nivāraye || 36.61{60} ||
iti pitrābhyanujñāte sā muktā parimoditā |
tāṃ śiraḥsadbhavāṃ muktāṃ mālāmutkartumaichata || 36.62{61} ||
tadā sā jananī dṛṣṭvā tāṃ muktāṃ svātmajāṃ priyām |
śirojāmapi tāṃ muktāṃ mālāmutkartu vāṃchinīm || 36.63{62} ||
sahasā gāḍhamāliṃgya vārayituṃ samudyatā |
galadaśruviliptākṣā vilapaṃtyaivamabravīt || 36.64{63} ||
hā priye svātmaje putrī kathamajñāsi sāṃpratam |
indriyavattanūdbhūtāmapi dātuṃ yadīchasi || 36.65{64} ||
yacchirojāṃ samutkṛtya muktāmālāṃ svadaivajām |
kimanyadiha paśyanti viprāya dātumichasi || 36.66{65} ||
yadi dāne'tivāṃchā te pradehi tadyadipsitam |
imāmeva śirojatāṃ mā dāḥ kasmai cidarthine || 36.67{66} ||
damiyaṃ svatanūdbhūtā sahajā jīvitendraiḥ |
tadiyaṃ kathamutkṛtya dātavyeti vicāraya || 36.68{67} ||
yadīmaṃ svaśirorūḍhāṃ balenotkṛtya dāsyasi |
tadeyaṃ nakṣyate nūnaṃ jīvitena saha dhruvam || 36.69{68} ||
vinaṣṭe jīvite kāye ko dharmaṃ sādhayetpunaḥ |
dharmaṃ vinātra saṃsāre nirarthaṃ sarvasādhanam || 36.70{69} ||
dharmamūlaṃ hi saṃsāre satkāyajīvitaṃ kila |
tajjīvitāśrayaḥ kāyo rakṣitavyaḥ prayatnataḥ || 36.71{70} ||
yatkāyaprāṇarakṣārthaṃ saṃsāre dharmasādhanam |
kāyaprāṇaṃ vinā ko'rthaḥ saṃsāre dharmasādhane || 36.72{71} ||
dharmārthakāmamokṣādi sādhanaṃ sukhakāraṇam |
abhāve jīvite kāye kasyārthe sukhasādhanam || 36.73{72} ||
yatsvajīve na pakṣyaivaṃ kimarthe dātumichasi |
svakāyajīvarakṣārthaṃ dānādi puṇyasādhanam || 36.74{73} ||
svakāyajīvitaṃ tyaktvā kimarthe puṇyasādhanam |
svakāyajīva bhadrārthe guṇadravyavṛṣārjanam || 36.75{74} ||
iti satyaṃ mayākhyātaṃ hitārthaṃ te priyātmaje |
śrutvemaṃ netravadrakṣyā mā dāḥ kasmai cidarthine || 36.76{75} ||
yadi te'sti vṛṣe vāṃchā datveto'nyadyathepsitam |
gṛhe vrataṃ caratyevaṃ sukhena puṇyamarjaya || 36.77{76} ||
etatpuṇyavipākena sarvatra te śubhaṃ bhavet |
sarvadā durgatiṃ naiva yāyātsadā tu sadgatim || 36.78{77} ||
evaṃ matvātmaje śrutvā saddharmaṃ saugatoditam |
triratnabhajanaṃ kṛtvā gṛhe vrataṃ sukhaṃ cara || 36.79{78} ||
etatpuṇyaviśuddhā tvaṃ sarvadā śubhabhāvinī |
kramātsaṃbodhimāsādya saṃbuddhapadamāpnuyāḥ || 36.80{79} ||
iti mātrā samākhyātaṃ śrutvā muktātmajāpi sā |
utthāyāśruvirukṣā yayau śokālaye śanaiḥ || 36.81{80} ||
tatra śayyātalāsīnā muktādānacintayā |
saṃbuddhasmaraṇaṃ kṛtvā tasthau dhyānasamāhitā || 36.82{81} ||
tatra taddhyānasaṃmūḍhā nidrāsuptendriyāpi sā |
tameva śrīghanaṃ smṛtvā tasthau niścaritāśayāḥ || 36.83{82} ||
tāmevāṃ saṃsthitāṃ dṛṣṭvā bhagavān sa munīśvaraḥ |
svapna iva samābhāṣya muktāmevaṃ vyabodhayat || 36.84{83} ||
vatse mātra viṣīda tvaṃ pradeyeyaṃ kajāpi te |
pradīyatāṃ samuddhṛtya punaḥ prādurbhaviṣyati || 36.85{84} ||
iti saṃbhāṣitaṃ śrutvā svapne iva samutthitā |
sā muktā vismayākrāntacittā tasthau suniśalā || 36.86{85} ||
tataḥ sā taṃ jagannāthaṃ smṛtvā taccintayākulā |
kimetatsatyamevaṃ syāditi jñātuṃ samaichata || 36.87{86} ||
tataḥ sā svayamuddhṛtya svaśiraḥsthāṃ svapāṇinā |
muktāmālāṃ mudālokya tāṃ śāstre samakalpayat || 36.88{87} ||
tato'naṃtaramevaṃ sā muktāmālābhiśobhitā |
tasyāḥ śirasamubhūtā rarāja tatsamānikā || 36.89{88} ||
tāṃ samālokya sā muktā kanyātivismayānvitā |
sahasā purato gatvā māturevaṃ nyavedayat || 36.90{89} ||
mātaradya munīndrasya kṛpādṛṣṭiprasādataḥ |
yathā mayā pratijñātaṃ pūryyeta me tathā dhruvam || 36.91{90} ||
ityuktvā sā mudā muktā samuddhṛtāṃ śiroruhām |
ye ubhe darśayitvāgre māturevamabhāṣata || 36.92{91} ||
mātarahaṃ vipannāśā śayyā talasamāśritā |
saṃbuddhaṃ manasā dhyātvā tiṣṭhāmi dānaciṃtayā || 36.93{92} ||
yadā me dhyānasuptāyā hṛdi nidropasarpate |
tadaivaṃ sarvanāthena samādiṣṭaṃ śrutaṃ mayā || 36.94{93} ||
pradeyeyaṃ śirojātā muktāmālā tvayā mudā |
punaḥ prādurbhavedbhūyo'pyetatsamānikā dhruvam || 36.95{94} ||
iti śrutvā prabuddhāhaṃ kimetatsūnṛtaṃ bhavet |
iti kṛtvā samuddhṛtya paśyāmīmāṃ pramoditā || 36.96{95} ||
tato'naṃtaramevaṃ me śirasyaitatsamānikā |
muktāmālā samudbhūtā dṛśyatāmiyamambike || 36.97{96} ||
ityuktvā sā sutā muktā tasyā mātuḥ puro mudā |
muktāṃ mālāmupasthāpya darśayantyevamāha ca || 36.98{97} ||
imāṃ māturjagacchāstre mudā saṃkalpayāmyaham |
tadenāṃ trijagacchāstre saṃpradātuṃ vrajeya hi || 36.99{98} ||
iti putryā samākhyātaṃ śrutvā sā jananī mudā |
tāṃ muktāsrajamālokya tacchirojāṃ ca harṣitā || 36.100{99} ||
muhurmuhuḥ samīkṣantī tāṃ sutāmevamabravīt |
dhanyāsi bhadrikā putri tvayā yathā samīhitam || 36.101{100} ||
tathā te siddhyate nūnaṃ saṃbuddhasyānubhāvātaḥ |
tadimāṃ tvaṃ samādāyā yathā saṃkalpitaṃ tvayā || 36.102{1} ||
tathā tasmai munīndrāya prābhyarcya dātumarhasi |
tathā te'ṅgīkṛtaṃ putri tatsatyaṃ setsyate dhruvam || 36.103{2} ||
etatpuṇyānubhāvaiśca sarvadā sadgatiṃ vrajeḥ |
iti matvā jagacchāsturupahṛtya bhajādarāt || 36.104{3} ||
tathā cāsmai dvijāyaivamādāya dātumarhati |
tathā te'ṅgī kṛte siddhe sarvatra maṃgalaṃ bhavet || 36.105{4} ||
saphalaṃ mānuṣe janma sadā ca sadgatau sthitiḥ || 36.106{5} ||
iti mātroditaṃ śrutvā pratisaṃmoditāśayā |
sā tatheti pratiśrutya tathā kartuṃ samaichata || 36.107{6} ||
tathā saitatpravṛttāṃtaṃ bhartturagre nyavedayat |
śrutvā bharttāpi puṣyaḥ samuditābhyanvamodata || 36.108{7} ||
tasminnavasare tatra śrāvastyāṃ jetakāśrame |
vihāre bhagavān so'rhan vijahāra sasāṃghikaḥ || 36.109{8} ||
tatra sa trijagacchāstā bodhicaryāṃ prakāśayan |
sarvasatvasahitārthena dharmamādeṣṭumārabhat || 36.110{9} ||
tadā te bhikṣavaḥ sarve śrāvakāścailakādayaḥ |
bhikṣuṇyo vratinaścāpi bodhisatvagaṇā api || 36.111{10} ||
upāsakagaṇāścāpi tathā copāsikā api |
sarve te samupāśritya samātasthuḥ samāhitāḥ || 36.112{11} ||
tathā śakrādayo devā brahmādyā brahmacāriṇaḥ |
dānavā lokapālāśca yakṣagaṃdharvakinnarāḥ || 36.113{11!} ||
rākṣasā garuḍā nāgāḥ siddhavidyādharādayaḥ |
yatayo yoginaścāpi nirgranthāstīrthikā api || 36.114{12} ||
ṛṣayo brāhmaṇāścāpi tapaśvino maharddhikāḥ |
rājānaḥ kṣatriyāścāpi vaiśyā rājakumārakāḥ || 36.115{} ||
śreṣṭhino maṃtriṇo'mātyā gṛhasthāśca mahājanāḥ |
vaṇijaḥ sārthavāhāśca paurikāḥ śilpino'pi ca || 36.116{24} ||
grāmyā jānapadāścāpi tathānyaddeśavāsinaḥ |
sarve te samupāgatya dṛṣṭvā taṃ śrīghanaṃ mudā || 36.117{15} ||
natvā pradakṣiṇīkṛtya samabhyarcya yathākramam |
kṛtāñjalipuṭā natvā parivṛtya samaṃtataḥ || 36.118{16} ||
tatsaddharmāmṛtaṃ pātumupatasthuḥ samāhitāḥ |
tadā sa bhagavān dṛṣṭvā tān sarvān samupasthitān |
ādimadhyāṃtakalyāṇaṃ saddharmaṃ samupādiśat || 36.119{17} ||
tatsaddharmaṃ samākarṇya sarve lokāḥ [prabodhikāh] prabodhitāḥ |
sarvasatvahitārtheṣu babhūvuḥ bodhicāriṇaḥ || 36.120{18} ||
tadā sā kanyakā muktā sakhībhiḥ saha moditā[ḥ] |
muktāmālāṃ samādāya dharmaṃ śrotumupācarat || 36.121{19} ||
tatra taṃ śrīghanaṃ dṛṣṭvā natvā sā sāṃjalirmudā |
tridhā pradakṣiṇīkṛtya tasya śāstuḥ purogatā || 36.122{20} ||
tatrāgre tāmupasthapya muktāmālāṃ kṛtāṃjaliḥ |
pādau natvā prasannāsyā dharmaṃ śrotumupāśrayat || 36.123{21} ||
tataḥ sa bhagavāṃstasyā dṛṣṭvāśayaṃ viśuddhitam |
āryasatyaṃ samārabhya saddharmaṃ samupādiśat || 36.124{22} ||
tatsaddharmaṃ samākarṇya sā muktā paribodhitā |
dharmaviśeṣamājñāya babhūva bodhivāñchinī || 36.125{23} ||
tatra sā kanyakā muktā satkāyadṛṣṭiparvatam |
hitvā jñānāsinā śrotaāpattiphalamāyayau || 36.126{24} ||
tataḥ sā bhadrikā muktā dṛṣṭasatyā pramoditā |
sāṃjaliścaraṇau śāsturnatvā svagṛhamāyayau || 36.127{25} ||
tataḥ sā ca samādāya muktāmālaṃ svamūrddhajam |
suprasannāśayā tasmai dvijāya pradadau mudā || 36.128{26} ||
tāṃ muktāsrajamādāya brāhmaṇaḥ saṃpramoditaḥ |
tasyai bhadrāśiṣaṃ datvā sahasā svagṛhe'vrajat || 36.129{27} ||
tathā sā muditā gatvā sarvārthibhyo yathepsitam |
triratnabhajanaṃ kṛtvā śubhadharme samācarat || 36.130{28} ||
tadā tasyā guṇāñchrutvā bahavaḥ kāmarāgiṇaḥ |
saratnaprābhṛtaṃ datvā tāṃ prārthayitumīchire || 36.131{29} ||
kumārā bhurbhujā tasyāḥ pitaraṃ puṣyamādarāt |
svasvadūtaiḥ saratnāni datvā saṃprārthayanmudā || 36.132{30} ||
tathā dvijakumārāśca maṃtriputrāśca kāminaḥ |
vaiśyaputrāstathānye na dhanino vaṇigātmajāḥ || 36.133{31} ||
sārthavāhasutāścāpi tathā śilpisutā api |
mahājanasutāścaivamanye'pi kāmamohitāḥ || 36.134{32} ||
sarve te tāṃ subhadrāṃgīṃ muktāṃ muktābhyalaṃkṛtām |
dṛṣṭvā rāgāgnisaṃtaptāstanmukhāmṛtavāṃchinaḥ || 36.135{33} ||
savastrālaṃkāraratnāni datva dūtaiḥ pṛthakpṛthak |
tasyāstaṃ janakaṃ puṣyaṃ prārthayannevamādarāt || 36.136{34} ||
tathā taiḥ prārthyamāno'sau puṣyo dūtaiḥ pṛthakpṛthak |
taddattaṃ prābhṛtaṃ dṛṣṭvā tasthau taṃ kṣubhitāśayaḥ || 36.137{35} ||
tata utthāya puṣyaḥ sa śokāgāre viṣaṇṇadhīḥ |
gatvā śayyātalāsīno manasaivaṃ vyaciṃtayat || 36.138{36} ||
hāre duḥkhaṃ prajātaṃ me kiṃ kariṣyāmi sāṃpratam |
ekaiva duhitā me'sti yācakā bahavo'pi me || 36.139{37} ||
yadyekasmai pradāsyāmi sarve'nye syurmama dviṣaḥ |
atrāhaṃ kiṃ kariṣyāmi yadupāyaṃ na manyate || 36.140{38} ||
iti cintāviṣaṇṇāsyastasthau mohaviṣārditaḥ || 36.141{39!} ||
tatraivaṃ saṃsthitaṃ dṛṣṭvā janakaṃ taṃ samohitam |
sametya sātmajā muktā natvaivaṃ paryapṛchata || 36.142{40} ||
tāta kiṃ te mano duḥkhaṃ yadevaṃ tiṣṭhase'dhumā |
tatsatyaṃ samupākhyā prabodhayātmajāmimām || 36.143{41} ||
iti saṃprārthite putryā tayā sa janakaḥ sudhīḥ |
taṃ muktāṃ svātmajaṃ dṛṣṭvā sucirādevamabravīt || 36.144{42} ||
ayi putri subhadrāṃgī kimatrāhaṃ vadeya hi |
yattvamekā sutā me'sti yācakā bahavāstava || 36.145{43} ||
yadyekasmai pradāsyāmi tvāmekaṃ divyasuṃdarīm |
te sarve'nye bhaveyurme śatravo hyapakāriṇaḥ || 36.146{44} ||
iti cintāvidagdhātmā tiṣṭhāmyevaṃ hi nānyathā |
tadupāyaṃ na paśyāmi yato me jāyate bhayam || 36.147{45} ||
iti pitroditaṃ śrutvā sā muktā duhitā punaḥ |
janakaṃ taṃ samālokya bodhayantyevamabravīt || 36.148{46} ||
nāhaṃ kāmārthinī tāta saṃbuddhaśāsane gatā |
pravrajya saṃvaraṃ dhṛtvā cariṣyāmi śubhāṃ carim || 36.149{47} ||
iti me niścitaṃ cittamanyathā na karomi hi |
tadatra mā viṣīda tvaṃ tadanujñāṃ pradehi me || 36.150{48} ||
iti putryā tathākhyātaṃ sa puṣyo janakaḥ sudhīḥ |
svātmajāṃ tāṃ samālokya punarevamabhāṣata || 36.151{49} ||
ayi putri kathaṃ tāvatpravrajyāṃ carituṃ saheḥ |
tvaṃ hi bālā subhadrāṅgī kumārī sukhamedhinī || 36.152{50} ||
tasmādyāvatkumārī tvaṃ tāvadgehe samāśritā |
triratnabhajanaṃ kṛtvā caropāsakasaṃvaram || 36.153{51} ||
iti pitryoditaṃ śrutvā sā muktā duhitā sudhīḥ |
tathā hīti pratiśrutya prabodhitānvamoditaḥ || 36.154{52} ||
tathā sā bhadrikā muktā śuddhaśīlā subhāvinī |
triratnabhajanaṃ kṛtvā cere upāsikāvratam || 36.155{53} ||
yadā sā yauvanī kāṃtā muktā muktātiśobhitā |
tadā kaniṣṭhaputreṇa dṛṣṭānāthānnasaṃstṛtaḥ || 36.156{54} ||
tataḥ so'nāthabhṛtputraḥ supriyākhyo'bhimoditaḥ |
kāmarāgāgnisaṃtapto rogī vābhūdviṣaṇṇadhīḥ || 36.157{55} ||
taṃ putraṃ kāmarāgārttaṃ dṛṣṭvā so'nāthapiṇḍadaḥ |
tannimittaṃ samālokya samāśvāsyaivamabravīt || 36.158{56} ||
bho putraivaṃ kimarthe tvaṃ rogīva tiṣṭhase'dhunā |
tadvadasva mamāgre'pi yadichati manastava || 36.159{57} ||
iti pitroditaṃ śrutvā supriyaḥ sa viniḥśvasan |
pitaraṃ taṃ samālokya śanairevamabhāṣata || 36.160{58} ||
tāta yo'sau subhadrāṅgī muktā muktātiśobhitā |
dṛṣṭvā mayānurūpeti mano me harate'pi sā || 36.161{59} ||
tadabhilāṣarāgāgnidāhasaṃtoṣitaṃ manaḥ |
tadrāgāgniśamopāyaṃ kartumarhati me'dhunā || 36.162{60} ||
iti putroditaṃ śrutvā so'nāthapiṇḍadaḥ pitā |
putrasya prāṇarakṣārthaṃ yācituṃ tāṃ samaihata || 36.163{61} ||
tataḥ sa dūtamāhūya pura evamabhāṣata |
sādho gacha gṛhasthasya puṣyasyaiva paro vada || 36.164{62} ||
sādho tadduhitā bhadrā taddīyatāṃ sutāya me |
evaṃ kṛte'pi saṃbaṃdhaṃ yāvajjīvaṃ sukhāya nau || 36.165{63} ||
ityuktvā tasya puṣyasya tāṃ muktākhyāṃ subhāvinīm |
prārthayitvā sutāyāsmai dātuṃ me'rhati sarvathā || 36.166{64} ||
iti tenoditaṃ śrutvā tatheti pratibhāṣya saḥ |
dūtaḥ prābhṛtamādāya puṣyasya samupācarat || 36.167{65} ||
tatra tasya puraḥ sthāpya prābhṛtaṃ saṃvinodayan |
yathānāthabhṛtādiṣṭaṃ tathā sarvaṃ nyavedayat || 36.168{66} ||
taduktaṃ sarvamākarṇya sa puṣyaḥ paribodhitaḥ |
muktāṃ tāṃ svātmajāṃ putrīṃ samāmaṃtryaivamabravīt || 36.169{67} ||
ayi priyātmaje putri tvamanāthabhṛtā satā |
supriyāya svaputrāya prārthitā tvaṃ prasīda tat || 36.170{68} ||
iti pitroditaṃ śrutvā muktā sā duhitā satī |
pitaraṃ taṃ samālokya praṇatvaivamabhāṣata || 36.171{69} ||
nāhaṃ kāmārthinī tāta saṃbuddhaśaraṇaṃ gatā |
pravrajyāsaṃvaraṃ dhṛtvā cariṣyāmi śubhāṃ carīm || 36.172{70} ||
iti putryoditaṃ śrutvā sa puṣyo'bhihatāśayaḥ |
duhitāṃ tāṃ ciraṃ dṛṣṭvā punarevamabhāṣata || 36.173{71} ||
he sute kathameva tvaṃ vadethāḥ kiṃ na manyase |
yadasau gṛhabhṛnnāthastadvacaḥ śrūyate na kaiḥ || 36.174{72} ||
tadyāvadyauvanī bhūtā tāvadbharttā sahānugāḥ |
triratnabhajanaṃ kṛtvā cara vrataṃ gṛhe sukham || 36.175{73} ||
yadā vṛddhā vyavasthāsyai tadā tvaṃ saugatāśrame |
pravrajyāsaṃvaraṃ dhṛtvā pracarasva śubhāṃ carīm || 36.176{74} ||
iti pitrā samādiṣṭaṃ śrutvā sā duhitā sudhīḥ |
pitro vākyaṃ kathaṃ laṃghyamiti matvaivamabravīt || 36.177{75} ||
nāhaṃ kāmarthinī tāta saddharmasādhanārthinī |
tathāpi tvadvaco'laṃghyamiti śroṣyāmi te vacaḥ || 36.178{76} ||
yadi me samayaṃ dhṛtvā satyavākyaṃ dadāti me |
vṛṇuyāṃ taṃ patiṃ nanumiti me vacanaṃ dhruvam || 36.179{77} ||
iti putryoditaṃ śrutva sa puṣyaḥ paribodhitaḥ |
etatsarvaṃ pravṛttāṃtaṃ dūtasyāgre nyavedayat || 36.180{78} ||
dūtāpi tatpravṛttāṃtaṃ śrutvā tatheti satvarāḥ |
anāthapiṇḍadasyāgre vistareṇa nyavedayat || 36.181{79} ||
so'nāthapiṇḍadaścāpi śrutvā sa pratimoditaḥ |
sarvametatpravṛttāṃtaṃ putrasyāgre nyavedayat || 36.182{80} ||
etatpitrā samākhyātaṃ śrutvā sa supriyaḥ sudhīḥ |
tathā hīti pratijñāya dūtasyāgre nyavedayat || 36.183{81} ||
dūtaśca tadvacaḥ śrutvā puṣyasya purato gataḥ |
sarvametatpravṛttāṃtaṃ vistareṇa nyavedayat || 36.184{82} ||
puṣyo'pi tatsamākhyātaṃ śrutvā patnyāḥ puro'vadat |
sāpi tajjananī śrutvā putryā agre nyavedayat || 36.185{83} ||
tanmātrā samupādiṣṭaṃ śrutvā sā duhitā satī |
tathā hīti pratiśrutya tūṣṇībhūtvādhyuvāsa tat || 36.186{84} ||
tataḥ sa janakaḥ puṣyo matvā putryādhivāsitam |
tasyā vivāhasāmagrīṃ sahasā samasādhayat || 36.187{85} ||
tataḥ sa janakaḥ puṣyo'pyanāthapiṇḍadātmajam |
supriyaṃ taṃ samānīya satkṛtya samamānayat || 36.188{86} ||
tatastāmātmajāṃ muktāṃ supriyāya subhāvine |
saṃkalpya pradadau puṣyo janakaḥ sa yathāvidhiḥ || 36.189{87} ||
tataḥ sa supriyo bharttā tāṃmuktāṃ ramaṇīṃ priyām |
bhāryāṃ svagṛhe ānīya reme bhuktvā yathāsukham || 36.190{88} ||
evaṃ sā bhāvinī muktā kāñcitkālaṃ gṛhe mudā |
bharttrā saha yathākāmaṃ bhuktvā reme pramoditā || 36.191{89} ||
tatastau daṃpatī dehe vṛddhatva samupākrame |
pitroḥ pādān praṇatvaivaṃ prārthayataṃ samādarāt || 36.192{90} ||
pitarau yatkṛtaṃ satyamāvābhyāṃ tatsusiddhaye |
pūrayituṃ samichāvastadājñāṃ dātumarhatha || 36.193{91} ||
iti saṃprārthitaṃ tābhyāṃ śrutvā pitā sa sā prasūḥ |
dṛṣṭvā tau daṃpatī śrāddhāyuktāvevaṃ samūcatuḥ || 36.194{92} ||
yadi vāṃ vidyate vāṃchā carituṃ saugataṃ vratam |
tatsatyapūraye tasmātpracarata samāhitau || 36.195{93} ||
iti tābhyāmanujñāte daṃpatī tau pramoditau |
pitroḥ pādān praṇatvaiva pratasthuḥ sahasā gṛhāt || 36.196{94} ||
tatastau jetakodyāne vihāre samupāgatau |
bhagavantaṃ tamānamya pravrajyāṃ samayācatām || 36.197{95} ||
bhagavannātha sarvajña vijānāti yadāvayoḥ |
satyaṃ tatpūrayiṣyāvastatpravrajyāṃ dadātu vām || 36.198{96} ||
iti saṃprārthite tābhyaṃ bhagavāṃ dakṣapāṇinā |
spṛṣṭvā tacchirasordṛṣṭvā samāmaṃtryaivamādiśat || 36.199{97} ||
evaṃ caratamādāya pravrajyāvratamuttamam |
svaparātmahitārtheṣu brahmacaryaṃ samāhitau || 36.200{98} ||
ityādiṣṭe munīndreṇa tāvubhāvapi muṃḍitau |
khikkhirīpātravibhrāṇau ca bhartuścīvarāvṛtau || 36.201{99} ||
tato'vidyāgaṇaṃ bhitvā prāptavidyāviśāradau |
sarvakleśagaṇāñjitvā sākṣādarhatvamāvatuḥ || 36.202{100} ||
tatastau vimalātmānau priśuddhatrimaṇḍalau |
jitendriyāū samācārau nirvikalpau niraṃjanau || 36.203{1} ||
sarveṣāmapi lokānāṃ traidhātukanivāsinām |
pūjyau mānyau ca vaṃdyau tāvabhūtaṃ brahmacāriṇau || 36.204{2} ||
taddṛṣṭvā te bhikṣavaḥ sarve vismayoddhatamānasā |
bhagavaṃtaṃ tamānamya paprachustatpurākṛtam || 36.205{3} ||
bhagavan kiṃ kṛtaṃ karma purābhyāṃ sukṛtaṃ kuha |
tatsarvaṃ samupādiśya sarvānnaḥ paribodhaya || 36.206{4} ||
iti saṃprārthite sarvairbhikṣubhiḥ sa munīśvaraḥ |
sarvāṃstāṃ śrāvakān bhikṣūn samālokyaivamādiśat || 36.207{5} ||
śṛṇuta bhikṣavaḥ karma yadābhyāṃ prakṛtaṃ purā |
tatsarvaṃ vaḥ pravakṣyāmi śrutvā cābhyanumodata || 36.208{6} ||
tadyathābhūtpurā śāstā kāśyapākhyamunīśvaraḥ |
sarvajño'rhaṃ jagannātho dharmarājastathāgataḥ || 36.209{7} ||
bhagavān sa mṛgāraṇye vārāṇasyāṃ jinārame |
sarvasatvahitārthena vijahāra sasāṃghikaḥ || 36.210{8} ||
tadā tatra mahān sādhuḥ śrīmāñchrīdopamaḥ sudhīḥ |
sadā tasya munīndrasya saddharmaśrāvako'bhajat || 36.211{9} ||
tataḥ sa buddhimān dātā sarvalokahitārthataḥ |
chandakabhikṣaṇaṃ tatra yācitvā paṃcavārṣikam || 36.212{10} ||
kāśyapasya munīndrasya sasaṃghaya niraṃtaram |
sapūjābhojanairnityamupasthātuṃ samaichata || 36.213{11} ||
tataḥ sa nṛpaterājñāṃ samāsādya pramoditaḥ |
hastiskandhe samāruhya ghaṇṭāvādyaṃ pravādayan || 36.214{12} ||
sarvatra viṣaye tatra vārāṇasyāḥ samaṃtataḥ |
chaṃdakabhikṣaṇaṃ sārddhaṃ yācituṃ prācarajjanaiḥ || 36.215{13} ||
taṃ dṛṣṭvā sārthavāhasya bhāryā bhadrā subhāvinī |
sā tasyai śiraso mucya muktāhāraṃ dadau mudā || 36.216{14} ||
tatastasyāḥ śiraskaṃ tanmuktāhāravyapohitam |
dṣṭvā sa vismito hāryyaṃ tāmeva paryapṛchata || 36.217{15} ||
kvāsau bhadre śirasthaste muktāhāro na vidyate |
kasyai dattastvayā kena hṛto vā tadvadasva me || 36.218{16} ||
iti bhartrābhipṛṣṭe sā muditā racitāṃjaliḥ |
bharttāraṃ taṃ praṇatvaivaṃ vinodayitumabravīt || 36.219{17} ||
āryaputra prasīdātra mā kṛthā roṣatāmiha |
yanmayā śraddhayā dattaśchaṃdakabhikṣaṇe'pyasau || 36.220{18} ||
iti bhāryoditaṃ śrutvā sārthavāhastataścaran |
niṣkrīya bahumūlyena tasyai patnyai dadau punaḥ || 36.221{19} ||
taṃ muktāhāramālokya sārthabhṛtaḥ priyāpi sā |
saṃpradattaṃ mayā śāstu iti taṃ nāgrahīdapi || 36.222{20} ||
tataḥ sa sārthabhṛtsvāmī tāṃ bhāryāṃ ramaṇīṃ priyām |
snehārdacakṣuṣā dṛṣṭvā punarevamabhāṣata || 36.223{21} ||
bahumūlyena bhadre yaṃ mayā krītastu dīyate |
tatkasmānnechasīdaṃ madgrahītuṃ kathameva hi || 36.224{22} ||
iti bhartroditaṃ śrutvā sā dattaṃ svāminā punaḥ |
taṃ muktāhāramādāya bharttuścittaṃ vyanodayat || 36.225{23} ||
tataḥ sā viṣayāraktā saddharmaguṇavāṃchinī |
taṃ saṃpūjāṃgamādāya yayau śāsturmudāśrame || 36.226{24} ||
tatraitadgandhakuṭyāṃ sā kṛtvā sugaṃdhilepanam |
supuṣpaiśca samākīryya natvā kṛtvā pradakṣiṇām || 36.227{25} ||
tataḥ sā muditā tasya kāśyapasya jagadguroḥ |
muktāhāraṃ puraḥ kṣiptvā praṇanāma kṛtāṃjaliḥ || 36.228{26} ||
tato buddhānubhavena muktāhāraḥ sa tatkṣaṇam |
tasya śāstuḥ śiraḥ saṃstho rarāja saṃprabhāsayan || 36.229{27} ||
taddṛṣṭvā sā suprasannāsyāḥ saṃbodhiguṇalālasāḥ |
śāstuḥ pādau praṇatvaivaṃ praṇidhānaṃ vyadhānmudā || 36.230{28} ||
etatpuṇyavipākena śāstāramīdṛśaṃ jinam |
ārāgyedṛgguṇānāṃ ca lābhī syāṃ sarvathā bhave || 36.231{29} ||
evaṃ sā bhāvinī kāṃtā tasya śāstu jagadguroḥ |
praṇidhānaṃ sadā kṛtvā prabheje saṃprasāditā || 36.232{30} ||
yo'sau sārthapaterbhāryā mukteyaṃ sā subhāvinī |
manyatāmiti yuṣmābhirnānyathā tu hi bhikṣavaḥ || 36.233{31} ||
yadanayā tadā tasya kāśyapasya jagadguroḥ |
pūjāṅgaiḥ śraddhayābhyarcya muktāhāraḥ praḍhokitaḥ || 36.234{32} ||
etatpuṇyavipākena jāteyaṃ sukule'dhunā |
śirasyasyāśca saṃrūḍho muktāmālā sahodbhavā || 36.235{33} ||
yathānayā kṛtaṃ tatra praṇidhānaṃ tathā'dhunā |
māṃ śāstāramāgamya sākṣādarhatvamāpyate || 36.236{34} ||
evaṃ hi yatkṛtaṃ yena tenaiva bhujyate phalam |
abhuktaṃ kṣīyate naiva karma na cānyathā kvacit || 36.237{35} ||
evaṃ matvātra saṃsāre sarvadā śubhavāṃchibhiḥ |
saddharmaṃ samupāśritya caritavyaṃ śubhe sadā || 36.238{36} ||
śubhe caranti ye nityaṃ na te gachaṃti durgatīm |
sadgatimeva saṃyānti bodhiṃ ca samavāpnuyuḥ || 36.239{37} ||
ityādiṣṭaṃ munīndreṇa śrutvā te sāṃghikā api |
sarve tatheti vijñapya prābhyanaṃdan prabodhitāḥ || 36.240{38} ||
ityādiṣṭaṃ hi me śāstrā śrutaṃ mayā tathocyate |
śrutvāpyevaṃ tathā rājaṃ caritavyaṃ śubhe sadā || 36.241{39} ||
prajāścāpi mahārāja bodhayitvā prayatnataḥ |
bodhimārge pratisthāpya pālayasva samāhitaḥ || 36.242{40} ||
tathā hi te sadā bhadraṃ sarvatrāpi bhaveddhruvam |
kramādbodhiṃ ca saṃprāpya saṃbuddhapadamāpnuyāḥ || 36.243{41} ||
iti tenārhatādiṣṭaṃ śrutvāśokaḥ sa bhūpatiḥ |
tathā hīti pratijñapya prābhyanaṃdatsapārṣadaḥ || 36.244{42} ||
muktāvadānaṃ manujā idaṃ ye śṛṇvaṃti ye cāpi niśāmayanti |
te sarva evaṃ sukhitā śubhāni kṛtvā prayānti sugatālayan te || 36.245{43} ||
iti ratnāvadānatatve muktāvadānaṃ samāptam
dhīmatyāvadāna
athāśoko mahīndro'sau kṛtāṃjaliḥ pramoditaḥ |
upaguptaṃ tamarhantaṃ natvaivaṃ punarabravīt || 37.1{1} ||
bhadanta ye mahāsatvā bodhisatvā yathepsitam |
sarvārthibhyaḥ pradatvaivaṃ dānaṃ kurvanti sarvadā || 37.2{2} ||
teṣāmevaṃ pradatte'pi taddravyaṃ kṣīyate na hi |
punarvṛddhitamaṃ yāti tatkathaṃ me samādiśa || 37.3{3} ||
katamadvratapuṇyasya prabhāvācchrīḥ pravarddhate |
tadvrataṃ me bhavāñchāstā samyagādeṣṭumarhati || 37.4{4} ||
iti saṃprārthite rājñā śrutvā so'rhan yatiḥ sudhīḥ |
upagupto narendraṃ taṃ samālokyaivamādiśat || 37.5{5} ||
śṛṇu rājanmahābhāga yathā me guruṇoditam |
tathāhaṃ te pravakṣyāmi loke dharmābhivṛddhaye || 37.6{6} ||
ye satyasamādhānāḥ śuddhaśīlāḥ śubhārthinaḥ |
vasudhārāmahādevyāścaranti vratamuttamam || 37.7{7} ||
teṣāṃ na kṣīyate dravyaṃ pradatte'pi sadā gṛhe |
pravṛddhitarameva syādvasudhārānubhāvataḥ || 37.8{8} ||
tadyathāsītpurā buddhaḥ śākyasiṃho munīśvaraḥ |
sarvajño'rhañjagacchāstā dharmarājastathāgataḥ || 37.9{9} ||
bhagavānnekadā tatra vārāṇasyāḥ purāntike |
mṛgadave jināvāse vijahāra sasāṃghikaḥ || 37.10{10} ||
tadā taddharmapīyūṣaṃ pātuṃ śakrādayo'marāḥ |
brahmādi brāhmaṇāścāpi sarve lokādhipā api || 37.11{11} ||
siddhā vidyādharā daityā yakṣagaṃdharvakinnarāḥ |
rākṣasā garuḍā nāgāstathānye'pi samāgatāḥ || 37.12{12} ||
yatayo yoginaścāpi tīrthikāśca tapaśvinaḥ |
nirgranthā vītarāgāśca munayo brahmacāriṇaḥ || 37.13{13} ||
evamanye'pi lokāḥ saṃbuddhadharmavāṃchinaḥ |
tatsaddharmāmṛtaṃ pātuṃ mudā tatra samāgatāḥ || 37.14{14} ||
tatpurādhipatī rājā viśvabhadrābhidho mahān |
tasya bhāryā priyādevī ratnamālābhidhā satī || 37.15{15} ||
tadātmajakumārāṃśaḥ sūryaprabhābhidhaḥ kṛtī |
tadātmajā ramākārā kumārī vimalābhidhā || 37.16{16} ||
anye'pyantarjanāścāpi sa jñātimitravāṃdhavāḥ |
purohitādayaścāpi vedavijñā dvijātayaḥ || 37.17{17} ||
vaiśyāśca maṃtriṇo'mātyā gṛhasthāśca mahājanāḥ |
vaṇijaḥ sārthavāhāśca śilpinaścāpi paurikāḥ || 37.18{18} ||
grāmyā jānapadāścāpi tathā kārpaṭikā api |
evamanye'pi lokāśca sarve te samupāgatāḥ || 37.19{19} ||
tatretya taṃ sabhāsīnaṃ sarvasaṃghapuraskṛtam |
saṃbuddhaṃ śrīghanaṃ dṛṣṭvā muditāḥ samupācaran || 37.20{20} ||
tatra sarve'pi te lokāḥ samabhyarcya yathākramam |
tridhā pradakṣiṇīkṛtvā praṇatvā taṃ munīśvaram || 37.21{21} ||
tatsaddharmāmṛtaṃ pātuṃ parivṛtya samaṃtataḥ |
puraskṛtya samādhāya samupatasthurādritā || 37.22{22} ||
atha sa bhagavān dṛṣṭvā tān sarvān samupasthitān |
bodhicaryāṃ samārabhya dideśa dharmamuttamam || 37.23{23} ||
tatsaddharmaṃ samākarṇya sarvalokāḥ prabodhitāḥ |
bodhicittaṃ samāsādya bhavaṃti bodhicāriṇaḥ || 37.24{24} ||
āsīttatsamaye tatra vārāṇasyāṃ gṛhādhipaḥ |
dharmadhvajābhidhaḥ śreṣṭhī dātā sādhurmahājanaḥ || 37.25{25} ||
tasya bhāryā priyā kāntā śāntamatīti viśrutā |
putrāśca bahavastasya sutāścāpyabhavaṃstathā || 37.26{26} ||
pautrāḥ karmakarāḥ preśyā dāsī dāsajanā api |
aneke parivārāśca vaṃdhumitrasuhṛjjanāḥ || 37.27{27} ||
sarve te poṣitāstena saṃbhṛtya paripālitāḥ |
poṣaṇīyā gṛhasthasya sarve hi samupāśritāḥ || 37.28{28} ||
arthino'pi sadā tasya gṛhe'nekasamāgatā |
śubhāśīrvacanairnityaṃ mahotsāhaṃ pracakrire || 37.29{29} ||
so'pi dharmadhvajo dātā tān sarvānarthino janān |
yathābhilaṣitairdravyaistoṣayitvābhyamodayat || 37.30{30} ||
evaṃ pradadatastasya kramātsaṃpatkṣayaṃ gatā |
taddṛṣṭvā sa gṛhī dānavighnaśaṃkārdito'bhavat || 37.31{31} ||
tataḥ śokālaye sthitvā tatsaṃpatkṣīyaciṃtayā |
viniśvasan viṣaṇṇātmā tasthau bhogyanirutsahaḥ || 37.32{32} ||
tatastasya priyā bhāryā śāntamatī vicakṣaṇā |
bharttāraṃ taṃ viṣaṇṇāsyaṃ dṛṣṭvaivaṃ paryapṛchata || 37.33{33} ||
svāminnevaṃ kimālokya tiṣṭhase duḥkhaciṃtayā |
yatte'tra jāyate duḥkhaṃ tadvadasva manogatam || 37.34{34} ||
iṭi bhāryoditaṃ śrutvā sa bharttābhiviniśvasan |
tāṃ bhāryāṃ supriyāṃ kāṃtāṃ samālokyaivamabravīt || 37.35{35} ||
ayi priye mahadduḥkhaṃ hṛdi me jāyate'dhunā |
yatsaṃpadaḥ parikṣīṇā āyā me'tra na vidyate || 37.36{36} ||
putrapautrādayo'neke bahayo me kuṭumbinaḥ |
karmakarādayo bhṛtyā dāsīdāsādayo janāḥ || 37.37{37} ||
anye'pi bahavaḥ preṣyāḥ pālanīyā divāniśam |
arthino yācakāśca bahavo nityamāgatāḥ |
kathaṃ tānstoṣayiṣyāmi kramātkṣīṇā hi saṃpadaḥ || 37.38{38} ||
nūnaṃ me yācakāḥ sarve śūnyahastā gṛhādataḥ |
parikhinnavibhinnāsyā vrajiṣyanti nirāśayāḥ || 37.39{39} ||
iti cintāviṣaṇṇe me hṛdi duḥkhaṃ prajāyate |
tadduḥkhānalasaṃtaptastiṣṭhāmyevaṃ vimohitaḥ || 37.40{40} ||
kiṃ hi dānamahotsāhaṃ vinā gehe sukhaṃ bhavet |
paśuvatkevalaṃ bhuktvā kiṃ sāraṃ satsukhaṃ vinā || 37.41{41} ||
iti bhartroditaṃ śrutvā sātha śāntamatī satī |
bharttāraṃ taṃ samāśvāsya bodhayantyevamabravīt || 37.42{42} ||
āryaputra kimatrāpi jātā te hṛdi duḥkhatā |
kiṃ viṣādenā siddhyeta kevalaṃ dahyate manaḥ || 37.43{43} ||
avaśyaṃ bhāvino bhāvā bhavanti mahatāmapi |
tadviṣādaṃ parityajya cara śubhe samāhitaḥ || 37.44{44} ||
iti bhāryoditaṃ śrutvā sa bharttābhiviniḥśvasan |
tāṃ bhāryāṃ supriyāṃ kāṃtāṃ samālokyaivamabravīt || 37.45{45} ||
ayi priye śubhaṃ kāryyaṃ nābhijānāmi sanmate |
tadupāyaṃ samākhyāya māṃ bodhayitumarhati || 37.46{46} ||
iti bharttroditaṃ śrutvā sā ca śāṃtamatī satī |
bharttāraṃ taṃ samāśvāsya prabodhayitumabravīt || 37.47{47} ||
āryaputra mahāsādho tadupāyaṃ prakathyate |
kauśāṃvyāṃ ghoṣirārāme śākyasiṃho munīśvaraḥ || 37.48{48} ||
sarvajñaḥ sugato buddho hīnadīnānukaṃpakaḥ |
sarvavidyākalābhijñaḥ ṣaḍabhijño vicakṣaṇaḥ || 37.49{49} ||
mārajillokavinnātho vināyakastathāgataḥ |
dvātriṃśallakṣaṇāśītivyaṃjanapratimaṇḍitaḥ || 37.50{50} ||
vyāma prabhābhibhāsvaṃtaṃ śatasūryādhikaprabham |
jaṃgamamiva ratnābhaṃ saumyaṃ samaṃtabhadrakam || 37.51{51} ||
sarvasatvahitārthena vijahāra sasāṃghikaḥ |
tatra gatvā praṇatvā tattaṃ buddhaṃ śṛṇutādarāt || 37.52{52} ||
tadā sa bhagavān buddhastadupāyaṃ samādiśet || 37.53{53!} ||
iti bhāryāsamādiṣṭaṃ śrutvā dharmadhvajo'pi saḥ |
tatheti paribhāṣitvā sa bharttā saṃpramoditaḥ || 37.54{54} ||
tadā sa dharmadhvajo bhāryaṃ velāmādāya prācarat |
tato vihāre gatvā sa bhagavaṃtaṃ sasāṃghikam |
dūrataḥ praṇatiṃ kṛtvā ekasthāne samāśrayat || 37.55{55} ||
tadā sa bhagavān dṛṣṭvā taṃ dharmadhvajaduḥkhitam |
jānannapyāgataṃ kāmaṃ dharmadhvaje samabravīt || 37.56{56} ||
sādho kasmādihāgatya tiṣṭhase tatsamādiśa |
iti śāstrā samādiṣṭaṃ śrutvā dharmadhvajo'pi saḥ || 37.57{57} ||
kṛtāṃjalipuṭo natvā bhagavaṃtamevamabravīt |
bhagavan yadvijānīyā nānya gacchāmi me vibho |
tvayi mama kāryyameka pṛchāmīti vyacintayat || 37.58{58} ||
tadyathā me mahāsaṃpatkṣīṇaṃ yāti daridratām |
punarvṛddhigataṃ dravyaṃ tatkathaṃ me samādiśa || 37.59{59} ||
katamadvratapuṇyasya prabhāvācchrī pravarddhate |
tadvrataṃ me bhavāñchāstā samyagādeṣṭumarhati || 37.60{60} ||
iti saṃprārthitaṃ tena śrutvā sa bhagavānmudā |
dharmadhvajaṃ tamāmaṃtrya saṃpaśyannevamabravīt || 37.61{61} ||
sādhu śṛṇu samādhāya tadvidhiṃ saṃpravakṣyate |
tacchrutvā te prabodhitvā samācara vidhānataḥ || 37.62{62} ||
vasudhārāvrataṃ samyakyathā śrutaṃ mayā purā |
vajradharābdhigaṃbhīranirghoṣeṇopadeśitam || 37.63{63} ||
tathāthā prathamaṃ tāvacchuddhabhūmau parigraham |
kṛtvā mṛdgomayāmbhobhiḥ saṃlipya paryaśobhayet || 37.64{64} ||
tataḥ sadgurumārādhya natvā kṛtāṃjalirmudā |
satkṛtya prārthayedevaṃ vasudhārāvrataṃ varam || 37.65{65} ||
tataḥ sa guruṇā sārddhaṃ snātvā tīrthe viśuddhadhīḥ |
śuddhavastrāvṛtāḥ śuddhacitto brahmavihārikaḥ || 37.66{66} ||
māse bhādrapade kṛṣṇapakṣe māghe'pi vā punaḥ |
tṛtīyāyāṃ tithau devīṃ samāvāhya vrataṃ caret || 37.67{67} ||
pūrvasmin divase vighnān kīlayetsarvadiksthitān |
tatra subhūtale sūtraṃ pātayitvā yathāvidhi || 37.68{68} ||
varttayenmaṃḍalaṃ devyāḥ sagaṇapratimaṇḍitam || 37.69{69!} ||
evaṃ tanmaṃḍalaṃ kṛtvā saptavrīhisuveṣṭitam |
tato'ṣṭayakṣaṇīcihnaṃ śobhitaiḥ kalaśairvṛtam || 37.70{70} ||
sapatākadhvajachatravitānaiḥ parimaṇḍitam |
kṛtvā tanmaṇḍalaṃ vajrī pratiṣṭhāpya yathāvidhi || 37.71{71} ||
vicitrapuṣpamālābhiḥ pradīpaiḥ saṃprabhojvalaiḥ |
saurabhyadhūpanaiścāpi samabhyarcyābhiśobhayet || 37.72{72} ||
tatastanmaṇḍale devī sagaṇāṃ parisaṃsmaran |
dhyātvā jāgaraṇaṃ kṛtvā tadrātrīṃ vyatilaṃghayet || 37.73{73} ||
tataḥ prātaḥ samutthāya tīrthe snātvā suśuddhadhīḥ |
śucivastrāvṛte paṃcagavyaiḥ svāṃgaṃ vyaśodhayet || 37.74{74} ||
tataḥ sa sadguruṃ natvā tadājñāṃ śirasāvahan |
uttarābhimukhaḥ śuddha āsane samupāśrayet || 37.75{75} ||
tatastāvadguruṃ natvā triratnaśaraṇaṃ gataḥ |
maṃḍale sagaṇāṃ devīṃ dhyātvāvāhya samarcayet || 37.76{76} ||
tato'rahaṃ saṃpradatvāgre praṇatvā sāṃjalirmudā |
japastotrādikaṃ kṛtvā kuryācca pāpadeśanām || 37.77{77} ||
puṇyānumodanāṃ kṛtvā prārthayedbodhisaṃvaram || 37.78{78!} ||
tataḥ pradakṣiṇāṃ kṛtvā natvāstāṃgaiśca sāṃjaliḥ |
tataḥ pītamayaṃ sūtraṃ ṣoḍaśabhedavarttitam |
śuddhaṃ gururmaṃtreṇa śodhayet || 37.79{79} ||
tatastadvratasūtraṃ sa sagururdhāraṇīṃ pathan |
tasmai śiṣyāya rakṣyārthaṃ datvābhiṣekamarppayet || 37.80{80} ||
tadvrataṃ sūtramādāya vaṃdhitvā svakare vratī |
yāvadvratasamāptaṃ na tāvattatsūtramādharet || 37.81{81} ||
tataḥ vratī yathāśakti gurave dānadakṣiṇam |
datvāṣṭāṅgapraṇāmeṇa natvā samabhitoṣayet || 37.82{82} ||
tataḥ paṃcāmṛtairyuktaṃ baliṃ kṣīrodanābharam |
sa yakṣayakṣaṇīlokapālebhyaḥ saṃpradāpayet || 37.83{83} ||
tataḥ kṣamārthanaṃ kṛtvā guruṇā saha pālanam |
tṛtīyapraharāgre'hneḥ kuryātsa yavapūrakaiḥ || 37.84{84} ||
pālanāṃte punaḥ snātvā tāṃ devīṃ sagaṇāṃ smaran |
paṭhañchrīdhāraṇīṃ rātrī pūjayecca yathāvidhi || 37.85{85} ||
evaṃ nityaṃ catuḥsaṃdhyaṃ pūjayecchrīvasuṃdharām |
yāvajjīvaṃ yathāsaktau varṣaikaṃ vā tathā caret || 37.86{86} ||
ṣaṇmāsaṃ māsamekaṃ ca yathāśakti vrataṃ caret |
navasaptadinānyevaṃ paṃcatrīṇi dinānyapi || 37.87{87} ||
aho rātraṃ tathaikaṃ vā vāramekaṃ vrataṃ caret |
tatpare dyurdine prātaḥ snātvā śuddhāśayo vratī |
tathā tāṃ sagaṇāṃ devīṃ samarcayedyathāvidhi || 37.88{88} ||
tataśca guruve'bhyarcya vastrālaṃkārabhūgṛham |
sadakṣiṇāṃ pradatvaivaṃ svātmānaṃ ca samarppayet || 37.89{89} ||
tataḥ sa gururādāya tasmai śiṣyāya sāśiṣam |
datvā kṣamārthanāṃ kṛtvā tanmaṇḍalaṃ visarjayet || 37.90{90} ||
tatastadraja ādāya bhaṇḍāgāre'bhigopitam |
saṃnidhāya sadā nityamabhyarcya sādaraṃ bhajet || 37.91{91} ||
tadrajaḥśeṣanirmālyaṃ nadyāṃ nāgān yathāvidhi |
samabhyarcya samarppitvā sarvaṃ jale pravāhayet || 37.92{92} ||
tatastajjalamādāya gṛhe gatvā mahotsavaiḥ |
tajjalairmaṇḍalāgārakoṣṭhe sarvatra siṃcayet || 37.93{93} ||
tatastadvratapūrṇārthaṃ rātrau devīṃ kumārikām |
yathāvidhi samāvāhya samabhyarcyābhyatoṣayet || 37.94{94} ||
satgurupramukhān sarvān yogino yoginīrapi |
samabhyarcya yathākāmaṃ bhojanaiḥ saṃpratoṣayet || 37.95{95} ||
tatastaṃ sagaṇaṃ cakraṃ natvā vratī sa sāṃjaliḥ |
kṣamāpya vinayaṃ kṛtvā prārthayecchrīsamṛddhitām || 37.96{96} ||
tatastatsagaṇaṃ cakraṃ tadbhaktyā saṃpratoṣitam |
tasmai vratine dadyātsusaṃpattisubhāṣitam || 37.97{97} ||
tatastasya gṛhe devī vasudhārā samāśritā |
sarvadravyābhisaṃpūrṇaṃ kṛtvā tiṣṭhetsadā sthirā || 37.98{98} ||
tatastaddravyamādāya sarvārthibhyo yathepsitam |
saṃpradatvā yathākāmaṃ bhuktvā bhavaṃ sukhaṃ caret || 37.99{99} ||
evaṃ gṛhapate matvā vasudhārāvrataṃ mahat |
yathāvidhi samādāya gṛhe cara samādarāt || 37.100{100} ||
tataste sarvadā gehe maṃgalaṃ nirupadravam |
bhavetsāpi mahādevī sagaṇā nivasetsthirā || 37.101{1} ||
tataste sarvasaṃpattiḥ pravarddhitā śubhānvitā |
bhavane saṃbhavantyeva na kadācitkṣayaṃ vrajet || 37.102{2} ||
tatastvaṃ sarvadā saukhyaṃ bhuktvā datvā yathepsitam |
triratnabhajanaṃ kṛtvā śubhe cara samāhitaḥ || 37.103{3} ||
etatpuṇyavipākena sadā tvaṃ sadgatiṃ tataḥ |
bodhicaryāṃ caran bodhiṃ prāpya buddhapadaṃ labheḥ || 37.104{4} ||
ityādiṣṭaṃ munīndreṇa śrutvā dharmadhvajo gṛhī |
sa mudā suprasannātmā vrataṃ caritumaichata || 37.105{5} ||
tataḥ sa mudito natvā sāṃjalistaṃ jagadgurum |
bhagavantaṃ jagannāthaṃ prārthayadevamādarāt || 37.106{6} ||
bhagavan sarvavicchāstarbhavatāṃ śaraṇaṃ gataḥ |
bhavadājñāṃ śiro dhṛtvā cariṣye'hamidaṃ vratam || 37.107{7} ||
tadbhavāṃ kṛpayā śāstastadanujñāṃ dadātu me |
bhavatātra yathādiṣṭaṃ cariṣye tattathā khalu || 37.108{8} ||
iti saṃprārthite tena bhagavān sa jagadguruḥ |
tasmai devyā mahāvidyāṃ dhāraṇīṃ saṃpaṭhan dadau || 37.109{10} ||
so'pi dharmadhvajo natvā sāñjalistaṃ munīśvaram |
dhāraṇīṃ tāṃ samādāya saṃpapāṭha pramoditaḥ || 37.110{10} ||
tataḥ sa suprasannātmā dharmadhvajaḥ kṛtāṃjaliḥ |
taṃ munīndraṃ jagannāthaṃ praṇatvā svagṛhaṃ yayau || 37.111{11} ||
tataḥ sa svagṛhaprāptaḥ suprasannamukhāmbujaḥ |
tāṃ śāṃtamatāṃ bhāryāṃ samāmaṃtryaivamabravīt || 37.112{12} ||
priye dhanyāsi kalyāṇī yattvayāhaṃ prabodhitaḥ |
saṃbuddhaśāsanaṃ ratnamāptavānahamāgataḥ || 37.113{13} ||
tadyathā samupādiṣṭaṃ munīndreṇānuśāsinā |
tathāvāṃ tadvrataṃ devyāścarevahi samādarāt || 37.114{14} ||
iti bhartrā samuddiṣṭe sāpi śāntamatī mudā |
bharttāraṃ taṃ samālokya sudṛṣṭyaivamabhāṣata || 37.115{15} ||
ayi svāmin kathaṃ śāstra samādiṣṭaṃ vadasva me |
yathādiṣṭaṃ munīndreṇa tatha carevahi dhruvam || 37.116{16} ||
iti bhāryoditaṃ śrutvā bharttā dharmadhvajo'tha saḥ |
mudā śāṃtamatīṃ bhāryāṃ tāṃ samīkṣyaivamabravīt || 37.117{17} ||
śṛṇu bhadre yathādiṣṭaṃ munīndreṇa tathā khalu |
yadvrataṃ vasudhārāyāstadvidhiṃ te pravakṣyate || 37.118{18} ||
ityukte svāminā tena sā ca śāntamatī priyā |
bharttāraṃ taṃ samālokya muditaivamabhāṣata || 37.119{19} ||
svāminstena munīndreṇa yathādiṣṭaṃ tathā bhavān |
suvistaraṃ samākhyāya mano me saṃvinodata || 37.120{20} ||
ityukte bhāryayā sātha dharmadhvajaḥ suvistaram |
yathādiṣṭaṃ munīndreṇa tathā sarvaṃ nyavedayat || 37.121{21} ||
tacchrutvā sā prabodhantī śāṃtamatyanumoditā |
tathā tasyā mahādevyā vrataṃ caritumaichata || 37.122{22} ||
tatastaṃ svāminaṃ natvā sā ca śāntamatī satī |
tadvratārambhasāmagrīṃ sādhitumevamabravīt || 37.123{23} ||
svāmin vrataṃ cariṣyāvo vasulakṣmyā yathāvidhi |
sāmagrīṃ sādhayeyāsya tadanujñāṃ pradehi me || 37.124{24} ||
iti bhāryoditaṃ śrutvā dharmadhvajaḥ sa moditaḥ |
sādhyatāṃ sarvasāmāgrīmiti tāṃ sāṃvyanodayat || 37.125{25} ||
tataḥ sā muditāḥ sarve sāmagrīṃ sādhayantyapi |
bharttāraṃ tamupāmaṃtrya darśayantyevamabravīt || 37.126{26} ||
sarvaṃ me sādhitaṃ siddhaṃ svāminnatra prasīdatu |
tatsadguruṃ samāmaṃtrya vratamālabdhumarhati || 37.127{27} ||
iti bhāryoditaṃ śrutvā sa dharmadhvaja ādarāt |
sadguruṃ samupārādhya natvevaṃ prārthayanmudā || 37.128{28} ||
bhadanta śrīvasudhārādevyā vrataṃ carevahi |
tasmātdevīṃ samārādhya vratopadeśamādiśa || 37.129{29} ||
iti saṃprārthite tena tatheti hi sa sadguruḥ |
pratiśrutya yathādiṣṭaṃ munīndreṇa tathācarat || 37.130{30} ||
tathā bhādrapade māse kṛṣṇapakṣe sa sadguruḥ |
dvitīyāyaṃ tithau tīrthe snātvā suvāsasā sudhīḥ || 37.131{31} ||
yathāvidhiṃ samārabhya maṃḍalaṃ samavarttayat |
tatra tāṃ sagaṇāṃ devīṃ pratiṣṭhāpya samarcayat || 37.132{32} ||
tatparedyustritīyāyāṃ susnātaṃ śubhāmbaram |
dharmadhvajaṃ sadāraṃ taṃ paṃcagavyairvyaśodhayat || 37.133{33} ||
tatastau daṃpatī śāstrā yathādiṣṭaṃ samāhitau |
śrutvā tathā samādhāya pracaratu vrataṃ mudā || 37.134{34} ||
yathā śāstrā samādiṣṭaṃ tathā kṛtvā vrataṃ mudā |
gaṇacakrādikaṃ sarvaṃ samāpya tau nanandatuḥ || 37.135{35} ||
tatastasya gṛhe devīṃ vasudhārā samāśritā |
sarvadravyaṃ samāpūryya tasthau sadā prasāditā || 37.136{36} ||
tatastasya gṛhe saṃpaddine pravarddhitā |
saratnadhātusadvastraśasyādi dravyajātayaḥ || 37.137{37} ||
tā evaṃ varddhitā dṛṣṭvā sadāraḥ sa gṛhādhipaḥ |
sa vismayo muditotphullavaktrāmbujo'bhyanaṃdata || 37.138{38} ||
tataḥ sa sarvadārthibhyo datvā dānaṃ yathepsitam |
triratnabhajanaṃ kṛtvā sukhaṃ bhuktvā śubhe'carat || 37.139{39} ||
taddṛṣṭvā tasya gehasya samīpastho dvijottamaḥ |
viṣṇudāsābhidho vijño vismayoddhatamānasaḥ || 37.140{40} ||
dhīmatīṃ ramaṇīṃ bhāryāṃ suviśuddhāśayāṃ satīm |
niḥśvasanstāṃ samāmaṃtrya punarevamabhāṣata || 37.141{41} ||
paśya bhadrasya mitrasya gṛhe saṃpatsamṛddhitā |
kimanena kṛtaṃ dharmaṃ yenaivaṃ śrīḥ pravarddhitā || 37.142{42} ||
kimāvābhyāṃ kṛtaṃ pāpaṃ yenaivaṃ no vipatsadā |
kimatrāvāṃ kariṣyāvo yena saṃpatpravarddhitā || 37.143{43} ||
tadupāyaṃ na jānāmi ko me upadiśeddhitam |
dhanyāste dhanavaṃtā ye yathākāma sukhāśinaḥ || 37.144{44} ||
kiṃ te sadguṇavaṃto'pi kṛpaṇā ye daridritā |
varaṃ prāṇaparityāgaṃ na tvevaṃ jīvitaṃ bhave || 37.145{45} ||
kiṃ teṣāṃ sadguṇaiścāpi yeṣāṃ saṃpadgṛhe na hi |
dhanavān puruṣo dhīraḥ kulīno nirguṇo'pi ca |
sarve hi dhanināṃ vaśyā paṃḍitāḥ suguṇā api || 37.146{46} ||
dhanavānnirguṇaścāpi mānyate guruvajjanaiḥ |
dhanahīnā na pūjyaṃte paṃḍitā brahmaṇā api || 37.147{47} ||
dhigme jīvitamevaṃ hi brāhmaṇasyāpi duḥkhinaḥ |
varamevādyetanmṛtyurna tveva cirajīvitum || 37.148{48} ||
kimanenāpi jīvena kevaladuḥkhabhoginā |
tadatra marttumichāmi naiva jīveya duḥkhabhāk || 37.149{49} ||
sarveṣāmapi jaṃtūnāmavaśyaṃ maraṇaṃ bhave |
tadatra vidyamāno'haṃ kathaṃ loke careya hi || 37.150{50} ||
ityevaṃ vilapaṃ vipro viṣṇudāsaḥ sa mohitaḥ |
galadaśruvirukṣākṣastasthau muhurviniḥśvasan || 37.151{51} ||
ityevaṃ vilapantan taṃ dṛṣṭvā sā dhīmatī priyā |
bodhayituṃ samāśvāsya bharttāramevamabravīt || 37.152{52} ||
mā viṣādaṃ kṛthāḥ svāmin dhairyyamālambya tiṣṭhatu |
kiṃ kariṣyāvahe hyatra daivātsaṃpanna tau gṛhe || 37.153{53} ||
tathāpi dhairyyamālambya yatnaṃ kuru samāhitaḥ |
yatnena siddhyate sarvaṃ guṇadravyādi sādhanam || 37.154{54} ||
tadatrāhaṃ vadiṣyāmi hitārthaṃ te śṛṇu prabho |
bhāryā hi svāmino bhartturhitārtham'bhyanudeśinī || 37.155{55} ||
yadayaṃ gṛhabhṛdbharttā dharmadhvajastava priyaḥ |
suhṛnmitraṃ mahāsādhustadādarātsa pṛchyatām || 37.156{56} ||
yathā tena samādiṣṭaṃ tathaiva krīyatāṃ prabho |
tatastasya yathā saṃpattathāsmākaṃ bhavedapi || 37.157{57} ||
iti bhāryoditaṃ śrutvā viṣṇudāsaḥ sa tairthikaḥ |
bauddhavākye viruddhatvādanichannevamabravīt || 37.158{58} ||
vayaṃ hi brāhmaṇā bhadre buddhadevī vasuṃdharā |
tadvidhiṃ vedaśāstreṣu kathitaṃ na kvacidapi || 37.159{59} ||
tatkathaṃ svakulācāraṃ tyaktvānyacharaṇaṃ gatāḥ |
bauddhopadeśamāsādya lakṣmyā vrataṃ caremahi || 37.160{60} ||
ye tyaktvā svakulācāramanyatra śaraṇaṃ gatāḥ |
vrataṃ caraṃti saṃpattilābhilābhākulāśayāḥ || 37.161{61} ||
te svadharmaparibhraṣṭā ihāpi kliṣṭabhoginaḥ |
rogiṇo duhitātmāno mṛtā yāsyaṃti durgatīm || 37.162{62} ||
iti bhadre na me vāṃchā bauddhadevyā vrate'śubhe |
varaṃ prāṇaparityāgaṃ nānyadvrataṃ caremahi || 37.163{63} ||
asmākamapi yā devī mahālakṣmī kuleśvarī |
tasyā vrataṃ samādhāya caremahi sadādarāt || 37.164{64} ||
tataḥ sā śrīmahādevī gṛhe'śmākaṃ samāśritā |
yathābhivāṃchitaṃ dravyaṃ sarvaṃ dadyātprasāditā || 37.165{65} ||
iti tasyā mahālakṣmyā vrataṃ carāvahe vayam |
tattadvratasya sāmagrīṃ sādhaya sarvamādarāt || 37.166{66} ||
iti bhartroditaṃ śrutvā dhīmatī sā pramoditā |
tathetyabhyanumodantī tatsāmagrīmasādhayat || 37.167{67} ||
tatra sā dhīmatī tasyāḥ śāṃtamatyāḥ puro gatā |
tadvṛttāntaṃ sāmākhyāya sāmagrīṃ samayācata || 37.168{68} ||
sāpi śāṃtamatī tasyai dhīmatyai saṃpramoditā |
yadyatsaṃprārthitaṃ dravyaṃ tattatsarvaṃ dadau mudā || 37.169{67} ||
taddravyaṃ sā samādāya bhartturagre samāgatā |
tatsarvaṃ samupasthāpya vinodyaivamabhāṣata || 37.170{70} ||
svāmin tvayā yathādiṣṭaṃ tatsarvaṃ sādhitaṃ mayā |
tathā tasyā mahālakṣmyā vratamārabha sāṃpratam || 37.171{71} ||
iti bhāryoditaṃ śrutvā viṣṇudāsaḥ sa moditaḥ |
yathāvidhi mahālakṣmīvrataṃ caritumārabhat || 37.172{72} ||
māse bhādrapade kṛṣṇapakṣe'ṣṭamyāṃ yathāvidhi |
maṇḍalādīn pratisthāpya śuddhaśīlaḥ samāhitaḥ || 37.173{73} ||
sadārastāṃ mahālakṣmīṃ samāvāhya samarcayan |
vidhinā tadvrataṃ kṛtvā saṃpattiṃ prārthayanmudā || 37.174{74} ||
tataḥ sa brāhmaṇaḥ kṛtvā tadvrataṃ susamāptitam |
sabhāryaḥ suprasannātmā prācarattāṃ śriyaṃ smaran || 37.175{75} ||
tathā tasya gṛhe saṃpatsamudbhūtābhyajāyata |
dṛṣṭvā tau daṃpatī tatra mahānaṃdamavāpatuḥ || 37.176{76} ||
tataḥ sa brāhmaṇo lubdho dṛṣṭvā tān saṃpadaṃ mudā |
sarvān gupte nidhāyaiva na kiṃ cittadvyayaṃ vyadhāt || 37.177{77} ||
bhūyastṛṣṇābhisaṃtapto mātsaryābhihatāśayaḥ |
kasmai cidapi taddravyaṃ kiṃ ciddānaṃ dadau kvacit || 37.178{78} ||
tathā saṃrakṣya taddravyaṃ nidhāya tu viyatnataḥ |
gopayitvā svayaṃ bhoktumapi naivābhyavāṃchata || 37.179{79} ||
tathāpi sa pralubdhātmā nityaṃ kṛpaṇo'rthivat |
dhanināṃ samupāśritya yācitvā dhanamārjayat || 37.180{80} ||
tathārjanaḥ kṛtastasya gṛhe dravyaṃ na varddhitam |
dine dine nihīnatvaṃ gatvā kṣayaṃ kramādyayau || 37.181{81} ||
tataḥ sa brāhmaṇo lubdho dhanināmarthino gṛhe |
dṛṣṭvātiruṣitaḥ sarvān paribhāṣyābhyaniṃdayat || 37.182{82} ||
tatpāpatāvipākena gṛhe yadgopitaṃ dhanam |
tatsarvaṃ nihṛtaṃ caurairnidagdhaṃ cāpi vahninā || 37.183{83} ||
tataḥ sa brāhmaṇastīvrakleśāgniparitāpitaḥ |
daridritāvibhagnāśaḥ sabhāryo nyavasadgṛhe || 37.184{34!} ||
tasminnavasare tasyā dhīmatyāḥ sa pitā dvijaḥ |
hariśarmābhidho gehe yajñaṃ karttuṃ sadārabhat || 37.185{35} ||
tadā sa vāṃdhavān sārvāñjñātīnmitrasuhṛjjanān |
bhaginībhāgineyāṃśca jāmātrīṃśtatsutānapi || 37.186{36} ||
duhitṝrapi sarvāśca nyamaṃtrayatsamādarāt || 37.187{37} ||
tatra te vāṃdhavā sarve jñātimitrasuhṛjjanāḥ |
bhaginyā bhāgineyāśca jāmātaro'pi tatsutāḥ || 37.188{38} ||
duhitarastathā pautrāḥ pautryādayo'pi cāpare |
sarve te samupāgatya samupatasthurādritāḥ || 37.189{39} ||
tatra sā kṛpaṇībhūtā durbhageti pitāpi saḥ |
tatra yajñamahotsāhe tāmekāṃ na nyamaṃtrayat || 37.190{40} ||
tadā sā dhīmatī śrutvā pituryajñamahotsavam |
kathaṃ me vismṛtaṃ pitrā mātrāpi vāṃdhavairapi || 37.191{41} ||
iti ciṃtāviṣāghrātahṛdayā parimūrchitā |
bhojane'pi nirutsāhā tasthau lajjānvitā gṛhe || 37.192{42} ||
tathā sa viṣṇudāso'pi sasurasya gṛhe tadā |
śrutvā yajñamahotsāhaṃ tāṃ bhāryāmevamabravīt || 37.193{43} ||
kathaṃ te janakenāvāṃ vismṛtau kena hetunā |
naiva nimaṃtritau bhadre yathā sarve nimaṃtritā || 37.194{44} ||
iti bhartroditaṃ śrutvā dhīmatī sātilajjitā |
bharttāraṃ viṣṇudāsaṃ taṃ bodhayantyevamabravīt || 37.195{45} ||
kimatrāhaṃ vadiṣyāmi yannau saṃpadgṛhe'sti na |
tena nau lajjayāmbāpi nimaṃtrituṃ na vāṃchate || 37.196{46} ||
iti sā dhīmatī lajjāsaṃkuṃcitāśayānanā |
bhartrā saha vilapyaiva tasthau gṛhe nirāśitā || 37.197{47} ||
tadā tajjanaṃ smṛtvā dhīmatīṃ tāmanāgatām |
kāñcitpaṭhigatāṃ ceṭīṃ samāhūyaivamabravīt || 37.198{48} ||
ayi sakhi sutā me sā dhīmatā yadi dṛśyate |
madvācasā sametyāsau vaktavyaivaṃ laghu tvayā || 37.199{49} ||
pituste mandire yajñamahotsāhaṃ pravarttate |
tatkathaṃ na samāyāsi sabharttāgantumarhasi || 37.200{50} ||
iti saṃdiśya te mātrā preṣitāhaṃ tvadantike |
tanmāturvacanaṃ śrutvāgacheti tatpuro vada || 37.201{51} ||
tatsaṃdiṣṭaṃ tayā śrutvā tatheti pratibhāṣya sā |
ceṭī tato'bhigachantī dhīmatyā bhavane yayau || 37.202{52} ||
tatra tāṃ dhīmatīṃ dṛṣṭvā sā ceṭī samupasthitā |
yathā mātrā samādiṣṭaṃ tathā sarvaṃ nyavedayat || 37.203{53} ||
tattayā kathitaṃ śrutvā dhīmatī sāśrumocinī |
viniḥśvasya prasūṃ smṛtvā vilapantyaivamabravīt || 37.204{54} ||
hā mātaḥ kathamadyāhaṃ saṃsmṛtā sāṃprataṃ tvayā |
kimāgacheya te putrī pāpinī duḥkhabhāginī || 37.205{55} ||
tasminneva kṣaṇe bharttā viṣṇudāsa upācarat |
tāṃ ceṭīṃ samupālokya kimarthamityapṛchata || 37.206{56} ||
iti tenābhisaṃpṛṣṭe sā ceṭī racitāṃjaliḥ |
brāhmaṇaṃ taṃ praṇamyaitatsarvaṃ vṛtāṃtamabravīt || 37.207{57} ||
tathā tatkathitaṃ śrutvā viṣṇudāso'pi sa dvijaḥ |
galadaśruvirukṣākṣastāṃ bhāryāmevamabravīt || 37.208{58} ||
kiṃ vadiṣyāvahe bhārye daivānnau jāyate vipat |
tathāpi dhairyyamālamvya gacha draṣṭuṃ piturmanaḥ || 37.209{59} ||
iti bhartroditaṃ śrutvā dhīmatī sā kuvāsinī |
nirmaṇḍanātsutān putrīḥ samādāya śanairyayau || 37.210{60} ||
tato māturgṛhe prāptā dṛṣṭvā tau pitarau puraḥ |
gatvā sā dhīmatī natvā tasthau lajjāhatāśayā || 37.211{61} ||
pitarau tāṃ sutāṃ dṛṣṭvā vichandāṃ lajjitāśayam |
kiṃ ciddṛṣṭvāgatāsīti kathitvaiva niṣedatuḥ || 37.212{62} ||
tataḥ sā dhīmatī sarvāñjñātīn bhrātrīṃśca vāṃdhavān |
bhaginīḥ suhṛdo jeṣṭhān praṇanāma yathākramam || 37.213{63} ||
tāṃ dṛṣṭvā jñātayaḥ sarve bhrātaro vāṃdhavā api |
bhaginyāpi suhṛdaścāpi dṛṣṭvopahasya jalpire || 37.214{64} ||
tathā prahasitaṃ sarve sā dṛṣṭvātitrapānvitā |
svadaivasmṛtimādhāya niḥśvasyaikānta āśrayat || 37.215{65} ||
tataḥ sa hariśarmā sa purohito yathāvidhi |
yajñakarmasamārabhya yathākāmaṃ samāpayat || 37.216{66} ||
tatastān vāṃdhavāñjñātīn suhṛnmitrajanānapi |
satkṛtyātyabhisaṃtuṣṭān sādareṇa vyasarjayat || 37.217{67} ||
tataste jñātayaḥ sarve vaṃdhumitrasuhṛjjanāḥ |
naṃditāstaṃ praśaṃsitvā svasvālayaṃ mudā yayuḥ || 37.218{68} ||
tataste susthitāḥ sarve jñātivaṃdhusuhṛjjanāḥ |
nānākathābhisaṃlāpaṃ kurvantaḥ saṃniṣedire || 37.219{69} ||
tataḥ sā dhīmatī paścādbhuktvaikānte nirādritā |
putraputrī samādāya śivasya bhavane'carat || 37.220{70} ||
tatra sā stṛṇamāstīrya putraputrīsamanvitā |
dāridryaduḥkhacintānuniḥśvasantī nyaṣīdata || 37.221{71} ||
tatra sa viṣṇudāso'pi paścādbhuktvā nirādritaḥ |
śivālaye sthitāṃ śrutvā saha sthātumupācarat || 37.222{72} ||
bharttāraṃ tamupāyātaṃ dṛṣṭvā sā dhīmatī tadā |
galadaśruvirukṣākṣā vilapantyevamabravīt || 37.223{73} ||
dhigme jīvaṃ daridrāyāḥ kimevaṃ jīvitena hi |
pitrorapi dayāsnehadṛṣṭirna vidyate mayi || 37.224{74} ||
yatpitroḥ samāḥ sarvā bhaginyaḥ svātmajā api |
tathāhaṃ mānitā naiva mānitāstā yathādarāt || 37.225{75} ||
dhigjīvitaṃ daridrāyā janmāpi dhikca mānuṣe |
kiṃ rūpaiḥ suguṇaiḥ vāpi vidyābhiḥ kiṃ dhanaṃ vinā || 37.226{76} ||
hā daiva kiṃ mayā pāpaṃ dāruṇaṃ prakṛtaṃ purā |
yanme na vidyate saṃpatpitro dadhāpi dayā mayi || 37.227{77} ||
tadatra kiṃ kariṣyāmi yāsyāmi śaraṇaṃ kuha |
kā māṃ rakṣetsvadaivotthadaridryaduḥkhatāpinīm || 37.228{78} ||
kimevaṃ jīvitenāpi varaṃ mṛtyurhi sāṃpratām |
avaśyameva marttavyaṃ sarveṣāmapi janminām || 37.229{79} ||
evaṃ nānāpralāpena vilapantī sutaṃ sutām |
bharttāraṃ ca muhurdṛṣṭvā kāruṇyārttā ruroda sā || 37.230{80} ||
evaṃ vilāpaṃ kurvantīṃ rudantīṃ tāṃ vilokya saḥ |
viṣṇudāso viniḥśvasya samāśvāsyaivamabravīt || 37.231{81} ||
priye'tra mā rudihi tvaṃ kiṃ kariṣyāvahe'dhunā |
yadāvābhyāṃ kṛtaṃ pāpaṃ bhoktavyameva tatphalam || 37.232{82} ||
tadatra dhairyyamālamvya tiṣṭha kiṃ vakṣyate bahu |
avaśyaṃ bhāvino bhāvā bhavaṃti mahatāmapi || 37.233{83} ||
śivo'pi sa virūpākṣo nīlakaṇṭho digaṃvaraḥ |
unmatto bhasmaliptāṃgaḥ kapālayo'pi rasthidhṛk || 37.234{84} ||
viṣṇurvīro'pi saṃsāre bhramitaḥ sa janārdanaḥ |
so'pi dāso validvāsthaścauro vyādheṣuṇāhataḥ || 37.235{85} ||
brahmā lokādhipaḥ so'pi kāmārtto'gamyakarmakṛt |
niyujyo nighnito vairisevakaśchinnamastaka || 37.236{86} ||
cicheda brahmaṇaḥ pūrvaṃ rudraḥ krodhātta paṃcamam |
tacchiro dustyajaṃ gṛhṇan brahmāṇḍaṃ parivabhrame || 37.237{87} ||
śaṃkhatīrthe gato rudrastacchiraḥ parimuktavān |
kapālamocanastīrtho dvitīyāvarttasaṃsthitaḥ || 37.238{88} ||
iti utkalakhaṇḍakṣetramāhātmye uktamāste |
evamanye'pi lokāśca munayastāpasā api || 37.239{89} ||
kopāgnidahitātmāno vrajanti narakeṣvapi |
evaṃ sarve'pi lokāśca svasvadaivānubhāvinaḥ || 37.240{90} ||
sukhino duḥkhino bhūtā bhramaṃti bhavasāgare |
kṣaṇāllabhanti saṃpattiṃ bhavanti ca daridritāḥ || 37.241{91} ||
sarve devādayo lokāḥ svakṛtakarmabhoginaḥ |
evaṃ matvātra saṃsāre sukhaṃ duḥkhaṃ svadaivajam || 37.242{92} ||
mā kṛthāstadviṣāda tvaṃ dhairyaṃ dhṛtvā samācara |
svakuleśamanusmṛtvā samabhyarcya yathāvidhiḥ |
śraddhayā śaraṇaṃ kṛtvā bhaja nityaṃ samāhitā || 37.243{93} ||
tatastatpuṇyapākena pāpaṃ no vilayaṃ vrajet |
tataḥ saṃpatsamṛddhiḥ syāttadā lapsyāvahe sukham || 37.244{94} ||
iti me vacanaṃ śrutvā dhairyyamālamvya sāṃpratam |
putraputrīṃ samādāya praihi gṛhe vrajāmahe || 37.245{95} ||
iti bhartroditaṃ śrutvā dhīmatī sābhibodhitā |
putraṃ putrīṃ purodhāya śanairbhartrā sahācarat || 37.246{96} ||
tatra sā svagṛhe gatvā kṣaṇaṃ sthitvā nirutsahā |
tataḥ śāntamatīṃ draṣṭuṃ samutthāya śanairyayau || 37.247{97} ||
tatra sā dhīmatī tasyāḥ śāṃtamatyāḥ purogatā |
galadaśruviliptāsyā niḥśvasaṃtyevamabravīt || 37.248{98} ||
śāṃtamate pituryajñamahotsāhapravarttitam |
tatsaṃdraṣṭu gatā tatra mahālajjānvitā caram || 37.249{99} ||
yanmāṃ dṛṣṭvā bhaginyo'pi jñātivaṃdhusuhṛjjanāḥ |
api sarve mitho dṛṣṭvā prajalpitābhyupāhasan || 37.250{100} ||
bhāgineyāśca sarve'pi māṃ dṛṣṭvā ruṣitāśayāḥ |
putraputrīṃ ca me dṛṣṭvā phutkṛtya pratyātāḍayan || 37.251{1} ||
pitṛbhyāṃ mānitāḥ sarvāḥ bhaginyastā yathādarāt |
tathāhaṃ mānitā naiva draṣṭumapi na caiṣyate || 37.252{2} ||
rātrāvapi gṛhe vāsaṃ na me dattaṃ nirādarāt |
śivālaye niṣaṇṇā tanniśāṃ kṛcchrādvyalaṃghayam || 37.253{3} ||
evamahaṃ mahallajjāduḥkhāgnipatitāpitā |
marttumapi tadicchāmi kimevaṃ jīvitenapi || 37.254{4} ||
yadi te'sti kṛpā bhadre duḥkhinyāṃ mayi vidyate |
taddravyopārjanopāyaṃ samupadeṣṭumarhati || 37.255{5} ||
iti tayoditaṃ śrutvā śāntamatīrdayārditaḥ |
dhīmatīṃ tāṃ samālokya samāśvāsyaivamabravīt || 37.256{6} ||
dhīmatī śrīmahāsaṃpatsādhanopāyamasti me |
yadyatrāpyasti te vāṃchā pravakṣyāmi śṛṇuṣva tat || 37.257{7} ||
yadi śraddhāsti te devyā vasulakṣmyā vrataṃ cara |
tataste śrīvasundhārā mahādevī vasedgṛhe || 37.258{8} ||
tatastasyā mahādevyāḥ kṛpādṛṣṭiprasādataḥ |
saṃpattiste samudbhūtā pravarddhitā bhavedgṛhe || 37.259{9} ||
tadā tvaṃ śraddhayārthibhyo dānaṃ datvā yathepsitam |
triratnabhajanaṃ kṛtvā śubhe cara sukhāśinī || 37.260{10} ||
etatpuṇyavipākena sarvadā sadgatiṃ gatā |
kramādbodhiṃ samāsādya saṃbuddhapadamāpnuyāḥ || 37.261{11} ||
evaṃ matvāsti te vāṃchā saṃbuddhapada sādhane |
śrīdevyāḥ śaraṇaṃ gatvā samādhāya vrataṃ cara || 37.262{12} ||
iti tayā samādiṣṭaṃ śrutva sā dhīmatī mudā |
tāṃ śāṃtamatīmānamya sāṃjalīrevamabravīt || 37.263{13} ||
sādhu bhadre vrataṃ dhṛtvā śrīdevyāḥ śaraṇaṃ gatā |
triratnabhajanaṃ kṛtvā cariṣyāmi śubhe sadā || 37.264{14} ||
tadbhavaṃtī kṛpādṛṣṭyā tadupadeśamādiśa |
yāvajjīvaṃ cariṣyāmi śrīdevyā vratamādarāt || 37.265{15} ||
iti tayoditaṃ śrutvā śāntamatiḥ samādarāt |
dhīmatīṃ tāṃ samālokya punarevamabhāṣata || 37.266{16} ||
yadi bhadre'sti te satyaṃ snātvā śuddhāmvarāvṛtā |
prehi taṃ śrīghanaṃ nāthaṃ saṃsmṛtvā prārthayāvahe || 37.267{17} ||
tayeti samupādiṣṭaṃ śrutvā sā dhīmatī mudā |
sahasā svagṛhe gatvā snātvā śuddhāmvarāvṛtā || 37.268{18} ||
sa lājākṣatapuṣpāṇi gṛhitvā sahasā mudā |
tasyāḥ śāṃtamateragre prayātevamabhāṣata || 37.269{19} ||
bhadre tvayā yathādiṣṭaṃ tathāhaṃ samupāgatā |
taṃ munīndramanusmṛtvā prārthayeha samāgamam || 37.270{20} ||
iti tayoditaṃ śrutvā sā śāntamatirādarāt |
tāṃ dhīmatīṃ samāmaṃtrya prāsādāntaṃ yayau mudā || 37.271{21} ||
tatra śāntamatiḥ sāṭṭe dhīmatyā saha saṃsthitā |
jānubhyāṃ bhuvi saṃsthitvā bhagavatsthitadigmukhā || 37.272{22} ||
saṃbhavantyuttarāsaṅgaṃ saha lājākṣatādibhiḥ |
sāmbupuṣpāñjaliṃ dhṛtvā natvaivaṃ prārthayanmunim || 37.273{23} ||
bhagavannātha sarvajña vijānīte bhavānapi |
yadiyaṃ dhīmatī sādhvī bhagavaccharaṇamāgatā || 37.274{24} ||
yadiyaṃ sāṃprataṃ śāsturupadeśādyathāvidhi |
śrīvasudhāmahādevyā vrataṃ caritumichati || 37.275{25} ||
tadatra samupāgatya kṛpayāsmai dayānidhe |
śrīsādhanaṃ vrataṃ śreṣṭhaṃ samupadeṣṭumarhati || 37.276{26} ||
iti saṃprārthya sā śāntamatistaṃ trijagadgurum |
anusmṛtvā tridhā nātvā prākṣipattajjalādikam || 37.277{27} ||
prakṣiptāni tayā tāni sāmbulājākṣatāṇyapi |
sadūrvākuṇḍapuṣpāṇi sarvāṇi gagaṇe śaran || 37.278{28} ||
tatastāni pragacchanti vihāyasā vihaṅgavat |
kośāmvyāṃ ghoṣirārāme vihāre samupāyayuḥ || 37.279{29} ||
tatrasthasya munīndrasya murddhā pari vihāyasi |
saṃsthitāni prabhāsanti virejustāni chatravat || 37.280{30} ||
taddṛṣṭvā bhikṣavaḥ sarve sāṃghikāste savismayāḥ |
kuto nimaṃtraṇamāyātamiti dhyātvābhitasthire || 37.281{31} ||
tatrānandaḥ samutthāya sāṃjaliḥ purato gataḥ |
taṃ munīndraṃ praṇatvaivaṃ papracha vismayānvitaḥ || 37.282{32} ||
bhagavan kuta etāni sāmbulājākṣatānyapi |
puṣpāṇi samupāyānti tatsamādeṣṭumarhati || 37.283{33} ||
iti tenoditaṃ śrutvā bhagavāṃ sa munīśvaraḥ |
tamānaṃdaṃ samāmaṃtrya samālokyaivamabravīt || 37.284{34} ||
yaḥ kāśīpure vipro viṣṇudāso daridritaḥ |
dharmadhvajo dhanī śrīmān gṛhastho dhanadopamaḥ || 37.285{35} ||
tayorbhārya ubhe sādhvau śāntamatī ramopamā |
dhīmatī brāhmaṇī bhadrā daridritā suduḥkhinī || 37.286{36} ||
tābhyāṃ śrīvasudhārāyā vrataṃ caritumichyate |
tadidaṃ nimaṃtraṇārthaṃ me upahāraṃ samāgatam || 37.287{37} ||
tasmācchrīvasudhārāyā vratavidhiṃ tvamātmanā |
upadeṣṭuṃ tayorgehe kāśyāṃ gaṃtuṃ samarhasi || 37.288{38} ||
ityādiṣṭaṃ munīndreṇa śrutvānaṃdastatheti saḥ |
śāsturājñāṃ śirodhṛtvā kāśyāṃ vihāyasā yayau || 37.289{39} ||
tatrākāśān sa ānaṃdaḥ śāṃtamatergṛhe puraḥ |
avatīrya sudhāraśmiriva tasthau prabhāsayan || 37.290{40} ||
tatrasthaṃ taṃ samālokya śāntamatiḥ sa dhīmatī |
muditā sahasā tasya praṇaṃtuṃ samupācarat || 37.291{41} ||
tatra sā samupāsṛtya śāṃtamatiḥ sadhīmatī|
tamānaṃdaṃ samālokya natvaivaṃ prārthayanmudā || 37.292{42} ||
bhadanta caraṇau vaṃde bhavatāṃ śaraṇaṃ gatā |
tadatra kṛpayāsmākamanugṛhya prasīdatu || 37.293{43} ||
yadasmākaṃ bhavāñchāstā tadgṛhe samupāśrayan |
vasudhārāmahādevyā vratavidhiṃ samādiśa || 37.294{44} ||
yadiyaṃ dhīmatī bhadrā bhavatāmupadeśataḥ |
vasulakṣmyā mahādevyā vrataṃ caritumichati || 37.295{45} ||
tadasyai śrīmahādevyā vratavidhiṃ yathākramam |
vistaraṃ samupādeṣṭumarhati kṛpayā bhavān || 37.296{46} ||
iti saṃprārthitaṃ śāntamatyā tayā niśamya saḥ |
ānaṃdaste ubhe dṛṣṭvā tatheti pratyabhāṣata || 37.297{47} ||
tataḥ sā dhīmatī natvā sāṃjalistaṃ jinātmajam |
ānaṃdaṃ suprasannāsyā dṛṣṭvaivaṃ prārthayanmudā || 37.298{48} ||
bhadaṃto'haṃ svadaivena pāpinī duḥkhabhāginī |
tadbhavaccharaṇaṃ gatvā samiche vasudhāvratam || 37.299{49} ||
tadbhavān kṛpayā śāstargṛhe me samupāgataḥ |
yathāvidhi samādiśya vrataṃ me dātumarhati || 37.300{50} ||
tayaivaṃ prārthitaṃ śrutvā sa ānaṃdo jinātmajaḥ |
tatra utthāya dhīmatyāstasyā gṛhe upācarat || 37.301{51} ||
tatra sā dhīmatī sādhvī śāntamatyā samanvitā |
sahasā svagṛhe gatvā prastārayattadāsanam || 37.302{52} ||
tataḥ sā dhīmatī tasya śāstuḥ śuddhāmbunā mudā |
pādaprakṣālanaṃ kṛtvā praṇatvaiva ca prārthayat || 37.303{53} ||
bhadaṃtārhanmahābhijña mamānugrahakāraṇam |
atrāsane samāśritya dharmamādeṣṭumarhati || 37.304{54} ||
tayeti prārthitaṃ śrutvā sa ānaṃdaḥ prasannadhīḥ |
taddattāsana āśritya tasthau dhyātvā prabhāsayan || 37.305{55} ||
taṃ dṛṣṭvā brāhmaṇaḥ so'pi viṣṇudāsaḥ prasāditaḥ |
sahasā sāṃjalirnatvā tatraikāṃta upāśrayan || 37.306{56} ||
tataḥ sā dhīmatī sādhvī sāṃtamatīsamanvitā |
tamānaṃdaṃ samabhyarcya bhojanenābhyatoṣayat || 37.307{57} ||
tataḥ sā praṇatiṃ kṛtvā nīcāsanasamāśritā |
tamānaṃdaṃ mahābhijñaṃ sāṃjalirevamabravīt || 37.308{58} ||
bhadanta bhavatāmājñāṃ dhṛtvāhaṃ śirasā mudā |
śrīdevyā vasudhārāyāścariṣyāmi vrataṃ dhruvam || 37.309{59} ||
tadbhadanta bhavāñchāstā tadvidhiṃ me yathākramam |
kṛpānugrahamādhāya samupādeṣṭumarhati || 37.310{60} ||
iti saṃprārthite tena viṣṇudāsena saddhiyā |
śrutvānaṃdaḥ sa saṃpaśyanstaṃ dvijamevamabravīt || 37.311{61} ||
śṛṇu vipra munīndreṇa yāthādiṣṭaṃ mayā śrutam |
tathāhaṃ te pravakṣyāmi śrīvratasya vidhiṃ kramāt || 37.312{62} ||
bhādrapade'śite pakṣe tṛtīyāyāṃ tithau tathā |
māghe vāpi śite pakṣe śuklapakṣe'pi vā sadā || 37.313{63} ||
tīrthe devālaye śuddhabhūpradeśe gṛheṣu vā |
samyagbhūśodhanaṃ kuryādgomayena mṛdāmbunā || 37.314{64} ||
prātarutthāya tīrtheṣu snātvā śucisamācaraḥ |
tridhācamyāmṛtaṃ kāyaṃ paṃcagavyena śodhayet || 37.315{65} ||
śuddhaśīlasamādhānaḥ pariśuddhāmvarāvṛtaḥ |
saṃbodhicittamādhāya sadgurorupadeśa dhṛk || 37.316{66} ||
subhūmau maṇḍalaṃ samyagvarttayitvā yathāvidhi |
dhvajachatrapatākābhi vitānaiścābhimaṃḍayet || 37.317{67} ||
maṃḍalasya vahistasya jvālāvalyā vahiḥ punaḥ |
paṃcavalyāvalībhiśca sa lājākṣatasaṃyutaiḥ || 37.318{68} ||
veṣṭayitvā tato vāhye kramān saṃsthāpayedghaṭān |
tīrthāmbupūritān pītavastrāvṛtān saciṃhnitān || 37.319{69} ||
tātra śrīvasudhāṃ devīṃ sagaṇāṃ vidhinā kramāt |
samāvāhya samabhyarcya pratiṣṭhāpyādhivāsayet || 37.320{70} ||
tṛtīyāyāṃ tathā prātaḥ snātvā śuddhāmvarāvṛtaḥ |
madhyāhne svāsanāsīna uttarādigmukhasthitaḥ || 37.321{71} ||
ādau guruṃ samabhyarcya ratnatrayaṃ samarcayet |
tato dhyātvā mahādevīṃ vasudhārāṃ śubhakarīm || 37.322{72} ||
sagaṇāṃ tāṃ samāvāhya datvā pādārghamādarāt |
pītamayopahāreṇa samabhyarcya yathāvidhi || 37.323{73} ||
sarvopakaraṇānyagre nivesya samaḍhokayat |
tatastaddhāraṇīṃ samyakpaṭhenmaṃtrāṇi ca kramāt || 37.324{74} ||
tataḥ kṛtāṃjaliḥ stutvā kuryāśca pāpadeśanām |
puṇyānumodanāṃ cāpi kuryācca praṇatiṃ smaran || 37.325{75} ||
kuryātpradakṣiṇānyevamaṣṭāṅgaiḥ praṇametpunaḥ |
bodhicittaṃ samādhāya saṃprārthayedabhīpsitam || 37.326{76} ||
tato valiṃ pradatvā ca sa bhūtān paritoṣayet |
tataḥ kṣamārthanāṃ kṛtvā vratasūtraṃ ca prārthayet || 37.327{77} ||
ṣoḍaśabheditaṃ sūtraṃ ṣoḍaśagraṃthisaṃyutam |
vratasūtraṃ samādāya rakṣārthaṃ vaṃdhayetkare || 37.328{78} ||
tato guruṃ samārādhya deyāddānaṃ samichitam |
sadakṣiṇaṃ susatkāraiḥ satkṛtya samatoṣayet || 37.329{79} ||
tatastṛtīyā yāme'hneḥ prāśnuyādyavapūrakān |
paṃcāmṛtaphalaskaṃdamūlapatrādikānapi || 37.330{80} ||
pālanānte punaḥ snātvā tāṃ śrīdevīmanusmaran |
taddhāraṇīṃ paṭhan rātrau pūjayecca samāhitaḥ || 37.331{81} ||
evaṃ nityaṃ catuḥ saṃdhyaṃ pūjayecchrīvasuṃdharām |
tatparedyustathābhyarcya tanmaṃḍalaṃ visarjayet || 37.332{82} ||
tadrajāṃsi samādāya saṃnidhāya śubhe ghaṭe |
bhaṃḍāgāre pratisthāpya sadārān bhajennamet || 37.333{83} ||
śeṣarajaḥ sa nirmālyaṃ nadyāṃ nāgān yathāvidhi |
samabhyarcya samarppitvā sarvaṃ jale pravāhayet || 37.334{84} ||
tatastajjalamādāya gṛhe gatvā samantataḥ |
prokṣya tanmaṃḍalasthāne kumārīṃ vidhinārcayet || 37.335{85} ||
tatastan sagaṇaṃ cakraṃ samabhyarcya yathāvidhi |
bhojanaiḥ saṃparitoṣya prārthayedyatsamīchitam || 37.336{86} ||
tathā tatsagaṇaṃ cakraṃ kṛtvā kṣamārthanāṃ tataḥ |
sāṃjaliśca kramānnatvā visarjayetprasādayan || 37.337{87} ||
tadā śrīvasudhā devī svayamāgatya tadgṛhe |
samāśritya sadā sarvadravyaiḥ saṃpūrayeddhruvam || 37.338{88} ||
tatastatra gṛhe nityaṃ maṃgalaṃ nirupadravam |
dānapuṇyamahotsāhapravarttitaṃ bhavetsadā || 37.339{89} ||
evaṃ tasyā mahādevyā vasulakṣmyā vratodbhavam |
puṇyaṃ mahadasaṃkhyeyamaprameyaṃ vidurjināḥ || 37.340{90} ||
evaṃ mahattaraṃ puṇyaṃ vijñāya dvija tattathā |
śraddhaye tadvrataṃ kuryā yadi saṃpattimichasi || 37.341{91} ||
yaśca tāṃ śrīmahādevīṃ vasudhārāmanusmaran |
yatkāryyamārabhettasya tatsarvaṃ hi samṛddhyati || 37.342{92} ||
ratnārthī labhate ratnaṃ dhanārthī labhate dhanam |
bhogyārthī labhate bhogyaṃ yaso'rthī labhate yaśaḥ || 37.343{93} ||
dravyārthī labhate dravyān guṇārthī labhate guṇān |
vidyārthī labhate vidyāṃ śubhārthī labhate śubham || 37.344{94} ||
mānyārthī labhate mānyaṃ jayārthī labhate jayam |
dharmārthī labhate dharmān sukhārthī labhate sukham || 37.345{95} ||
rājyārthī labhate rājyaṃ gṛhārthī labhate gṛham |
kṣetrārthī labhate kṣetrān rasārthī labhate rasān || 37.346{96} ||
bhāryārthī labhate bhāryāṃ sutārthī labhate sutām |
putrārthī labhate putrān pautrārthī pautramāpnuyāt || 37.347{97} ||
jñānārthī labhate jñānaṃ mokṣārthī mokṣamāpnuyāt |
evamanyāni vastūni sarvopakaraṇānyapi || 37.348{98} ||
sarvāṇi dravyajātāni vastrādi bhūṣaṇānyapi |
samāpnuyātsamṛddhāni vasulakṣmyāḥ prasādataḥ || 37.349{99} ||
tato dānāni dadyātsa śuddhaśīlaḥ samācaret |
kṣāntiṃ ca bhāvayennityaṃ dharmavīryyaṃ samārabhet || 37.350{100} ||
tathā dhyānaṃ samādhāya prajñābdhiṃ saṃtareddrutam |
sarvopāyavidhānajño bodhipraṇidhimānasaḥ || 37.351{1} ||
sadbuddhibalavān dhīraḥ sa bodhijñānamāpnuyāt |
sarvamāragaṇāñjitvā niḥkleśo'rhañjitendriyaḥ |
saṃbuddhapadamāsādya nirvṛtapadamāpnuyāt || 37.352{2} ||
evaṃ brāhmaṇa vijñāya saddharmmaṃ yadi vāṃchasi |
tāṃ śrīdevīṃ mahālakṣmīṃ vasudhārāṃ sadā bhaja || 37.353{3} ||
tathā te sarvadā gehe maṃgalaṃ nirupadravam |
sarvatreha paratrāpi bhavennūnaṃ na saṃśayaḥ || 37.354{4} ||
tathā dānāni datvā tvaṃ svayaṃ tadvrataṃ saṃcaran |
sarvasatvahitaṃ kṛtvā yāvajjīvaṃ sukhaṃ cara || 37.355{5} ||
tataḥ kleśavimuktātmā saṃbodhipraṇidhānadhṛk |
bodhicaryāvratāraktā bodhisatvo bhaveddhruvam || 37.356{6} ||
sarve'pi sugatā bodhisatvāścāpi jinātmajāḥ |
vasudhārāvrataṃ dhṛtvā saṃvibhrati jagattrayam || 37.357{7} ||
iti tenārhatādiṣṭaṃ śrutvā sa brāhmaṇo mudā |
tathā śrīvasudhārāyā vrataṃ caritumaichata || 37.358{8} ||
tataḥ sa viṣṇudāso'pi bhāryāṃ tāṃ dhīmatīṃ mudā |
suprasannamukhāmbhojaḥ samālokyaivamabravīt || 37.359{9} ||
priye dhanyāsi kalyāṇī yadahaṃ tvatprayatnataḥ |
śrīdevyā vrataṃ ratnaṃ prāpnomi sāṃprataṃ khalu || 37.360{10} ||
tadyathānena śāstrātra samādiṣṭaṃ tathā khalu |
śrīdevyāḥ śaraṇaṃ gatvā vāṃchāmi carituṃ vratam || 37.361{11} ||
tattvamapi samādhāya mayā sārddhaṃ tathādarāt |
śrīdevyāḥ śaraṇaṃ kṛtvā vrataṃ caritumarhati || 37.362{12} ||
iti bhartroditaṃ śrutvā dhīmatī sānumoditā |
bharttāraṃ taṃ samālokya punarevamabhāṣata || 37.363{13} ||
tathā satyaṃ samādhāya śraddhayā śaraṇaṃ gatau |
śrīdevyā vratamatrāvāṃ samādarāccarāvahe || 37.364{14} ||
ityuktvā sā satī bhadrā dhīmatī sahasādarāt |
vratopacārasāmagrīṃ muditā samasādhayat || 37.365{15} ||
tatastaṃ gurumānaṃdaṃ natvā sā sāṃjalirmudā |
tadvratārambhamādhātuṃ prārthayedevamādarāt || 37.366{16} ||
bhadanta śrīmahādevyā vratadina upāsthitaḥ |
tadyathāvidhimādiśya saṃcārayatāṃ vratam || 37.367{17} ||
tayeti prārthitaṃ śrutvā sa ānaṃdo jinātmajaḥ |
sarvavidhiṃ samādiśya vratāraṃbhaṃ samākramat || 37.368{18} ||
tatra bhūśodhanaṃ kṛtvā sa ānaṃdo yathāvidhi |
maṃḍalaṃ varttayitvā tatpratisthāpyādhyavāsayat || 37.369{19} ||
tathā tau daṃpatī tīrthe snātvā śuddhāmvarāvṛtau |
yathā śāstrā samādiṣṭaṃ sarvaṃ kṛtvopatasthatuḥ || 37.370{20} ||
tatastau daṃpatī tasya śāsturādeśataḥ kramāt |
śuddhāsanasamāsīnāvuttarābhimukhasthitau || 37.371{21} ||
gavyaiḥ kāmādi vastūni sarvāṇi śuddhitānyapi |
kṛtvā śāstrā yathādiṣṭaṃ tathāraṃbhaṃ pracakratuḥ || 37.372{22} ||
ādau guruṃ samabhyarcya tato ratnatrayaṃ mudā |
abhyarcya śaraṇaṃ kṛtvā praṇatvā vidhinādarāt || 37.373{23} ||
tatastanmaṇḍale devīṃ sagaṇāṃ śrīvasuṃdharām |
dhyātvā tathā samāvāhya datvā pādārghamādarāt || 37.374{24} ||
tatastāṃ sagaṇāṃ devīṃ samabhyarcya yathākramam |
sarvapūjopacāraiśca paṭhitvā dhāraṇīṃ tataḥ || 37.375{25} ||
sahṛdvījādimaṃtrāṇi yathākramaṃ jajalpatuḥ |
tato'ṣṭāṅgapraṇāmaiśca natvā kṛtvā pradakṣiṇām || 37.376{26} ||
guṇābhivādanai stotrai stutvā natau vavaṃdatuḥ || 37.377{27!} ||
tatastau sāṃjalī sthitvā prakṣitvā pāpadeśanām |
puṇyānumodanāṃ cāpi saṃprārthya śrīsamṛddhyatām || 37.378{28} ||
salokapālabhūtebhyo baliṃ paṃcāmṛtānvitam |
yathāvidhi praḍhaukitvā prārthayatāṃ susaṃpadam || 37.379{29} ||
tato dānaṃ pradatvaivaṃ gurave'pi sudakṣiṇām |
tataḥ kṣamārthanāṃ kṛtvā samāptaṃ cakraturvratam || 37.380{30} ||
tato gurustayoḥ śāstā vratasūtraṃ samarppayat |
saṃpattisasamṛddhāśīrvādaṃ dadau prasāditaḥ || 37.381{31} ||
tato guruṃ puraskṛtya yathoktaṃ vratapālanam |
kṛtvā tataḥ punaḥ snātvā devīṃ dhyātvā niṣedituḥ || 37.382{32} ||
tato rātrau samādhāya sagaṇāṃ vasuṃdharām |
devīṃ dhyātvā samabhyarcya dharmmaṃ śrutvā niṣedatuḥ || 37.383{33} ||
tataḥ prātaḥ samutthāya snātvā śuddhāmvarāvṛtau |
tathā tāṃ sagaṇāṃ devīmāvāhyānarcatuḥ kramāt || 37.384{34} ||
tatastadvratasaṃpūrṇaṃ kṛtvā dānaṃ sadakṣiṇam |
pradatvā gurave natvā tanmaṇḍalaṃ vyasarjatām || 37.385{35} ||
tatastadrajamādāya bhaṃḍāgāre yathoditam |
nidhāya tatsa nirmālyaṃ śeṣaṃ nadyāṃ yathāvidhim || 37.386{36} ||
gururnāgān samabhyarcya samarpya prākṣipajjale |
tatastajjalamādāya mahotsāhairgṛhaṃ yayau || 37.387{37} ||
tata gṛhe tadambhobhirabhiṣiṃcya samantataḥ |
kumārīṃ vidhinārādhya gaṇacakraṃ samarcayat || 37.388{38} ||
tatastau daṃpatī natvā sāṃjali sādaraṃ mudā |
saṃpattiprārthanāṃ kṛtvā kṣamāpya tadvyasarjatām || 37.389{39} ||
tatastasyā gṛhe devī vasundhārā svayaṃ sthitā |
sarvadravyasamāpūrṇaṃ kṛtvā tasthau prasāditā || 37.390{40} ||
sarvadravyābhisaṃpūrṇaṃ dṛṣṭvā tau daṃpatī gṛham |
vismayānaṃdasaṃpannau mitha evaṃ samūcatuḥ || 37.391{41} ||
aho satyaṃ jagacchāsturyathādiṣṭaṃ tathā khalu |
tathāvāṃ śaraṇaṃ gatvā devyā vrataṃ carevahi || 37.392{42} ||
ityuktvā tau prasīdaṃtau śaraṇaṃ gatvā samāhitau |
tasyāḥ śrīmahādevyāḥ sadā vrataṃ praceratuḥ || 37.393{43} ||
tadārabhyaḥ sadārthibhyo datvā dānaṃ yathārthitam |
triratnabhajanaṃ kṛtvā praceratuḥ śubhe sadā || 37.394{44} ||
tato'sau yatirānaṃdastau samāmaṃtrya daṃpatī |
pakṣivatsahasākāśātkauśāṃvyāṃ samupācarat || 37.395{45} ||
tatra sa taṃ jagannāthaṃ praṇatvā sāṃjaliḥ punaḥ |
etatsarvaṃ pravṛttāṃtaṃ nivedyaikāntamāśrayat || 37.396{46} ||
tataḥ sa bhagavān dṛṣṭvā tamānaṃdaṃ sabhāṃ ca tām |
dhīmatyāḥ śāṃtamatyāśca prasāditvaivamādiśat || 37.397{47} ||
dhanyā sā dhīmatī śāntamatī cāpi subhāvinī |
yatte śrīvasudhārāyā bhavetāṃ vratadhāriṇī || 37.398{48} ||
tanme ubhe mahatpuṇyalābhinyau śrīsamanvite |
sarvasatvahitādhānabodhicaryāsamudyate || 37.399{49} ||
kramādbodhiṃ samāsādya buddhapadamavāpsyata || 37.400{50!} ||
ityādiṣṭaṃ munīndreṇa śrutvā te sarvasāṃghikāḥ |
tāṃ śrīvasuṃdharāṃ devīṃ smṛtvā nityaṃ prabhejire || 37.401{51} ||
tadā tasyāḥ pitā viprā dhīmatyā duhiturgṛhe |
samudbhūtā mahāsaṃpadityaśroṣījjanoditam || 37.402{52} ||
tataḥ sa brāhmaṇo bhāryāṃ dhīmatyā mātaraṃ mudā |
sahasā samupāmaṃtrya pura evamabhāṣata || 37.403{53} ||
kiṃ tvayānuśrutaṃ bhārye dhīmatyā yadgṛhe'dhunā |
mahāsaṃpatsamudbhūtā mayeti śrūyate khalu || 37.404{54} ||
iti bhartrā samākhyātaṃ śrutvā sā vismayānvitā |
kathametadbhavetsatyamiti taṃ patimabravīt || 37.405{55} ||
iti bhāryoditaṃ śrutvā hariśarmāpi sa dvijaḥ |
savismayenākhyātaṃ śrutvā caivamabhāṣata || 37.406{56} ||
